# Vellmouth Consolidated Municipal Utility Tariff Code 2026

Preamble. The Council of the Corporation of the City of Vellmouth, under the Municipal Utilities Act of the Province of Selvane, makes this Code fixing the charges for water supply, wastewater, trade waste, services and stormwater for the tariff year, and the rebates, minimum charges, instalments and interest that apply to them.

Parts: 1 Preliminary; 2 Tariff classes; 3 Zones; 4 Water supply charges; 5 Wastewater, trade waste and catchment levy; 6 Service and stormwater charges; 7 Surcharges; 8 Administration; 9 Rebates; 10 Carrying forward of rebates; 11 Minimum charges; 12 Instalments, payments and interest; 13 Records and notices; 14 Review and procedure; 15 Amending and corrective provisions; 16 Rulings, explanatory memoranda and examples.

## Part 1. Preliminary

#### Division 1-1. Commencement, application and legal effect

### Section 1-001. Short title

(1) This Code is the Vellmouth Consolidated Municipal Utility Tariff Code 2026.

(2) This Code consolidates the Tariff Code as originally made together with every amending, correcting and transitional provision made for the tariff year, each printed in the Part in which it was made and not written into the provision it affects.

### Section 1-002. Commencement

(1) This Code applies to water supplied, wastewater received and services provided by the Corporation in the tariff year.

(2) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Revenue Manager may decline to forward the statement of account.

### Section 1-003. Application to accounts

(1) This Code applies to every account recorded in the accounts register and to every property, meter, return, claim, entry and payment recorded for it in the other registers.

(2) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

### Section 1-004. Text that has legal effect

(1) Every provision of this Code has legal effect, except as provided by this section.

(2) The title, the preamble, the headings of Parts, Divisions and sections, and every paragraph beginning with the word Note have no legal effect.

(3) Division 15-9 sets out proposed amendments published for consultation. They have not been made and have no legal effect, whatever their form or wording.

(4) Part 16 contains Binding Rulings, Explanatory Memoranda and Illustrative Examples. The first line of each document states its designation. Only a document designated as a Binding Ruling has legal effect. An Explanatory Memorandum or an Illustrative Example has no legal effect, and a figure, method or statement in it is not law even where it purports to describe a provision of this Code.

(5) An amending provision applies to the period stated in it whether that period begins before or after the day on which it was made.

### Section 1-005. Binding Rulings prevail

(1) A Binding Ruling prevails over every provision of this Code with which it is inconsistent, to the extent of the inconsistency, and governs the manner in which the provision it concerns is applied.

(2) A Binding Ruling does not change a rate, amount or other figure unless it states the figure.

### Section 1-006. Order in which provisions apply

(1) The value of every rate, charge, allowance, factor, limit, period, designation, priority and amount under this Code is found by applying its provisions in the order set out in this section.

(2) First, the original provisions, read with every correction made by Division 15-2. A correction is read into the text it corrects as if that text had always been printed as corrected. This applies equally to a correction of a provision of Division 15-1, which then applies in its corrected form; a corrected figure, day or date of making is the figure, day or date of making of that provision.

(3) Second, any provision outside Part 15 that provides that a value stated in another provision is taken to be, is, or has effect as if it were, a different value, or that such a value is increased or reduced. Such a provision forms part of the original text and displaces, for the whole tariff year, the value stated in the provision to which it refers.

(4) Third, the amendments made by Division 15-1, in the order of the days on which they were made, as stated in subsection (1) of the section that makes them. Amendments made on the same day apply in ascending order of section number and then of subsection. Each amendment displaces, for the period for which it has effect, the value then in force; the value in force outside that period is unaffected.

(5) An amendment expressed to have effect from a day, or for the period beginning on a day, has effect from that day to the end of the period to which the value relates. An amendment expressed to have effect for a quarter only, or for a period from one day to another day, has effect for that quarter, or from the first day to the second day, both inclusive. An amendment expressed to have effect for a quarter and later quarters has effect from the first day of that quarter. An amendment that states no day or period has effect for the whole tariff year.

(6) A provision of Division 15-3 that establishes a rebate is an original provision of Part 9.

(7) Fourth, the Binding Rulings in Part 16, under section 1-005.

(8) An amendment of this Code does not revive a value that a later amendment has displaced, and the printing of a figure in an original provision is not evidence that the figure is in force.

### Section 1-007. Crown bound

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a declaration. Subject to this Division, an owner of land who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the summons within forty-two days.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a summons that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. The Tariff Committee must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the receipt of the application. A register entry that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to lodge the application.

(4) The Records Officer must enter in the register the zone code of each application received under section 1-004. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The Records Officer must enter in the register the property identifier of each connection agreement received under section 1-004. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a permit that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

### Section 1-008. Extraterritorial operation

(1) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 1-003.

(2) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the record is illegible, the General Manager may decline to endorse the access request. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an occupier who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the application within sixty days. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Revenue Manager may decline to lodge the review application. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the description of the works, the property identifier and the date of issue.

### Section 1-009. Relationship with other laws

(1) Subject to this Division, the Customer Liaison Officer must retain the record through the customer portal within sixty days after the end of the month concerned. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(2) A meter reader may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 1-003. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

(3) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing.

### Section 1-010. Saving of existing permits

(1) The Review Panel may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 1-004.

(2) Notwithstanding section 1-004, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for filing a declaration by a further twenty-eight days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 1-003. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 1-004. An authorised officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each connection agreement received under section 1-003.

(3) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the property identifier of each access request received under section 1-003. Notwithstanding section 1-003, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for serving a summons by a further sixty days. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an occupier to endorse a certificate within thirty days. Notwithstanding section 1-003, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Tariff Committee must endorse the notice through the customer portal within fourteen days after the close of the inspection.

### Section 1-011. Validity of acts done in good faith

(1) Notwithstanding section 1-003, a meter reader may extend the time for filing a summons by a further thirty days. The Revenue Manager must keep each permit for at least six years after the close of the inspection. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an occupier to certify a review application within fourteen days.

(2) A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. A meter reader must enter in the register the zone code of each record received under section 1-004. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 1-003. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the reference number, the postal address and the zone code.

(3) Notwithstanding section 1-003, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for forwarding a review application by a further sixty days.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Notwithstanding section 1-004, an inspection report served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

### Section 1-012. Notes and examples

(1) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to retain a connection agreement within ninety days. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) A record that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each statement of account received under section 1-004. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives an application may request a copy of the meter test report within sixty days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must produce the application in writing within sixty days after the end of the month concerned. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a licensed contractor who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the description of the works, the reference number and the signature of the officer.

#### Division 1-2. Rules of computation

### Section 1-080. Money and exact computation

(1) Every amount of money under this Code is computed and stated in cents.

(2) Rates per kilolitre, per kilogram, per square metre and per day are stated in dollars to four decimal places. Consumption is measured in litres; one kilolitre is 1,000 litres.

(3) No amount is rounded except as section 1-082 directs. Every intermediate quantity, including a fraction of a litre, of a cent or of a day's consumption, is carried exactly.

### Section 1-081. Methods of rounding

(1) To round an amount to the nearest cent, ties to even, is to take the nearest whole number of cents and, where the amount is exactly halfway between two whole numbers of cents, the even one.

(2) To round an amount half up is to take the nearest whole number of cents and, where the amount is exactly halfway between two whole numbers of cents, the greater one.

### Section 1-082. Where rounding occurs

(1) Amounts are rounded at the following points and by the following methods, and at no other point:

(a) the water supply charge of each meter interval, to the nearest cent, ties to even;

(b) the wastewater charge of each meter interval, half up;

(c) the daily service charges of each meter for the tariff year, summed over its days, to the nearest cent, ties to even;

(d) the stormwater charge of each property, half up;

(e) the trade waste charge of each return, to the nearest cent, ties to even;

(f) the catchment levy of each account, half up;

(g) the regional surcharge of each account, to the nearest cent, ties to even;

(h) the current-year amount of each rebate, the leak value of each leak claim multiplied by the rate of the rebate, each percentage limit of a rebate and the limit in section 9-002, to the nearest cent, ties to even;

(i) a minimum annual charge reduced in proportion to a number of days, half up; and

(j) the interest on each part of an instalment, half up.

### Section 1-083. Instruments to be read with this Division

(1) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 1-082. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A permit that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in writing, require an agent of an account holder to produce a register entry within fourteen days.

(2) A review application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

### Section 1-084. Counting of days

(1) A period described as from one day to another day includes both days.

(2) The tariff year has 365 days. A proportion of a year is a number of days divided by 365.

(3) Where a quantity is divided evenly over the days of a period, each day bears the quantity divided by the number of days in the period.

### Section 1-085. References to registers

(1) An application that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(2) Notwithstanding section 1-084, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a summons by a further ninety days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must forward the summons in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. A review application that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives a permit may request a copy of the notice within forty-two days.

(3) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Revenue Manager may decline to forward the access request.

### Section 1-086. Order of rebates

(1) Rebates are applied to an account in ascending order of their priority, being the priority stated in the provision that establishes each rebate, as that provision is in force.

(2) Rebates of the same priority are applied in ascending order of the section number of the provisions that establish them.

### Section 1-088. Business days

(1) A business day is a day that is not a Saturday, a Sunday or a public holiday listed in section 1-090.

(2) Where this Code requires a day to be moved to the next business day, it is moved forward one day at a time until it is a business day.

### Section 1-090. Public holidays

(1) The public holidays for the purposes of this Code are 1 January 2026, 26 January 2026, 9 March 2026, 3 April 2026, 6 April 2026, 15 May 2026, 8 June 2026, 17 August 2026, 5 October 2026, 16 November 2026, 25 December 2026, 28 December 2026, 1 January 2027, 26 January 2027, 15 February 2027, 8 March 2027, 26 March 2027, 29 March 2027 and 26 April 2027.

(2) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

### Section 1-092. Quarters

(1) The first quarter of the tariff year is January to March, the second quarter is April to June, the third quarter is July to September and the fourth quarter is October to December.

(2) A quarter is written Q1, Q2, Q3 or Q4 in a register.

### Section 1-094. Instruments of delegation

(1) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each meter test report received under section 1-092. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an occupier to register a record within forty-two days. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each inspection report for at least seven years after the close of the inspection. A meter reader must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made.

(3) For the purposes of this section, the General Manager must certify the review application by electronic mail to the address on the register within thirty days after the date of the decision. The Review Panel must enter in the register the zone code of each declaration received under section 1-092. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the property identifier of each summons received under section 1-092.

#### Division 1-3. Dictionary

### Section 1-201. Meaning of Volumetric Charges

(1) In this Code, Volumetric Charges means the sum of the Water Supply Charges and the Wastewater Charges of an account.

(2) A reference to Volumetric Charges includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-202. Meaning of meter interval

(1) In this Code, meter interval means a period formed under section 4-030.

### Section 1-203. Meaning of payments register

(1) In this Code, payments register means the register of payments (payments.csv).

### Section 1-204. Meaning of customer portal

(1) In this Code, customer portal means the electronic facility maintained by the General Manager for the lodgment of documents.

### Section 1-205. Meaning of zone

(1) In this Code, zone means a zone established by Part 3.

(2) A reference to zone includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-206. Meaning of properties register

(1) In this Code, properties register means the register of properties (properties.csv), which records for each property its account, zone, class, land area and the days of connection and disconnection.

(2) A reference to properties register includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-207. Meaning of Supply Total

(1) In this Code, Supply Total means the sum of the Base Charges of an account and its regional surcharge under Part 7.

(2) A reference to Supply Total includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-208. Meaning of Water Supply Charges

(1) In this Code, Water Supply Charges means the sum of the water supply charges under Part 4 for all meter intervals of all meters of the properties of an account.

### Section 1-209. Meaning of assessment date

(1) In this Code, assessment date means the date stated as the assessment date in the batch manifest (batch.json).

### Section 1-210. Meaning of public account

(1) In this Code, public account means an account whose account class is public.

### Section 1-211. Meaning of authorised officer

(1) In this Code, authorised officer means a person appointed as an authorised officer under Part 8.

### Section 1-212. Meaning of repair date

(1) In this Code, repair date means the date of repair recorded for a leak claim in the leak claims register (leak_claims.csv).

### Section 1-213. Meaning of Net Charge

(1) In this Code, Net Charge means the Gross Charge of an account, less the rebates applied under Part 9, plus any minimum charge top-up under Part 11.

(2) A reference to Net Charge includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-214. Meaning of community organisation

(1) In this Code, community organisation means an account that is a public account or whose holder form is association.

### Section 1-215. Meaning of network

(1) In this Code, network means the water supply and sewerage infrastructure owned or operated by the Corporation.

### Section 1-216. Meaning of occupier

(1) In this Code, occupier means the person in actual occupation of a property, whether or not that person holds the account.

(2) A reference to occupier includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-217. Meaning of Trade Waste Charges

(1) In this Code, Trade Waste Charges means the sum of the trade waste charges under section 5-110 for the properties of an account.

(2) A reference to Trade Waste Charges includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-218. Meaning of rebate

(1) In this Code, rebate means a rebate established by Part 9 or Division 15-3.

### Section 1-219. Meaning of billing zone

(1) In this Code, billing zone means the zone of an account determined under section 3-002.

(2) A reference to billing zone includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-220. Meaning of trade waste returns register

(1) In this Code, trade waste returns register means the register of trade waste returns (trade_waste.csv).

(2) A reference to trade waste returns register includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-221. Meaning of General Manager

(1) In this Code, General Manager means the General Manager of the Corporation.

(2) A reference to General Manager includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-222. Meaning of holder form

(1) In this Code, holder form means the form of the holder of an account recorded in the accounts register, being individual, partnership, company, trust, association or government body.

(2) A reference to holder form includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-223. Meaning of standpipe

(1) In this Code, standpipe means a temporary connection to a hydrant for the drawing of water.

(2) A reference to standpipe includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-224. Meaning of tariff year

(1) In this Code, tariff year means the year beginning on 1 January 2026 and ending on 31 December 2026.

(2) A reference to tariff year includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-225. Meaning of drought property

(1) In this Code, drought property means a property whose zone is designated a Drought Zone.

(2) A reference to drought property includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-226. Meaning of Review Panel

(1) In this Code, Review Panel means the Utility Tariff Review Panel continued by Part 14.

(2) A reference to Review Panel includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-227. Meaning of readings register

(1) In this Code, readings register means the register of meter readings (meter_reads.csv), which records each reading of a meter in litres and the day on which it was taken.

(2) A reference to readings register includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-228. Meaning of owner

(1) In this Code, owner means the person recorded as owner of land in the land titles register.

(2) A reference to owner includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-229. Meaning of hydrant

(1) In this Code, hydrant means a fire hydrant connected to the network.

(2) A reference to hydrant includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-230. Meaning of supply window

(1) In this Code, supply window means (a) for an account, the period from the later of 1 January 2026 and the day on which the account was opened to the earlier of 31 December 2026 and the day on which it was closed; (b) for a property, the part of the supply window of its account from the later of the first day of that window and the day on which the property was connected to the earlier of the last day of that window and the day on which it was disconnected; and (c) for a meter, the part of the supply window of its property from the later of the first day of that window and the day on which the meter was installed to the earlier of the last day of that window and the day on which it was removed. Each period includes both of its end days.

(2) A reference to supply window includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-231. Meaning of Large Meter

(1) In this Code, Large Meter means a meter of 80 mm or more whose supply window contains at least one day.

(2) A reference to Large Meter includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-232. Meaning of meters register

(1) In this Code, meters register means the register of meters (meters.csv), which records for each meter its property, its size in millimetres and the days of installation and removal.

(2) A reference to meters register includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-233. Meaning of carry-forward register

(1) In this Code, carry-forward register means the register of rebate amounts carried forward from earlier tariff years (carryforwards.csv).

### Section 1-234. Meaning of account class

(1) In this Code, account class means the class recorded for an account in the accounts register, being residential, commercial or public.

(2) A reference to account class includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-235. Meaning of strata scheme

(1) In this Code, strata scheme means a scheme registered under the strata titles legislation of the Province.

(2) A reference to strata scheme includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-236. Meaning of trade waste

(1) In this Code, trade waste means liquid waste discharged to the sewer from a commercial process.

### Section 1-237. Meaning of Drought Zone

(1) In this Code, Drought Zone means a zone designated a Drought Zone by its entry in Part 3.

### Section 1-238. Meaning of property

(1) In this Code, property means a parcel of land recorded in the properties register.

### Section 1-239. Meaning of Corporation

(1) In this Code, Corporation means the Corporation of the City of Vellmouth.

### Section 1-240. Meaning of plumbing work

(1) In this Code, plumbing work means work on a water service, sanitary drainage or fixture connected to the network.

(2) A reference to plumbing work includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-241. Meaning of commercial account

(1) In this Code, commercial account means an account whose account class is commercial.

### Section 1-242. Meaning of meter

(1) In this Code, meter means a device recorded in the meters register for measuring water supplied to a property.

### Section 1-243. Meaning of claims register

(1) In this Code, claims register means the register of rebate claims (concession_claims.csv).

### Section 1-244. Meaning of account

(1) In this Code, account means an account recorded in the accounts register (accounts.csv).

### Section 1-245. Meaning of Registrar of Accounts

(1) In this Code, Registrar of Accounts means the officer of the Corporation who keeps the registers.

### Section 1-246. Meaning of consumption

(1) In this Code, consumption means the quantity of water, in litres, recorded by a meter.

### Section 1-247. Meaning of class

(1) In this Code, class means a tariff class established by Part 2 or Division 15-3.

(2) A reference to class includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-084.

### Section 1-248. Meaning of residential account

(1) In this Code, residential account means an account whose account class is residential.

### Section 1-249. Meaning of Service Charges

(1) In this Code, Service Charges means the sum of the daily service charges under section 6-010 for all meters of an account and the stormwater charges under section 6-030 for all of its properties.

### Section 1-250. Meaning of rebate code

(1) In this Code, rebate code means the code by which a rebate is identified.

(2) A reference to rebate code includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-251. Meaning of backflow device

(1) In this Code, backflow device means a device that prevents water flowing back into the network from a property.

### Section 1-252. Meaning of Gross Charge

(1) In this Code, Gross Charge means the sum of the Supply Total, the catchment levy under section 5-210 and the Trade Waste Charges of an account.

### Section 1-253. Meaning of Wastewater Charges

(1) In this Code, Wastewater Charges means the sum of the wastewater charges under section 5-010 for all meter intervals of an account.

(2) A reference to Wastewater Charges includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-254. Meaning of Base Charges

(1) In this Code, Base Charges means the sum of the Volumetric Charges and the Service Charges of an account.

(2) A reference to Base Charges includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-255. Meaning of easement

(1) In this Code, easement means an easement for water supply, drainage or sewerage purposes.

### Section 1-256. Meaning of concession card

(1) In this Code, concession card means a pensioner concession card, a health care card or a veteran concession card recorded for an account in the accounts register; the register records them as pensioner, health and veteran.

(2) A reference to concession card includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

### Section 1-257. Meaning of register

(1) In this Code, register means a register mentioned in this Division, as kept by the Registrar of Accounts.

(2) A reference to register includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-003.

### Section 1-258. Meaning of active days

(1) In this Code, active days means the number of days in the supply window of an account.

(2) A reference to active days includes a reference to a matter treated as such under section 1-092.

## Part 2. Tariff classes

#### Division 2-1. General

### Section 2-001. Classification of properties

(1) Every property is assigned in the properties register to a class established by this Part or by Division 15-3.

(2) The entries in sections 2-101 to 2-172 together form Schedule 2. Each entry states the description, sector, block rates, catchment levy treatment, band and discharge factor of a class.

(3) Notwithstanding section 2-002, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for acknowledging a review application by a further sixty days. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the name of the occupier, the account number and the signature of the officer.

### Section 2-002. Bands

(1) Each class is assigned to Band A, Band B or Band C by paragraph (f) of its entry.

(2) The band of a class determines the daily allowances under section 4-020 that apply to the meter intervals of its properties.

### Section 2-003. Discharge factors

(1) The discharge factor of a class, stated in paragraph (g) of its entry, is the proportion of consumption taken to be discharged to the sewer under section 5-010.

### Section 2-004. Catchment levy treatment

(1) Paragraph (e) of each entry states whether the supply charges of the class are exempt from the catchment levy under section 5-210.

(2) Where paragraph (e) of an entry does not state that the supply charges of the class are exempt, they are not exempt.

### Section 2-005. Sectors

(1) The sector stated in paragraph (b) of an entry describes the users for whom the class was designed and does not of itself affect any charge.

(2) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the description of the works, the property identifier and the zone code. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

#### Division 2-2. Schedule 2: entries for classes

### Section 2-101. Class TC-101: Detached dwelling houses

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for detached dwelling houses, including car parking areas and landscaped grounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-103.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.3070 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.3650 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.9300 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 79 per cent.

(h) Inspection. In addition to any other requirement, the Tariff Committee must forward the review application by registered post within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served.

### Section 2-102. Class TC-102: Semi-detached and terrace dwellings

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of semi-detached and terrace dwellings, including staff amenities and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-164.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to lodge the notice.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.8990 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.5540 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.0870 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 80 per cent.

(h) Inspection. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each declaration received under section 8-010.

### Section 2-103. Class TC-103: Residential flat buildings of up to four units

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of residential flat buildings of up to four units, including irrigation of surrounds and ancillary storage, but does not include premises within Class TC-147.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 2-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.5400 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $1.9910 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.0030 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 87 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 2-005.

### Section 2-104. Class TC-104: Residential flat buildings of five or more units

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of residential flat buildings of five or more units, including ancillary storage and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-151.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the postal address, the zone code and the property identifier.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.3360 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.1140 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.4550 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 78 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 1 rate of this class was $1.3020 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-105. Class TC-105: Rural residential lifestyle lots

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of rural residential lifestyle lots, including staff amenities and wash-down areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-163.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Notwithstanding section 2-001, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.9860 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.7010 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.3820 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 81 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A meter test report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

### Section 2-106. Class TC-106: Retirement villages

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for retirement villages, including wash-down areas and irrigation of surrounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-123.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. The General Manager must keep each notice for at least six years after the receipt of the application. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.4860 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.3140 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.3960 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 73 per cent.

(h) Inspection. The Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 2-005.

### Section 2-107. Class TC-107: Boarding and rooming houses

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for boarding and rooming houses, including irrigation of surrounds and fire sprinkler connections, but does not include premises within Class TC-103.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 4-030.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.2490 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.4470 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.8610 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 90 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. The General Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each declaration received under section 2-005.

### Section 2-108. Class TC-108: Caravan and manufactured home parks

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of caravan and manufactured home parks, including car parking areas and fire sprinkler connections, but does not include premises within Class TC-169.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.2980 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.4090 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.0570 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 76 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or an inspection report. Notwithstanding section 8-010, a meter reader may extend the time for certifying an application by a further fourteen days.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 2 rate of this class was $3.3320 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-109. Class TC-109: Dual occupancy dwellings

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of dual occupancy dwellings, including car parking areas and wash-down areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-140.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.3170 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.1920 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.5970 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 70 per cent.

(h) Inspection. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require an owner of land to serve a declaration within forty-two days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a licensed contractor who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the notice within fourteen days.

### Section 2-110. Class TC-110: Ancillary granny flats

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for ancillary granny flats, including ancillary storage and staff amenities, but does not include premises within Class TC-113.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Notwithstanding section 2-001, an authorised officer may extend the time for filing a review application by a further forty-two days.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.2950 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.2390 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.8030 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 75 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or an inspection report. For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the notice within fourteen days.

### Section 2-111. Class TC-111: Residential properties with private swimming pools

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for residential properties with private swimming pools, including car parking areas and landscaped grounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-163.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 4-030.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.4880 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.2910 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.7100 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 76 per cent.

(h) Inspection. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

### Section 2-112. Class TC-112: Strata townhouse complexes

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of strata townhouse complexes, including fire sprinkler connections and staff amenities, but does not include premises within Class TC-149.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 4-030.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.3080 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.4690 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.2890 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 87 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Customer Liaison Officer must endorse the summons through the customer portal within sixty days after the hearing of the matter.

### Section 2-113. Class TC-113: Student accommodation

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as student accommodation, including wash-down areas and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-105.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 2-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.6370 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.6060 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.0600 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 85 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Notwithstanding section 2-005, the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a declaration by a further ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a statement of account.

### Section 2-114. Class TC-114: Holiday cottages let short-term

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for holiday cottages let short-term, including staff amenities and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-156.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Notwithstanding section 2-001, a register entry served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.2910 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.3850 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.8460 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 87 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 2-005.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 3 rate of this class was $4.8160 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-115. Class TC-115: Farmhouses on rural holdings

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of farmhouses on rural holdings, including landscaped grounds and staff amenities, but does not include premises within Class TC-127.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a declaration.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.5770 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.2160 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.1190 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 77 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Where a meter reader is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, a meter reader may decline to forward the connection agreement. A register entry that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 1 rate of this class was $1.5100 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-116. Class TC-116: Vacant residential land with standpipe

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as vacant residential land with standpipe, including staff amenities and fire sprinkler connections, but does not include premises within Class TC-128.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 2-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.5030 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.6760 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.7130 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 77 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Notwithstanding section 8-010, the Review Panel may extend the time for endorsing a register entry by a further thirty days. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 2-117. Class TC-117: Residential care cottages

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as residential care cottages, including landscaped grounds and an onsite caretaker's residence, but does not include premises within Class TC-127.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 2-001. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to produce the notice.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.2020 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.7900 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.2690 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 73 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A meter reader may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 2-005.

### Section 2-118. Class TC-118: Heritage residential dwellings

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of heritage residential dwellings, including landscaped grounds and fire sprinkler connections, but does not include premises within Class TC-101.

(b) Sector. The class is a residential class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an application that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.9660 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.8790 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.6910 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band A.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 88 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 8-010. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 3 rate of this class was $3.6560 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-119. Class TC-119: Hotels and serviced apartments

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of hotels and serviced apartments, including staff amenities and landscaped grounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-129.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.2590 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.0080 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.4610 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 78 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 2-005. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the account number of each certificate received under section 8-010.

### Section 2-120. Class TC-120: Licensed clubs and taverns

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of licensed clubs and taverns, including fire sprinkler connections and wash-down areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-114.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a strata manager to forward a meter test report within thirty days.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.2800 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.3520 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.1500 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 86 per cent.

(h) Inspection. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post.

### Section 2-121. Class TC-121: Restaurants and cafes

(a) Description. This class comprises the operation on premises of restaurants and cafes, including wash-down areas and an onsite caretaker's residence, but does not include premises within Class TC-103.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A summons that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.0290 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.9370 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.3800 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 69 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Notwithstanding section 2-005, a permit served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 3 rate of this class was $4.3020 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-122. Class TC-122: Commercial laundries and linen services

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as commercial laundries and linen services, including landscaped grounds and irrigation of surrounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-138.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Notwithstanding section 2-001, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for endorsing a connection agreement by a further twenty-one days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a register entry.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.4790 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.2740 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.4450 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band C.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 62 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the signature of the officer.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 3 rate of this class was $3.4080 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-123. Class TC-123: Car washing establishments

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of car washing establishments, including irrigation of surrounds and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-113.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 2-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.4060 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.1720 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.5050 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 52 per cent.

(h) Inspection. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each meter test report for at least five years after the date of the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a certificate may request a copy of the record within forty-two days.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 1 rate of this class was $1.3190 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-124. Class TC-124: Breweries and distilleries

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as breweries and distilleries, including an onsite caretaker's residence and landscaped grounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-119.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.4310 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.3820 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $2.9050 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band C.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 94 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

### Section 2-125. Class TC-125: Soft drink and bottled water plants

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of soft drink and bottled water plants, including staff amenities and wash-down areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-120.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Subject to this Division, the Review Panel must endorse the meter test report by registered post within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.8210 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.7590 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.9870 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band C.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 83 per cent.

(h) Inspection. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

Note: In the tariff for 2025 the block 3 rate of this class was $3.9490 per kilolitre.

### Section 2-126. Class TC-126: Dairy processing works

(a) Description. This class comprises the occupation of premises as dairy processing works, including ancillary storage and landscaped grounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-158.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.0700 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.4870 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $3.4330 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band C.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 75 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Notwithstanding section 8-010, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for certifying an application by a further twenty-eight days. The Review Panel may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to retain a meter test report within ninety days.

### Section 2-127. Class TC-127: Abattoirs and meat processing

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of abattoirs and meat processing, including car parking areas and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-139.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

(c) Metering. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a summons that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $2.3650 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.3870 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.2560 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band C.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 52 per cent.

(h) Inspection. An authorised officer must keep each review application for at least six years after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 8-010, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a certificate by a further twenty-eight days.

### Section 2-128. Class TC-128: Fruit and vegetable packing sheds

(a) Description. This class comprises the use of premises for fruit and vegetable packing sheds, including an onsite caretaker's residence and irrigation of surrounds, but does not include premises within Class TC-159.

(c) Metering. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice through the customer portal, require an owner of land to file a record within twenty-eight days. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 2-001.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(i) for block 1 consumption, at $1.3230 per kilolitre;

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $2.0910 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $2.9150 per kilolitre.

(e) Catchment levy. Supply charges under this class are not exempt from the catchment levy.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 54 per cent.

(h) Inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. An application that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

### Section 2-129. Class TC-129: Bakeries and small food manufacture

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of bakeries and small food manufacture, including fire sprinkler connections and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-137.

(f) Band. The class is assigned to Band B.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 88 per cent.

(a) Description. This class comprises the conduct on premises of office buildings, including an onsite caretaker's residence and car parking areas, but does not include premises within Class TC-170.

(b) Sector. The class is a commercial class.

Notwithstanding section 2-001, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for certifying an application by a further ninety days.

(d) Block rates. The water supply charge under this class is charged:

(ii) for block 2 consumption, at $3.4590 per kilolitre; and

(iii) for block 3 consumption, at $4.5680 per kilolitre.

(g) Discharge factor. The discharge factor of the class is 87 per cent.

## Part 3. Zones

#### Division 3-1. General

### Section 3-001. Zones and regions

(1) The area of supply is divided into the zones established by the entries in sections 3-101 to 3-148, which together form Schedule 3.

(2) Each zone forms part of the North, South, West or East region, as stated in paragraph (b) of its entry.

### Section 3-002. Billing zone of an account

(1) The billing zone of an account is the zone of the property of the account that was connected earliest, among the properties whose supply window contains at least one day.

(2) Where two or more of those properties were connected on the same earliest day, the billing zone is the zone of the one with the lowest property identifier.

(3) The region of an account is the region of its billing zone.

### Section 3-003. Drought Zones

(1) A zone is a Drought Zone if paragraph (c) of its entry designates it a Drought Zone.

(2) For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-eight days. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

### Section 3-004. Boundaries of zones

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an applicant to serve a register entry within thirty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to retain a statement of account within thirty days. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a declaration may request a copy of the inspection report within twenty-one days. Notwithstanding section 3-003, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a statement of account by a further twenty-eight days. In addition to any other requirement, a licensed contractor who receives a declaration may request a copy of the permit within fourteen days.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the meter test report within thirty days.

#### Division 3-2. Schedule 3: entries for zones

### Section 3-101. Zone Z-01: Ardleigh

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Ardleigh on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a party to a review to produce an inspection report within ninety days.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The zone is designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $3.1200 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.5340 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. The Records Officer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 3-004. An authorised officer must enter in the register the postal address of each meter test report received under section 3-004.

### Section 3-102. Zone Z-02: Bramblecote

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Bramblecote on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 3-004.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.9220 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.3870 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 8-020. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the permit.

### Section 3-103. Zone Z-03: Carrowmore

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Carrowmore on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.7670 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.1310 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. The General Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each register entry received under section 8-020. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each statement of account received under section 8-020.

### Section 3-104. Zone Z-04: Dunstable Reach

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Dunstable Reach on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 3-004.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.1460 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.1580 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. Notwithstanding section 3-004, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A register entry that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

### Section 3-143. Zone Z-43: Underwood

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Underwood on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 3-004.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.1820 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.4320 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a declaration.

### Section 3-144. Zone Z-44: Verwood

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Verwood on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. An authorised officer must enter in the register the reference number of each application received under section 3-004.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the North region.

(c) Designations. The zone is designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $1.5980 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.5030 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 8-020.

### Section 3-145. Zone Z-45: Whinmoor

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Whinmoor on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each permit for at least seven years after the receipt of the application.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the West region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $1.4060 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.2770 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. A certificate that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 3-146. Zone Z-46: Yelverton

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Yelverton on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. Notwithstanding section 3-004, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the East region.

(c) Designations. The zone is designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $1.5750 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.4520 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. Notwithstanding section 8-020, a summons served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

### Section 3-147. Zone Z-47: Ashgrove

(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Ashgrove on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. Notwithstanding section 3-004, a register entry served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The zone is not designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.4920 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.2040 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 3-004.

### Section 3-148. Zone Z-48: Brookfield
