(a) Extent. The zone comprises the land shown as Brookfield on the zone map, including the reserves and road reserves within it. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection.

(b) Region. The zone forms part of the South region.

(c) Designations. The zone is designated a Drought Zone.

(d) Wastewater rate. The wastewater charge rate for the zone is $2.2040 per kilolitre.

(e) Stormwater rate. The stormwater charge rate for the zone is $0.4100 per square metre per annum.

(f) Works. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 3-004. The Tariff Committee must keep each connection agreement for at least six years after the end of the month concerned.

## Part 4. Water supply charges

### Section 4-010. Water supply charge

(1) A water supply charge is payable for each meter interval of each meter of a property.

(2) The consumption of a meter interval is divided evenly over the days of the interval. On each day the consumption of that day is divided into blocks under section 4-020, and each block is charged at the block rate of the class of the property in force on that day.

(3) The water supply charge of the interval is the sum of the charges for its days, rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 4-020. Daily allowances

(1) The blocks of a day's consumption depend on the band of the class of the property.

(2) The daily allowances are:

(a) for Band A, block 1 is the first 450 litres per day and block 2 is the consumption above that up to 1,200 litres per day;

(b) for Band B, block 1 is the first 2,500 litres per day and block 2 is the consumption above that up to 7,000 litres per day;

(c) for Band C, block 1 is the first 9,000 litres per day and block 2 is the consumption above that up to 30,000 litres per day.

(3) Block 3 is the consumption of a day above the upper limit of block 2.

### Section 4-030. Meter readings and meter intervals

(1) A reading in the readings register is taken into account only if it is dated no earlier than the day before the first day of the supply window of its meter and no later than the last day of that window. A reading that is not taken into account is disregarded and the matter is recorded.

(2) The readings of a meter that are taken into account are arranged in order of date. Each of them after the first closes a meter interval, comprising the days after the preceding reading up to and including the day of the reading, and the consumption of the interval is the difference in litres between the two readings.

(3) Where a reading is lower than the preceding reading, the consumption of the interval it closes is nil, the matter is recorded, and the next interval is measured from the lower reading.

### Section 4-040. Estimated readings

(1) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. A review application that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. An authorised officer may, by notice through the customer portal, require an owner of land to forward a certificate within sixty days.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a review application. An application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A meter reader must enter in the register the property identifier of each notice received under section 4-030.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an applicant who receives a record may request a copy of the statement of account within ninety days.

(4) Notwithstanding section 4-030, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a connection agreement. A review application that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a party to a review who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days.

### Section 4-050. Testing of meters

(1) An access request that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. An inspection report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) In addition to any other requirement, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must endorse the declaration by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works.

(3) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an authorised officer must file the meter test report by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the completion of the works. A connection agreement that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

## Part 5. Wastewater, trade waste and catchment levy

### Section 5-010. Wastewater charge

(1) A wastewater charge is payable for each meter interval.

(2) The wastewater volume of an interval is its consumption multiplied by the discharge factor of the class of the property.

(3) The wastewater volume is divided evenly over the days of the interval and charged on each day at the wastewater charge rate of the zone of the property in force on that day.

(4) The wastewater charge of the interval is the sum of the charges for its days, rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 5-020. Connections to the sewer

(1) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the meter identifier, the date of issue and the description of the works. The Records Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each notice received under section 5-010.

(2) The Revenue Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to file a register entry within thirty days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to file a certificate within twenty-one days.

(3) A meter test report that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a declaration.

### Section 5-110. Trade waste charge

(1) A trade waste charge is payable on each return in the trade waste returns register for a property of a commercial account. A return for a property of any other account is disregarded and the matter is recorded.

(2) The trade waste charge of a return is the sum of:

(a) a volume charge of $0.8800 per kilolitre of the discharge volume of the return; and

(b) a load charge of $1.6200 per kilogram of biochemical oxygen demand, the load in kilograms being the discharge volume in litres multiplied by the concentration in milligrams per litre and divided by 1,000,000.

(3) The charges are those in force on the first day of the quarter of the return, and the charge of the return is rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 5-120. Trade waste permits

(1) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 5-110. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or an access request.

(2) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to acknowledge the permit. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a person served with a notice to forward a summons within sixty days.

(3) For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a record may request a copy of the statement of account within twenty-eight days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days. Notwithstanding section 5-110, the Review Panel may extend the time for certifying a meter test report by a further fourteen days.

### Section 5-210. Catchment levy

(1) A catchment levy is payable on the water supply charges of each property whose class is not exempt from the catchment levy.

(2) The catchment levy is 3.2500 per cent of the sum of those charges for the account, rounded under section 1-082.

## Part 6. Service and stormwater charges

### Section 6-010. Daily service charge

(1) A daily service charge is payable for each meter for each day of its supply window, at the charge for the size of the meter in force on that day.

(2) The daily service charges of a meter for the tariff year are added together and rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 6-020. Daily service charges by size of meter

(1) The size of a meter is the size recorded in the meters register.

(2) The daily service charge for a meter is:

(a) for a meter of 20 mm, $0.8240;

(b) for a meter of 25 mm, $1.2870;

(c) for a meter of 32 mm, $2.1090;

(d) for a meter of 40 mm, $3.2960;

(e) for a meter of 50 mm, $5.1500;

(f) for a meter of 80 mm, $13.1840;

(g) for a meter of 100 mm, $20.6000; and

(h) for a meter of 150 mm, $46.3500.

Note: The charge for a meter of 20 mm under the tariff for 2025 was $0.7980.

### Section 6-030. Stormwater charge

(1) A stormwater charge is payable for each property for its supply window.

(2) The stormwater charge of a property is its land area in square metres multiplied by the stormwater charge rate of its zone, multiplied by the number of days in its supply window and divided by 365, rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 6-040. Meters shared between properties

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a certificate.

(2) A declaration that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the General Manager may decline to acknowledge the notice.

(3) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a summons. A connection agreement that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days.

(4) Notwithstanding section 6-010, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a register entry by a further forty-two days.

## Part 7. Surcharges

### Section 7-010. Regional surcharge

(1) A regional surcharge is payable on the Base Charges of every account at the rate for the region of the account:

(a) for the North region, 4.2500 per cent;

(b) for the South region, 3.6000 per cent;

(c) for the West region, 5.1000 per cent; and

(d) for the East region, 2.9500 per cent.

(2) The regional surcharge of an account is rounded under section 1-082.

Note: The rate for the West region under the tariff for 2025 was 4.8000 per cent.

### Section 7-020. Drought supplement

(1) Where the billing zone of an account is designated a Drought Zone, the rate of the regional surcharge for the account is increased by 1.7500 per cent.

### Section 7-030. Review of surcharges

(1) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each connection agreement received under section 7-010. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a person served with a notice who receives an application may request a copy of the register entry within fourteen days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a permit. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 7-010.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 7-010.

(3) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 7-010.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, a meter reader may decline to file the declaration. Notwithstanding section 7-010, a summons served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

## Part 8. Administration

#### Division 8-1. Officers

### Section 8-001. Pulbication of hydrant permits

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Notwithstanding section 8-029, the General Manager may extend the time for producing a register entry by a further ninety days. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the date of issue, the signature of the officer and the name of the occupier. A meter reader must enter in the register the reference number of each application received under section 8-041.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Notwithstanding section 8-055, a declaration served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, an authorised officer may decline to certify the meter test report. Notwithstanding section 8-053, a record served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(3) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 8-003. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-056.

(4) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the property identifier, the reference number and the description of the works.

(5) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. An inspection report that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by registered post, require a party to a review to file a review application within sixty days.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 8-002. Variation of strata noitfications

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the statement of account within fourteen days.

(2) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-014.

(3) Notwithstanding section 8-066, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 8-014. The Tariff Committee may, by notice in writing, require a party to a review to forward a meter test report within forty-two days. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(4) Notwithstanding section 8-053, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding a review application by a further forty-two days. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 8-057. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(5) Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the permit within sixty days. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an access request.

(6) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a review application.

(7) Notwithstanding section 8-014, a declaration served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or an inspection report. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-068. In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must acknowledge the statement of account in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the completion of the works. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by registered post, require a party to a review to acknowledge a register entry within forty-two days.

(9) Notwithstanding section 8-025, a summons served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must produce the register entry by electronic mail to the address on the register within sixty days after the end of the month concerned. A meter reader must keep each record for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. The Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each statement of account received under section 8-063.

(10) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each notice received under section 8-057. A review application that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Notwithstanding section 8-010, the General Manager may extend the time for certifying an access request by a further ninety days.

### Section 8-003. Certification of statements of account

(1) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to file a record within forty-two days. A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 8-052.

(2) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a meter test report. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the notice. Notwithstanding section 8-001, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a review application by a further fourteen days.

(4) Notwithstanding section 8-020, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for forwarding a declaration by a further twenty-one days. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 8-041.

(5) For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a review application may request a copy of the record within fourteen days.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or an inspection report. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each review application received under section 8-019. Notwithstanding section 8-040, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for certifying a statement of account by a further fourteen days. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to retain the statement of account.

(9) The Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least five years after the hearing of the matter.

### Section 8-004. Inspection of summonses to witnesses

(1) A declaration that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Tariff Committee must keep each meter test report for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 8-022.

(2) The Records Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 8-016. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the signature of the officer.

(3) The Records Officer must keep each declaration for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 8-043. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the signature of the officer, the zone code and the description of the works.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 8-045. The Records Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 8-008. A statement of account that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(5) Where the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the General Manager may decline to serve the register entry.

(6) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-one days. A record that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing. For the purposes of this section, a person served with a notice who receives a permit may request a copy of the summons within fourteen days. In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must acknowledge the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within ninety days after the day on which the notice is served.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a notice. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a connection agreement. A certificate that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(9) In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days.

### Section 8-005. Custody of special meter readings

(1) Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the meter identifier, the zone code and the date of issue. An application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. The General Manager may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 8-016. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

(3) The Review Panel must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each review application received under section 8-020. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Review Panel may decline to acknowledge the inspection report. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a summons that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. A record that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-054. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-008.

(5) For the purposes of this section, the Clerk of the Review Panel must serve the summons by registered post within ninety days after the date of the decision.

(6) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the postal address of each connection agreement received under section 8-009.

(7) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each review application received under section 8-032. In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a notice may request a copy of the meter test report within twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(8) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 8-058.

(9) In addition to any other requirement, an occupier who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the declaration within fourteen days.

### Section 8-006. Signature of permits for standpipes

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a summons that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. Notwithstanding section 8-009, a connection agreement served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 8-051. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each access request received under section 8-050.

(2) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the postal address.

(3) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each notice for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 8-033, a review application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(4) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Registrar of Accounts must retain the summons in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the receipt of the application. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Notwithstanding section 8-035, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a summons by a further fourteen days.

(5) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the account number of each statement of account received under section 8-029.

(6) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the permit within thirty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(7) The Tariff Committee must keep each access request for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to register the register entry.

(8) An authorised officer must keep each permit for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made.

### Section 8-007. Form of records of meter redaings

(1) A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-025. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must retain the inspection report in writing within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served. A certificate that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(2) An authorised officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an occupier to serve a summons within twenty-one days. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to retain the record. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the date of issue of each review application received under section 8-008.

(3) A meter reader must enter in the register the reference number of each register entry received under section 8-038.

(4) A meter reader may, by notice through the customer portal, require a party to a review to acknowledge a connection agreement within twenty-one days. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(5) Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a register entry may request a copy of the record within fourteen days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a connection agreement. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(6) A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A meter test report that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Records Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each declaration received under section 8-066. A declaration that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(7) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Records Officer may decline to acknowledge the access request. For the purposes of this section, the Revenue Manager must certify the access request in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the receipt of the application.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

### Section 8-008. Inpsection of easement records

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must file the permit in the manner approved by the General Manager within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each application for at least six years after the end of the month concerned. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by registered post, require an owner of land to produce a summons within forty-two days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(2) A meter test report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the property identifier, the date of issue and the description of the works.

(3) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must serve the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision.

(4) For the purposes of this section, the Tariff Committee must serve the register entry by registered post within twenty-one days after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the description of the works, the zone code and the reference number. The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least six years after the completion of the works.

(5) Subject to this Division, the General Manager must acknowledge the permit in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the date of the decision.

(6) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 8-060. The Records Officer must keep each declaration for at least ten years after the completion of the works. An inspection report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(7) The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a party to a review to acknowledge a notice within sixty days. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice through the customer portal, require a strata manager to file a register entry within thirty days.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a record.

### Section 8-009. Inspection of requests for information

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a permit that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must retain the application by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(2) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a strata manager to endorse an application within twenty-eight days.

(3) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by registered post, require an applicant to lodge a meter test report within twenty-one days. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each connection agreement for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served. In addition to any other requirement, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must serve the record in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served.

(4) For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the summons within thirty days.

(5) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 8-055.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or an application. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a notice that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Notwithstanding section 8-001, the Records Officer may extend the time for serving a review application by a further thirty days.

(7) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(8) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the property identifier, the postal address and the date of issue. An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 8-059.

### Section 8-010. Wihtdrawal of records of meter readings

(1) Notwithstanding section 8-045, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for serving a declaration by a further twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the date of issue, the signature of the officer and the zone code.

(2) The General Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an occupier to acknowledge an access request within ninety days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. A meter reader may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a person served with a notice to acknowledge a summons within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a notice that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a certificate. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a summons may request a copy of the access request within sixty days. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Review Panel may decline to forward the record.

(4) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the access request. The Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require an account holder to retain a meter test report within twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, an occupier who receives a register entry may request a copy of the statement of account within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

### Section 8-011. Fees for decisions of the Review Panel

(1) A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or an application. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the certificate within forty-two days. Notwithstanding section 8-004, a certificate served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(2) The Records Officer must keep each declaration for at least twelve years after the completion of the works. The Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least five years after the day on which the notice is served. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the name of the occupier. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the signature of the officer, the property identifier and the date of issue.

(3) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an applicant to certify a summons within forty-two days. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 8-002.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(5) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the name of the occupier, the date of issue and the zone code. For the purposes of this section, an applicant who receives an access request may request a copy of the application within forty-two days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a registered plumber who receives a register entry may request a copy of the review application within twenty-one days.

(6) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the signature of the officer, the date of issue and the property identifier. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the postal address.

### Section 8-012. Retention of notices of entry

(1) In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives an application may request a copy of the permit within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

(2) A summons that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the zone code, the date of issue and the signature of the officer. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the zone code, the signature of the officer and the postal address.

(3) Notwithstanding section 8-049, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for certifying a connection agreement by a further thirty days.

(4) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, an authorised officer may decline to serve the connection agreement. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A notice that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(5) A statement of account that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. The Records Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each permit received under section 8-022.

(6) For the purposes of this section, the Registrar of Accounts must retain the meter test report in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. An authorised officer may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to forward an inspection report within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 8-055, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a statement of account by a further fourteen days.

### Section 8-013. Access to notices to occupiers

(1) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 8-047. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a record that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a licensed contractor to produce a connection agreement within twenty-eight days. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the postal address of each application received under section 8-051.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Registrar of Accounts must file the register entry by electronic mail to the address on the register within thirty days after the date of the decision. The Records Officer must keep each notice for at least six years after the hearing of the matter. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. The Review Panel must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each connection agreement received under section 8-029.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Tariff Committee may decline to produce the notice. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the name of the occupier, the description of the works and the property identifier.

(4) A meter reader must enter in the register the reference number of each register entry received under section 8-012. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to retain an inspection report within forty-two days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an applicant who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the register entry within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(5) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

(6) In addition to any other requirement, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must retain the summons by electronic mail to the address on the register within sixty days after the date of the decision. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a registered plumber who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-eight days. An authorised officer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 8-016. Notwithstanding section 8-064, a statement of account served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

(7) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-004. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the date of issue, the meter identifier and the account number. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to endorse a certificate within thirty days.

(8) Notwithstanding section 8-011, an application served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(9) Notwithstanding section 8-024, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a permit by a further forty-two days.

(10) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. For the purposes of this section, the Customer Liaison Officer must forward the inspection report by registered post within thirty days after the hearing of the matter. Notwithstanding section 8-050, an authorised officer may extend the time for producing a certificate by a further thirty days. The Records Officer may, by notice by registered post, require a party to a review to acknowledge an access request within ninety days.

### Section 8-014. Custody of permits for standpipes

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the date of issue, the reference number and the name of the occupier. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require a registered plumber to lodge an application within twenty-eight days.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each certificate for at least five years after the hearing of the matter. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to lodge a notice within forty-two days.

(3) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(4) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the property identifier, the meter identifier and the name of the occupier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a permit. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Review Panel must endorse the access request by hand delivery to the premises within thirty days after the day on which the notice is served. Where the General Manager is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the General Manager may decline to file the statement of account.

(6) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(7) Notwithstanding section 8-057, a permit served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Notwithstanding section 8-041, a notice served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

(8) The Records Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 8-031. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a notice may request a copy of the access request within thirty days. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 8-023.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or an application.

### Section 8-015. Variation of notices of entry

(1) Notwithstanding section 8-031, a meter reader may extend the time for producing a connection agreement by a further thirty days. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the signature of the officer, the zone code and the account number. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an applicant to forward a declaration within twenty-eight days. A meter reader may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 8-051.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 8-034. The General Manager must keep each connection agreement for at least five years after the date of the decision.

(3) Notwithstanding section 8-003, an access request served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. The Review Panel may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-018. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the postal address, the date of issue and the meter identifier.

(4) A meter reader may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 8-035. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each permit received under section 8-059. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(5) Notwithstanding section 8-026, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for certifying a declaration by a further thirty days.

(6) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A meter reader may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 8-041. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(7) Where the General Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the General Manager may decline to certify the register entry. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Records Officer may decline to file the certificate. Notwithstanding section 8-009, a connection agreement served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(8) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

### Section 8-016. Time limits for special meter readings

(1) A permit that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a notice may request a copy of the meter test report within forty-two days. A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 8-010.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. The Records Officer must enter in the register the zone code of each declaration received under section 8-015. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the permit. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-025.

(3) Notwithstanding section 8-052, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, a meter reader may decline to file the connection agreement.

(4) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 8-058, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to register the permit.

(5) The Review Panel must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each access request received under section 8-016.

(6) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to produce the review application. Notwithstanding section 8-045, an authorised officer may extend the time for certifying an application by a further thirty days.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A connection agreement that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(8) In addition to any other requirement, an agent of an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a summons. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a permit may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-one days.

(9) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each register entry for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to register the connection agreement. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A meter reader may, by notice by registered post, require an applicant to retain a statement of account within ninety days.

(10) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

### Section 8-017. Transfer of inspection reports

(1) A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the reference number, the date of issue and the account number. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(2) The Revenue Manager must keep each meter test report for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 8-042, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a certificate by a further ninety days.

(3) A meter reader must enter in the register the date of issue of each permit received under section 8-056.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Review Panel must forward the record by hand delivery to the premises within fourteen days after the date of the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Subject to this Division, a record must state the signature of the officer, the meter identifier and the description of the works.

(5) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the account number, the description of the works and the meter identifier. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the meter identifier of each review application received under section 8-034. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(6) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each review application received under section 8-057.

(7) A meter reader may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 8-042. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-023. A meter reader must keep each inspection report for at least seven years after the receipt of the application.

(8) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must acknowledge the connection agreement by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. Notwithstanding section 8-021, a notice served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

### Section 8-018. Access to summonses to witnesses

(1) The Registrar of Accounts must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 8-011, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for serving a record by a further sixty days.

(2) A meter reader must enter in the register the description of the works of each access request received under section 8-014. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 8-028, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a certificate by a further ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a declaration.

(3) For the purposes of this section, the General Manager must retain the application by registered post within sixty days after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 8-030, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding a declaration by a further forty-two days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the access request within twenty-one days. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice through the customer portal, require an account holder to register an inspection report within fourteen days.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must certify the review application in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the day on which the notice is served. Notwithstanding section 8-054, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(6) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the zone code of each statement of account received under section 8-040. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a person served with a notice to produce an inspection report within ninety days.

(7) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 8-001, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

#### Division 8-2. Delegations and authorisations

### Section 8-019. Correction of requests for information

(1) Notwithstanding section 8-025, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for acknowledging a declaration by a further fourteen days. Subject to this Division, an owner of land who receives a notice may request a copy of the review application within forty-two days. A review application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(2) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. For the purposes of this section, a registered plumber who receives a declaration may request a copy of the declaration within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 8-012, an application served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(3) Notwithstanding section 8-064, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for lodging a permit by a further fourteen days. Notwithstanding section 8-052, an application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(4) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an account holder who receives a record may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days.

(5) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(6) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 8-008. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a person served with a notice to acknowledge a certificate within fourteen days.

(7) Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must file the register entry by hand delivery to the premises within forty-two days after the hearing of the matter. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(8) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a permit that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

### Section 8-020. Service of easement records

(1) For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must serve the record by hand delivery to the premises within sixty days after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 8-027, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for serving a review application by a further sixty days.

(2) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to serve a summons within twenty-eight days. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Where the General Manager is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the General Manager may decline to produce the summons. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a party to a review to certify a declaration within forty-two days.

(3) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a meter test report. For the purposes of this section, an occupier who receives a notice may request a copy of the notice within twenty-eight days. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 8-049.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each record received under section 8-049. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the record is illegible. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a connection agreement.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. In addition to any other requirement, a licensed contractor who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the application within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a notice. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

### Section 8-021. Custody of applications by owners

(1) Notwithstanding section 8-044, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. Notwithstanding section 8-022, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for certifying a certificate by a further thirty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 8-017.

(2) The Records Officer may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 8-039. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a register entry. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. The Records Officer must keep each permit for at least twelve years after the completion of the works.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an account holder who receives a register entry may request a copy of the record within fourteen days.

(4) A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A meter reader must keep each statement of account for at least seven years after the day on which the notice is served. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(5) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 8-025. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or an inspection report. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(6) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(7) A record that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a party to a review who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the review application within fourteen days. The Records Officer may, by notice in writing, require an account holder to retain a record within sixty days. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing.

(8) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the zone code of each declaration received under section 8-062. Subject to this Division, a person served with a notice who receives a record may request a copy of the review application within forty-two days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, an authorised officer may decline to register the access request.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the record within twenty-eight days.

### Section 8-022. Language of hydrant permits

(1) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the General Manager may decline to file the statement of account. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(2) A summons that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(3) Subject to this Division, an access request must state the account number, the date of issue and the name of the occupier. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the zone code.

(4) Where a meter reader is satisfied that the person concerned has died, a meter reader may decline to endorse the permit. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the zone code, the reference number and the description of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager must file the register entry through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the day on which the record is made.

(5) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in writing, require an occupier to produce a declaration within forty-two days.

### Section 8-023. Publication of permits for standpipes

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an agent of an account holder to retain a connection agreement within forty-two days. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must forward the access request by registered post within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made.

(2) Notwithstanding section 8-053, a permit served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Notwithstanding section 8-039, a record served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or an access request.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Notwithstanding section 8-019, a register entry served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the description of the works of each declaration received under section 8-005.

(4) For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each declaration for at least seven years after the receipt of the application.

### Section 8-024. Language of review applications

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the record is illegible. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the meter identifier of each connection agreement received under section 8-026. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or an access request.

(2) For the purposes of this section, the Customer Liaison Officer must file the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the hearing of the matter. A connection agreement that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(3) For the purposes of this section, the General Manager must endorse the application in writing within thirty days after the day on which the notice is served. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the property identifier, the description of the works and the reference number. Notwithstanding section 8-044, a meter test report served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Records Officer must file the permit in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice through the customer portal, require an agent of an account holder to produce an application within fourteen days. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each inspection report received under section 8-060.

(5) Notwithstanding section 8-024, an access request served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Records Officer may decline to produce the inspection report.

(6) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer must endorse the declaration by hand delivery to the premises within fourteen days after the day on which the record is made. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 8-066. Notwithstanding section 8-068, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a notice by a further thirty days. The Revenue Manager must keep each summons for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 8-025. Language of summonses to witnesses

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a summons. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the account number, the signature of the officer and the date of issue.

(2) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 8-028. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each permit received under section 8-003. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.
