(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel must acknowledge the record in the manner approved by the General Manager within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to endorse the connection agreement. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the property identifier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(4) Notwithstanding section 8-022, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. A certificate that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. A meter reader must keep each review application for at least five years after the date of the decision.

### Section 8-026. Destruction of access requests

(1) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the General Manager may decline to lodge the inspection report. Notwithstanding section 8-032, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for endorsing a meter test report by a further sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing.

(2) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must forward the certificate by electronic mail to the address on the register within thirty days after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, a meter reader must register the inspection report by electronic mail to the address on the register within ninety days after the date of the decision.

(3) The Review Panel must enter in the register the zone code of each application received under section 8-061. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each review application for at least six years after the receipt of the application. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the reference number of each inspection report received under section 8-032.

(5) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to acknowledge an access request within thirty days.

(6) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 8-010. A meter reader must keep each statement of account for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made. An authorised officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each access request received under section 8-041. Notwithstanding section 8-039, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for lodging a permit by a further sixty days.

(7) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the account number, the zone code and the reference number. The Tariff Committee must keep each register entry for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. A register entry that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a strata manager to acknowledge a notice within ninety days.

### Section 8-027. Lodgment of connection applications

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must forward the declaration in writing within forty-two days after the date of the decision. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the date of issue. A declaration that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(2) A declaration that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the postal address, the meter identifier and the property identifier. A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(3) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(4) The Revenue Manager must keep each inspection report for at least six years after the end of the month concerned.

(5) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the record within forty-two days.

### Section 8-028. Time limits for statements of account

(1) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Revenue Manager may decline to acknowledge the statement of account. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-010. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the account number, the reference number and the name of the occupier.

(2) Notwithstanding section 8-010, an access request served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Notwithstanding section 8-062, an application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 8-038. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an account holder to endorse a certificate within twenty-eight days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept an access request that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. The Review Panel may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 8-045.

(4) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 8-029. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each notice received under section 8-007.

### Section 8-029. Retention of decisions of the Review Panel

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each statement of account for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the description of the works of each statement of account received under section 8-020. A meter reader may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a licensed contractor to file a certificate within forty-two days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a record may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader must produce the certificate through the customer portal within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-041. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a strata manager who receives an application may request a copy of the permit within fourteen days.

(3) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-030. In addition to any other requirement, a registered plumber who receives a notice may request a copy of the summons within twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the property identifier.

(4) Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must serve the meter test report in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the completion of the works. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(5) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a person served with a notice to certify a statement of account within thirty days. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(6) An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A meter reader may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to produce a statement of account within sixty days.

### Section 8-030. Language of records of meter readings

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each inspection report for at least six years after the close of the inspection. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 8-043. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 8-044. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(2) The Review Panel must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned. Notwithstanding section 8-006, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for producing a certificate by a further ninety days.

(3) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal. For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the summons within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the account number, the reference number and the zone code.

(4) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(5) In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the statement of account within forty-two days. A declaration that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Notwithstanding section 8-044, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

### Section 8-031. Lodgment of declarations by agents

(1) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 8-007.

(2) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Review Panel may decline to serve the access request. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an owner of land who receives a review application may request a copy of the connection agreement within thirty days. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to forward the connection agreement.

(3) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Review Panel must serve the meter test report by registered post within ninety days after the receipt of the application. Notwithstanding section 8-052, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a record by a further ninety days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must serve the declaration in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-eight days after the day on which the record is made.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a summons that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the meter identifier, the reference number and the zone code. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the zone code, the signature of the officer and the description of the works.

(5) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a strata manager to register a record within sixty days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a record. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(6) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each summons for at least seven years after the completion of the works. An authorised officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 8-005. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a review application. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the meter identifier and the account number.

### Section 8-032. Signature of hydrant permits

(1) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to register a review application within fourteen days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing.

(2) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an agent of an account holder who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the statement of account within fourteen days. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. In addition to any other requirement, the Registrar of Accounts must forward the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within forty-two days after the close of the inspection.

(3) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 8-053. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(4) Subject to this Division, a record must state the meter identifier, the account number and the description of the works.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 8-068. An authorised officer must keep each inspection report for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter. For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives a declaration may request a copy of the permit within thirty days.

(6) A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the property identifier, the zone code and the account number.

(7) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, an authorised officer may decline to file the certificate.

(8) Notwithstanding section 8-013, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for endorsing a notice by a further twenty-eight days. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the signature of the officer, the description of the works and the meter identifier.

(9) A declaration that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

### Section 8-033. Time limits for decisions of the Review Panel

(1) Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the date of issue, the zone code and the name of the occupier.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each application for at least five years after the end of the month concerned. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. Notwithstanding section 8-036, a notice served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(3) Subject to this Division, a record must state the postal address, the name of the occupier and the account number. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to file the register entry. An authorised officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each register entry received under section 8-043.

(4) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each certificate received under section 8-050. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a strata manager to forward a notice within twenty-one days. Notwithstanding section 8-029, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(5) An authorised officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 8-033. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 8-010. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the reference number, the account number and the name of the occupier.

(6) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each connection agreement received under section 8-048.

(7) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 8-010. For the purposes of this section, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must file the summons by registered post within twenty-eight days after the close of the inspection.

(8) Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the postal address, the date of issue and the property identifier. A review application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each application for at least twelve years after the day on which the record is made.

(9) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Revenue Manager may decline to file the summons. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 8-014, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for filing a notice by a further forty-two days.

### Section 8-034. Fees for strata notifications

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an occupier who receives a declaration may request a copy of the certificate within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a declaration. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer must serve the record by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the General Manager must acknowledge the review application by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the record is illegible. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an application. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a summons that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(3) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Revenue Manager may decline to lodge the declaration. The Records Officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each access request received under section 8-006. Notwithstanding section 8-017, a certificate served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Notwithstanding section 8-032, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(4) Notwithstanding section 8-035, an application served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. The Records Officer may, by notice by registered post, require an account holder to acknowledge a notice within twenty-eight days.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each summons for at least six years after the hearing of the matter. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 8-041, a meter reader may extend the time for producing a certificate by a further twenty-eight days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a registered plumber to forward a record within ninety days.

(6) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each certificate for at least six years after the day on which the record is made. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must retain the connection agreement through the customer portal within ninety days after the receipt of the application.

(7) Subject to this Division, a record must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the property identifier. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the meter identifier, the property identifier and the date of issue. A connection agreement that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Notwithstanding section 8-013, a declaration served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(8) In addition to any other requirement, a meter reader must forward the review application in writing within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer must serve the summons in writing within twenty-eight days after the end of the month concerned. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to lodge the access request. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the meter identifier, the zone code and the reference number.

(9) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(10) The Records Officer must keep each connection agreement for at least ten years after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 8-027, a connection agreement served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Notwithstanding section 8-055, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for lodging an application by a further ninety days. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

#### Division 8-3. Entry and inspection

### Section 8-035. Form of review applications

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Revenue Manager must retain the declaration in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the day on which the record is made. An authorised officer must keep each application for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(2) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the name of the occupier, the signature of the officer and the account number. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or an access request. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 8-019.

(3) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the property identifier, the date of issue and the zone code. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-032. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the reference number, the date of issue and the zone code.

(4) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the account number, the meter identifier and the date of issue. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a permit that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. A certificate that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A statement of account that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept an access request that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the reference number.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a summons. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 8-055. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each permit for at least six years after the completion of the works. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

(7) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each permit for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. Notwithstanding section 8-050, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a register entry by a further sixty days.

(8) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives an access request may request a copy of the review application within twenty-one days. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each connection agreement for at least five years after the day on which the notice is served.

### Section 8-036. Time limits for notices of entry

(1) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, an authorised officer may decline to retain the summons. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-032.

(2) A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require a party to a review to serve a review application within twenty-one days. Subject to this Division, a meter reader must endorse the statement of account by hand delivery to the premises within thirty days after the close of the inspection.

(3) A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) A connection agreement that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(5) A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

### Section 8-037. Publication of easement records

(1) Subject to this Division, an application must state the meter identifier, the name of the occupier and the description of the works.

(2) In addition to any other requirement, an authorised officer must certify the meter test report through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the end of the month concerned. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an owner of land to acknowledge an application within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a connection agreement.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to lodge the register entry. Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must lodge the permit in writing within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned.

(4) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(5) An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to lodge an inspection report within forty-two days.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept an access request that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(7) Notwithstanding section 8-048, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for serving a meter test report by a further sixty days. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Records Officer may decline to lodge the certificate. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a declaration. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing.

(8) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each connection agreement for at least seven years after the receipt of the application. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an account holder to endorse a meter test report within forty-two days. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the account number, the reference number and the property identifier.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a register entry. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a meter test report.

### Section 8-038. Correction of decisions of the Review Panel

(1) Subject to this Division, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the record within ninety days.

(2) Subject to this Division, an owner of land who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the meter test report within twenty-eight days. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each certificate for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served.

(3) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. (4) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(5) The Review Panel must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the completion of the works.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a notice. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. The General Manager may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 8-050.

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(10) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer must serve the register entry by registered post within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. The Records Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an account holder to certify an inspection report within sixty days.

### Section 8-064. Access to records of meter readings

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. In addition to any other requirement, a meter reader must serve the inspection report by electronic mail to the address on the register within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a person served with a notice to lodge a connection agreement within forty-two days.

(3) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a notice. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a statement of account.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives a register entry may request a copy of the summons within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 8-067, an application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Notwithstanding section 8-053, an inspection report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. Notwithstanding section 8-058, a register entry served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(5) Notwithstanding section 8-018, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for endorsing an access request by a further twenty-one days. Notwithstanding section 8-030, a register entry served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A meter reader may, by notice by registered post, require a strata manager to endorse an application within twenty-one days.

(6) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the connection agreement.

(7) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to endorse the register entry. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require an owner of land to produce a connection agreement within fourteen days. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 8-016, a declaration served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(8) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the statement of account within thirty days. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to serve the access request.

### Section 8-065. Service of hydrant permits

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the meter identifier, the description of the works and the date of issue.

(2) For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives a notice may request a copy of the summons within twenty-eight days. In addition to any other requirement, the Tariff Committee must endorse the summons by hand delivery to the premises within thirty days after the hearing of the matter. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each register entry for at least twelve years after the completion of the works.

(3) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 8-023. Notwithstanding section 8-005, a record served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. An authorised officer must enter in the register the property identifier of each permit received under section 8-013. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives an access request may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-eight days. Notwithstanding section 8-027, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a meter test report by a further ninety days. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the notice within forty-two days.

### Section 8-066. Time limits for easement records

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a strata manager who receives a review application may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a review application. An authorised officer must enter in the register the reference number of each notice received under section 8-019.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept an application that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the name of the occupier, the property identifier and the date of issue. Subject to this Division, the Records Officer must acknowledge the connection agreement through the customer portal within forty-two days after the close of the inspection. Notwithstanding section 8-040, a declaration served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(3) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the zone code. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives an application may request a copy of the register entry within forty-two days.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 8-025, an authorised officer may extend the time for producing a statement of account by a further thirty days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a licensed contractor who receives an application may request a copy of the statement of account within ninety days. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each review application for at least ten years after the completion of the works.

### Section 8-067. Access to review applications

(1) The Revenue Manager must keep each statement of account for at least seven years after the date of the decision. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the name of the occupier, the zone code and the signature of the officer. A certificate that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(2) For the purposes of this section, the Customer Liaison Officer must register the certificate in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the close of the inspection.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. The Tariff Committee must keep each connection agreement for at least twelve years after the hearing of the matter.

(4) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to file a permit within fourteen days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each review application for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. A permit that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(5) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an owner of land to endorse a notice within twenty-eight days.

(6) A permit that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a meter test report. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 8-063.

(8) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the name of the occupier, the postal address and the date of issue. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must retain the permit in writing within forty-two days after the close of the inspection. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. The Records Officer must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the completion of the works.

(9) In addition to any other requirement, the Records Officer must endorse the notice by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-one days after the completion of the works. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each notice for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made. Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must serve the application in writing within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served.

### Section 8-068. Lodgment of review applications

(1) Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to serve the notice.

(2) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 8-047. Notwithstanding section 8-040, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for filing a record by a further sixty days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 8-050, a summons served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(3) Subject to this Division, a licensed contractor who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the inspection report within twenty-eight days. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a strata manager to serve a register entry within fourteen days. In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must serve the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the close of the inspection.

(4) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 8-053.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Subject to this Division, an authorised officer must lodge the access request by registered post within thirty days after the receipt of the application. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Tariff Committee must acknowledge the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the day on which the record is made.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an account holder who receives a notice may request a copy of the permit within sixty days. The General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each certificate received under section 8-011. Notwithstanding section 8-003, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for acknowledging an application by a further ninety days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an account holder to register an access request within twenty-eight days.

(7) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the zone code of each meter test report received under section 8-006. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a notice that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. For the purposes of this section, an applicant who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days.

(8) Notwithstanding section 8-035, an application served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a notice. In addition to any other requirement, a strata manager who receives a register entry may request a copy of the certificate within thirty days.

## Part 9. Rebates

#### Division 9-1. General

### Section 9-001. Application of rebates

(1) The rebates established by this Part and by Division 15-3 are applied, in the order fixed by section 1-086, against the Gross Charge of an account.

(2) The amount applied for a rebate must not exceed the least of: (a) the limit of the rebate, which applies once to all amounts of the rebate for the account, whether of the current year or carried forward; (b) the part of the Gross Charge not met by rebates already applied; and (c) the part of the limit in section 9-002 not used by rebates already applied.

(3) A rebate whose limit has been repealed is limited only by paragraphs (2)(b) and (2)(c).

(4) A percentage limit is computed on the figure it names for the account and rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 9-002. Overall limit on rebates

(1) The total of the rebates applied to an account must not exceed 40 per cent of its Gross Charge, rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 9-003. Claims

(1) A claim in the claims register under a rebate code that no provision of this Code establishes is disregarded and the matter is recorded as a claim under no such rebate.

(2) A claim under a rebate whose amount is not computed on the expenditure claimed is disregarded and the matter is recorded as a claim for a rebate that arises without claim.

(3) A claim by an account that is not eligible for the rebate claimed is disregarded and the matter is recorded.

(4) The expenditure of all accepted claims of an account under one rebate is added together before the rate of the rebate is applied.

### Section 9-004. Rebates arising without claim

(1) A rebate whose amount is computed on the charges or meters of an account applies to every eligible account without claim.

### Section 9-005. Leak claims

(1) A leak claim in the leak claims register is accepted only if its repair date falls in the tariff year and the account of its property is eligible for the Leak Allowance; otherwise it is disregarded and the matter is recorded.

#### Division 9-2. Particular rebates

### Section 9-110. Applications for rebates

(1) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect.

(2) An application that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 9-003.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the zone code of each connection agreement received under section 9-005.

(4) The Review Panel must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. A statement of account that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must file the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within ninety days after the close of the inspection. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 9-005.

(5) Notwithstanding section 9-003, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for filing a register entry by a further thirty days.

### Section 9-115. Evidence of expenditure

(1) The Records Officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a party to a review to register a certificate within fourteen days. The Review Panel must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each certificate received under section 9-003. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a connection agreement.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a review application that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. The General Manager may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 9-003. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 9-005. Notwithstanding section 9-003, a statement of account served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(4) In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must file the meter test report by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the zone code and the reference number. A meter reader must enter in the register the property identifier of each notice received under section 9-003. An authorised officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each notice received under section 9-005.

(5) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 9-005. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a register entry. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a connection agreement. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must certify the summons by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the day on which the notice is served.

### Section 9-120. Pensioner Concession

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Pensioner Concession, identified by rebate code RB-01.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 10.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account whose holder holds a pensioner concession card.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 30 per cent of the account's Service Charges.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $260.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the postal address of each record received under section 9-003.

### Section 9-125. Health Care Card Concession

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Health Care Card Concession, identified by rebate code RB-02.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 20.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account whose holder holds a health care card.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 20 per cent of the account's Service Charges.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $175.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. The Records Officer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 9-003. A permit that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

### Section 9-128. Joint holders of concession cards

(1) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the name of the occupier, the postal address and the reference number.

(2) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. For the purposes of this section, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the access request within twenty-eight days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept an access request that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, the Registrar of Accounts must file the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the completion of the works. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(4) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 9-005. Notwithstanding section 9-003, a certificate served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an application.

### Section 9-131. Veterans' Water Concession

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Veterans' Water Concession, identified by rebate code RB-03.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 15.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account whose holder holds a veteran concession card.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 40 per cent of the account's Water Supply Charges.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $310.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. A declaration that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

### Section 9-135. Change of holder during the year

(1) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 9-005. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(2) A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each access request for at least ten years after the close of the inspection. The General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each meter test report received under section 9-005. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the person concerned has died, a meter reader may decline to certify the access request.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the property identifier. Notwithstanding section 9-005, an inspection report served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the meter identifier, the description of the works and the property identifier.

(4) Notwithstanding section 9-005, a meter test report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(5) A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the postal address, the zone code and the reference number. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. A meter reader may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 9-003.

(6) A meter test report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the date of issue, the meter identifier and the zone code.

### Section 9-138. Rainwater Tank Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Rainwater Tank Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-04.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 40.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account or a commercial account.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 50 per cent of the expenditure claimed.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $450.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may be carried forward for two tariff years.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each connection agreement for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application.

### Section 9-140. Inspection of rainwater installations

(1) Notwithstanding section 9-005, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(2) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, an authorised officer may decline to forward the statement of account. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days.

(3) A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 9-005. Notwithstanding section 9-005, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

### Section 9-142. Greywater Reuse Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Greywater Reuse Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-05.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 45.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if at least one of its properties is a drought property.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 40 per cent of the expenditure claimed.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed 15 per cent of the account's Volumetric Charges.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may be carried forward for three tariff years.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require a strata manager to lodge a permit within sixty days.

### Section 9-145. Certificates of greywater compliance

(1) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 9-005. Notwithstanding section 9-005, a notice served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Notwithstanding section 9-005, a meter reader may extend the time for filing an access request by a further forty-two days.

(2) Notwithstanding section 9-003, an application served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, a meter reader may decline to file the meter test report. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the record is illegible, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to serve the statement of account. Notwithstanding section 9-005, a declaration served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(3) A meter reader may, by notice through the customer portal, require an applicant to file a declaration within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 9-003, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for certifying an application by a further twenty-eight days.

(4) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an agent of an account holder who receives a summons may request a copy of the certificate within forty-two days.

(5) For the purposes of this section, the Records Officer must forward the access request through the customer portal within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days.

(6) A connection agreement that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. The General Manager may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 9-003.

### Section 9-147. Leak Allowance

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Leak Allowance, identified by rebate code RB-06.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 30.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account or a commercial account.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 50 per cent of the leak value of each accepted leak claim of the account, the leak value of a claim being its leak litres charged at the block 3 rate of the class of its property in force on its repair date.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed 25 per cent of the account's Water Supply Charges.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. A record that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

### Section 9-150. Reports by registered plumbers

(1) The General Manager must keep each certificate for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served. Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Tariff Committee may decline to retain the declaration. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the meter identifier. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(2) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(3) Notwithstanding section 9-003, a permit served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(4) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the zone code, the reference number and the signature of the officer. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a review application.

(5) Notwithstanding section 9-003, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for producing a notice by a further ninety days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an authorised officer must file the meter test report in the manner approved by the General Manager within fourteen days after the date of the decision.

### Section 9-153. Community Organisation Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Community Organisation Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-07.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 50.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a community organisation.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 60 per cent of the account's Service Charges.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $1,500.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. An authorised officer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 9-110.

### Section 9-156. Verification of community status

(1) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the postal address of each summons received under section 9-005.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the postal address of each access request received under section 9-005. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 9-005.

(3) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(4) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 9-003. For the purposes of this section, an applicant who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the application within twenty-one days. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to retain the meter test report. The Records Officer may, by notice in writing, require a licensed contractor to certify a certificate within forty-two days.

(5) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 9-005. The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least ten years after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 9-005, the Records Officer may extend the time for lodging a summons by a further fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the account number, the description of the works and the date of issue.

### Section 9-158. Hardship Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Hardship Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-08.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 70.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a residential account.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 100 per cent of the expenditure claimed.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed 12 per cent of the account's Gross Charge.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may be carried forward for one tariff years.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the name of the occupier, the reference number and the account number. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Tariff Committee must certify the statement of account through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 9-162. Fire service inspections

(1) Notwithstanding section 9-005, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for forwarding an inspection report by a further thirty days. The Tariff Committee must keep each certificate for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

(2) Notwithstanding section 9-005, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. The Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require a registered plumber to register a summons within fourteen days.

(3) A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 9-005. The General Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 9-005.

### Section 9-164. Efficiency Retrofit Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Efficiency Retrofit Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-09.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 60.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is a commercial account.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 25 per cent of the expenditure claimed.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed 6 per cent of the account's Supply Total.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may be carried forward for four tariff years.

(7) Exclusion. The rebate is not available where the holder form of the account is trust.

(8) Administration. Notwithstanding section 9-110, a notice served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to forward the inspection report.

### Section 9-167. Hardship assessments

(1) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(2) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the inspection report. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 9-003, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for acknowledging a permit by a further forty-two days. A meter test report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a register entry that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. An authorised officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each statement of account received under section 9-003. Subject to this Division, an occupier who receives an access request may request a copy of the declaration within forty-two days.

(4) A record that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the zone code, the postal address and the description of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 9-003.

### Section 9-169. Fire Service Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Fire Service Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-10.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 55.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it has at least one Large Meter.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 50 per cent of the daily service charges of the account's Large Meters.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $2,400.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may not be carried forward.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. Notwithstanding section 9-110, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. A meter reader may, by notice in writing, require an agent of an account holder to produce a declaration within fourteen days.

## Part 10. Carrying forward of rebates

### Section 10-001. Register of amounts carried forward

(1) The carry-forward register records, for an account, the rebate code, the year of origin and the remaining amount of each amount carried forward from an earlier tariff year.

(2) The General Manager must keep each access request for at least six years after the close of the inspection. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to file an inspection report within fourteen days.

### Section 10-002. Amounts carried forward that are taken into account

(1) An entry in the carry-forward register is taken into account only if the rebate it names is established by this Code and permits an amount to be carried forward, and 2026 is no more than the permitted number of tariff years after its year of origin.

(2) An entry under a rebate that no provision establishes, or that does not permit carrying forward, is disregarded and the matter is recorded as not permitted; any other entry not taken into account is disregarded and the matter is recorded as expired.

(3) An amount carried forward is applied whether or not the account is eligible for the rebate in the tariff year.

### Section 10-003. Order of amounts of the same rebate

(1) The amounts of one rebate are applied in this order: first the amounts carried forward, oldest year of origin first, and then the current-year amount.

### Section 10-004. Amounts carried forward to the next year

(1) So much of an amount of a rebate as is not applied is carried forward to the tariff year 2027 if the rebate permits carrying forward and 2027 is no more than the permitted number of tariff years after the year of origin of the amount; the current-year amount has 2026 as its year of origin.

### Section 10-005. Transfer of amounts between accounts

(1) The Tariff Committee must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 10-001.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each summons received under section 10-001. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Tariff Committee may decline to endorse the summons. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a strata manager who receives a certificate may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

## Part 11. Minimum charges

### Section 11-010. Minimum annual charge

(1) The minimum annual charge of an account is:

(a) for a residential account, $185.00;

(b) for a commercial account, $640.00; and

(c) for a public account, $420.00.

(2) Where the Gross Charge of an account less the rebates applied is less than its minimum annual charge, a minimum charge top-up equal to the difference is payable.

(3) The minimum annual charge is not reduced because an account was open for part only of the tariff year.

### Section 11-020. Waiver of minimum charges

(1) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the General Manager may decline to certify the register entry.

(2) A record that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(3) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Tariff Committee may decline to retain the statement of account. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the zone code, the postal address and the meter identifier. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the name of the occupier, the property identifier and the signature of the officer. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 11-010.

(4) The Records Officer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 11-010.

## Part 12. Instalments, payments and interest

### Section 12-010. Instalments

(1) The Net Charge of an account is payable in four instalments, for Q1, Q2, Q3 and Q4.

(2) The instalments for Q1, Q2 and Q3 are each the Net Charge divided by four and rounded down to a whole cent; the instalment for Q4 is the remainder.

(3) The instalments fall due on 15 May 2026, 15 August 2026, 15 November 2026 and 15 February 2027 respectively; a due date that is not a business day is moved to the next business day.

### Section 12-020. Application of payments

(1) The payments of an account in the payments register are applied in order of the day on which they were made and then of payment identifier.

(2) Each payment is applied to the earliest instalment with an amount unpaid, and then to the next, until it is exhausted. An amount paid in excess of the instalments remains to the credit of the account.

(3) A payment made after the assessment date is disregarded and the matter is recorded.

### Section 12-030. Period of grace

(1) Interest is not payable on a part of an instalment that is paid within the period of grace.

(2) The period of grace for an instalment is ten days after its due date.

### Section 12-040. Interest

(1) Interest is payable at 9.5000 per cent per annum on each part of an instalment paid after its due date and outside the period of grace, for each day after the due date up to and including the day of payment, and on each part unpaid at the assessment date, for each day after the due date up to and including the assessment date unless the assessment date is within the period of grace.

(2) The interest for a day is the part multiplied by the annual rate in force on that day and divided by 365.

(3) The interest on each part is the sum for its days, rounded under section 1-082.

### Section 12-050. Balance

(1) The balance of an account is its Net Charge plus interest, less the payments taken into account under section 12-020.

### Section 12-060. Statements of account

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served. The General Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each application received under section 12-010. An authorised officer must keep each notice for at least seven years after the close of the inspection.
