An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a record.

## Part 13. Records and notices

#### Division 13-1. Registers

### Section 13-001. Delegation in relation to backflow test certificates

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the account number of each connection agreement received under section 13-067.

(2) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. The Records Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each certificate received under section 13-037. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. A notice that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-091, a connection agreement served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to produce an inspection report within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a record must state the postal address, the meter identifier and the description of the works. The General Manager must keep each record for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter.

(4) For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a notice may request a copy of the review application within forty-two days. An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-118, a meter test report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice through the customer portal, require an agent of an account holder to forward a certificate within thirty days. An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(6) In addition to any other requirement, an account holder who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the inspection report within thirty days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a meter test report. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(7) An access request that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the postal address.

### Section 13-002. Fees for notices to occupiers

(1) A permit that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the meter test report within sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing.

(2) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A notice that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each summons received under section 13-095.

(3) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Review Panel may decline to file the access request. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 13-016.

(4) An application that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-074, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for acknowledging a connection agreement by a further sixty days. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-017.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-083, an inspection report served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Review Panel may decline to file the declaration. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives an application may request a copy of the permit within forty-two days. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(7) The General Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an applicant to retain a permit within ninety days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice through the customer portal, require a strata manager to forward a statement of account within forty-two days. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

### Section 13-003. Copies of connection applications

(1) Subject to this Division, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must acknowledge the statement of account by hand delivery to the premises within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each declaration for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served. Notwithstanding section 13-087, a permit served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(3) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a person served with a notice to register a notice within twenty-one days. The General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 13-098.

(4) A statement of account that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Notwithstanding section 13-085, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each record received under section 13-116.

(5) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 13-069, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a permit by a further twenty-eight days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an account holder to acknowledge a summons within twenty-one days.

(7) A meter reader may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-065. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to serve the permit.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-075, the General Manager may extend the time for serving a statement of account by a further forty-two days. A meter reader must keep each meter test report for at least six years after the close of the inspection. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a party to a review to acknowledge a register entry within forty-two days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Review Panel must lodge the inspection report by hand delivery to the premises within sixty days after the close of the inspection.

(9) Notwithstanding section 13-041, an authorised officer may extend the time for forwarding an application by a further twenty-one days. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each inspection report received under section 13-049. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the application within sixty days.

(10) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each application received under section 13-110.

### Section 13-004. Inspection of permits for standpipes

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-091, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing an inspection report by a further twenty-one days. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-094.

(2) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-092. Notwithstanding section 13-018, a certificate served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(3) The Records Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each permit received under section 13-065. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the register entry within sixty days. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-106.

(4) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

(5) A meter test report that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-048. The Review Panel may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-022. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the meter identifier and the zone code.

(6) In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the declaration within fourteen days.

### Section 13-005. Service of plumbing certificates

(1) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Records Officer may decline to file the access request. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-108. Notwithstanding section 13-011, a meter test report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(2) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 13-110. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-095. Notwithstanding section 13-005, a register entry served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an application that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(3) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to endorse the permit. Notwithstanding section 13-061, a meter reader may extend the time for endorsing a notice by a further thirty days. A permit that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(4) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the zone code. A connection agreement that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the date of issue, the account number and the name of the occupier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept an application that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must produce the certificate by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. The General Manager must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the date of the decision. The General Manager may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-103.

(6) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to forward the review application. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Records Officer may decline to serve the summons. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to file a review application within twenty-one days. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each connection agreement for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter.

(7) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Records Officer may decline to forward the record.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager must lodge the statement of account by registered post within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application.

(9) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the property identifier of each connection agreement received under section 13-055. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the date of issue, the signature of the officer and the property identifier. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a meter test report.

(10) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice through the customer portal, require an agent of an account holder to file a review application within thirty days. Notwithstanding section 13-089, a certificate served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. For the purposes of this section, an applicant who receives a register entry may request a copy of the certificate within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

### Section 13-006. Service of strata notifications

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. The Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require a strata manager to certify an access request within forty-two days.

(2) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-055. A meter reader may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 13-076. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-102, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a permit by a further twenty-one days. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-029.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Notwithstanding section 13-107, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding an application by a further twenty-eight days. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the completion of the works.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-003, a register entry served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Notwithstanding section 13-107, a declaration served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a record may request a copy of the notice within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 13-113, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for lodging a permit by a further sixty days.

(6) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-079. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a permit that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-046. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each record for at least seven years after the date of the decision.

(8) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the signature of the officer, the date of issue and the property identifier.

### Section 13-007. Access to backflow test certificates

(1) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A meter reader may, by notice by registered post, require a strata manager to register a meter test report within forty-two days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must endorse the certificate through the customer portal within forty-two days after the completion of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a meter test report.

(3) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Records Officer may decline to forward the review application. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each application for at least ten years after the close of the inspection.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-036, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for producing a notice by a further sixty days. Notwithstanding section 13-037, a permit served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a permit that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. A meter reader may, by notice through the customer portal, require a registered plumber to certify a summons within twenty-eight days.

(5) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 13-023. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a record that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a register entry may request a copy of the record within ninety days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

### Section 13-008. Inspection of disconnection notices

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Tariff Committee may decline to register the register entry. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-019. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(2) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Revenue Manager may decline to register the inspection report. Notwithstanding section 13-052, the Records Officer may extend the time for certifying a certificate by a further fourteen days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a register entry. In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the record within sixty days.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-056, an inspection report served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. An authorised officer must keep each connection agreement for at least six years after the close of the inspection. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. A permit that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(4) The General Manager may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-085. An authorised officer must keep each summons for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-053. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(5) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 13-109, a notice served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(6) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each application received under section 13-007.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-055, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for acknowledging a statement of account by a further sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a permit that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Notwithstanding section 13-072, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a statement of account by a further fourteen days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a record.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-099, the Records Officer may extend the time for producing a notice by a further twenty-one days.

### Section 13-009. Access to easement records

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a registered plumber to certify a record within sixty days. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the reference number, the zone code and the signature of the officer.

(2) An authorised officer may, by notice in writing, require an account holder to serve a statement of account within thirty days.

(3) An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or an access request.

(4) In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the review application within sixty days.

(5) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-036. Notwithstanding section 13-030, a record served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Subject to this Division, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the inspection report within fourteen days.

### Section 13-010. Withdrawal of permits for standpipes

(1) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-010. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the meter identifier, the reference number and the zone code.

(3) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a review application may request a copy of the declaration within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 13-097, a permit served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(4) A meter reader must keep each permit for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made.

### Section 13-011. Copies of notices of entry

(1) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the General Manager may decline to produce the access request. A permit that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the postal address.

(2) A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-116. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader must certify the declaration in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the completion of the works.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A meter reader may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an occupier to serve an application within twenty-one days. The Revenue Manager must keep each review application for at least ten years after the date of the decision.

(5) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-083. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a strata manager who receives a review application may request a copy of the meter test report within sixty days.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-022, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for filing an access request by a further twenty-one days.

(7) Subject to this Division, an owner of land who receives a permit may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days.

### Section 13-012. Publication of meter test reports

(1) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the postal address, the property identifier and the zone code.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to register an inspection report within fourteen days. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. A register entry that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(3) A meter reader must enter in the register the property identifier of each summons received under section 13-070. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-120. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-104.

(4) A review application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Records Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each connection agreement received under section 13-027.

(5) An inspection report that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 13-040, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for serving an access request by a further forty-two days.

### Section 13-013. Fees for permits for standpipes

(1) The Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 13-026. Notwithstanding section 13-075, the General Manager may extend the time for forwarding a meter test report by a further twenty-one days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a statement of account.

(2) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a notice.

### Section 13-014. Lodgment of meter test reports

(1) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to acknowledge the inspection report. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an account holder who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the connection agreement within sixty days. The Records Officer must keep each permit for at least seven years after the day on which the record is made.

(3) Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the description of the works, the account number and the reference number. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Notwithstanding section 13-088, a permit served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-075, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for forwarding a certificate by a further twenty-one days. Where the General Manager is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the General Manager may decline to produce the summons. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the name of the occupier, the postal address and the signature of the officer.

(5) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. An authorised officer must enter in the register the reference number of each review application received under section 13-010. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a meter reader may decline to retain the meter test report.

(7) Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives a summons may request a copy of the notice within twenty-one days.

(8) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the description of the works, the property identifier and the reference number.

(9) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-035. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the account number, the zone code and the postal address. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a statement of account. Notwithstanding section 13-008, an authorised officer may extend the time for endorsing a meter test report by a further ninety days.

### Section 13-015. Language of requests for information

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. An authorised officer must keep each statement of account for at least six years after the end of the month concerned. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to lodge the connection agreement.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must forward the inspection report in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the end of the month concerned. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-008. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 13-019.

(3) The Records Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each summons received under section 13-006. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 13-019.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a record that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

### Section 13-016. Publication of summonses to witnesses

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a record that is incomplete if the record is illegible. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in writing, require a registered plumber to forward a summons within fourteen days. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(3) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the account number, the signature of the officer and the reference number. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(4) A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-031.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a review application that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. An authorised officer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an agent of an account holder to register a summons within fourteen days.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a certificate. Notwithstanding section 13-029, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a summons by a further fourteen days. The General Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each certificate received under section 13-095. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered plumber who receives a certificate may request a copy of the access request within fourteen days.

(7) The General Manager must keep each application for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-094, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a record by a further fourteen days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 13-059. An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Records Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-037.

(9) A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-039. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require a party to a review to forward a notice within twenty-one days. A register entry that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(10) A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-086. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the property identifier of each inspection report received under section 13-098.

### Section 13-017. Custody of strata notifications

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-019, a permit served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-008, the Review Panel may extend the time for filing a register entry by a further fourteen days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an account holder who receives a review application may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days.

(3) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Records Officer may decline to acknowledge the review application. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. The General Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each permit received under section 13-048.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. The General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to endorse a record within twenty-eight days.

### Section 13-018. Access to registers of contractors

(1) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(2) The Revenue Manager must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each statement of account for at least five years after the date of the decision. An inspection report that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(3) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to register an inspection report within fourteen days. The Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each permit received under section 13-085. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the meter identifier, the signature of the officer and the zone code.

(4) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Records Officer may decline to register the register entry. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(5) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the date of issue of each certificate received under section 13-002. The Review Panel must enter in the register the zone code of each inspection report received under section 13-105. In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the certificate within twenty-one days.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Tariff Committee must serve the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a summons.

(7) The General Manager must keep each register entry for at least twelve years after the completion of the works.

(8) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each access request received under section 13-074. A meter reader may, by notice in writing, require a licensed contractor to lodge a declaration within twenty-eight days. Notwithstanding section 13-065, a meter reader may extend the time for serving a declaration by a further ninety days.

(9) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

### Section 13-019. Correction of complaints by occupiers

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-076, a connection agreement served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. A notice that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-065.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives a declaration may request a copy of the notice within forty-two days. A meter reader must keep each notice for at least seven years after the receipt of the application. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

(4) The Records Officer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-023. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the zone code. The General Manager may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-004. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the application within forty-two days.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-030, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-093.

### Section 13-020. Variation of decisions of the Review Panel

(1) The Revenue Manager may, by notice in writing, require a party to a review to produce a notice within forty-two days.

(2) In addition to any other requirement, an owner of land who receives an access request may request a copy of the review application within twenty-eight days.

(3) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) Where a meter reader is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, a meter reader may decline to file the declaration. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. Subject to this Division, a record must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the property identifier.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Revenue Manager must register the record in writing within twenty-eight days after the receipt of the application. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-081. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each statement of account received under section 13-036. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an occupier to acknowledge a record within twenty-one days.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a record. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-011, a certificate served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

### Section 13-021. Retention of access requests

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-022, the Records Officer may extend the time for producing a notice by a further fourteen days. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the zone code, the reference number and the date of issue.

(2) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-117. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, the Customer Liaison Officer must lodge the application by hand delivery to the premises within sixty days after the day on which the notice is served. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(4) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the name of the occupier, the reference number and the meter identifier. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must certify the permit in the manner approved by the General Manager within thirty days after the close of the inspection.

(5) For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a permit may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the summons within sixty days.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 13-069, a certificate served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each permit received under section 13-066. Notwithstanding section 13-015, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a review application by a further twenty-eight days.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a certificate. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the account number, the property identifier and the meter identifier. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Notwithstanding section 13-006, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or an inspection report. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to produce a connection agreement within sixty days.

### Section 13-022. Certification of declarations by agents

(1) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-013.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a register entry.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. A meter reader may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-104. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the meter identifier, the zone code and the reference number.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each meter test report for at least five years after the hearing of the matter. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-063. The Tariff Committee may, by notice through the customer portal, require an owner of land to certify a notice within forty-two days.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-076, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for acknowledging an application by a further twenty-eight days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a statement of account. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered plumber who receives a summons may request a copy of the review application within fourteen days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader must serve the permit in writing within thirty days after the day on which the notice is served.

(8) In addition to any other requirement, the General Manager must lodge the statement of account in writing within forty-two days after the receipt of the application.

(9) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-023. Transfer of decisions of the Review Panel

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-070, a record served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(2) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-093. The Records Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 13-091. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a permit.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-030, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Review Panel may decline to produce the application. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the date of issue, the property identifier and the name of the occupier.

(4) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the account number, the reference number and the signature of the officer. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each statement of account received under section 13-045. Subject to this Division, a party to a review who receives a summons may request a copy of the application within ninety days.

(5) The Records Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each application received under section 13-093. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a statement of account. The General Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require an occupier to file a summons within forty-two days.

(6) The Records Officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an applicant to serve a register entry within twenty-one days. The Records Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each meter test report received under section 13-099.

(7) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to file the register entry. A meter test report that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Notwithstanding section 13-094, an authorised officer may extend the time for lodging a review application by a further forty-two days. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-056.

(8) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-091.

(9) A certificate that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

### Section 13-024. Correction of statements of account

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Notwithstanding section 13-114, an access request served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(2) An authorised officer may, by notice in writing, require an agent of an account holder to register an inspection report within ninety days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(3) The General Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each meter test report received under section 13-109. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(4) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the property identifier, the date of issue and the meter identifier. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-025. Inspection of plumbing certificates

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by registered post, require an occupier to acknowledge a review application within twenty-one days. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to retain a record within forty-two days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a certificate may request a copy of the inspection report within twenty-one days.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-105, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for serving a permit by a further fourteen days. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 13-002. An access request that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(3) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the date of issue of each permit received under section 13-081. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an inspection report. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each register entry for at least seven years after the day on which the notice is served. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must register the notice by electronic mail to the address on the register within thirty days after the hearing of the matter.

(4) The General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 13-104. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, an authorised officer may decline to file the meter test report.

(5) Subject to this Division, a summons must state the name of the occupier, the date of issue and the description of the works.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the record is illegible. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the postal address, the date of issue and the signature of the officer. Notwithstanding section 13-004, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for acknowledging a permit by a further thirty days.

(7) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, an authorised officer may decline to acknowledge the declaration. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Revenue Manager may decline to endorse the register entry.

### Section 13-026. Inspection of backflow test certificates

(1) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(2) Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the description of the works, the postal address and the zone code.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-078, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for endorsing a meter test report by a further fourteen days. In addition to any other requirement, a delegate of the General Manager must endorse the declaration in writing within sixty days after the completion of the works. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-072.

(4) A certificate that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each notice received under section 13-096.

(5) Where a meter reader is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, a meter reader may decline to acknowledge the declaration. Notwithstanding section 13-088, a review application served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. A statement of account that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(6) Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must lodge the permit through the customer portal within twenty-eight days after the close of the inspection. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a registered plumber to file an inspection report within sixty days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each certificate received under section 13-119. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a register entry.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or an inspection report. The General Manager must keep each certificate for at least twelve years after the date of the decision. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a statement of account. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(8) A record that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-066.

(9) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by registered post, require a person served with a notice to serve an access request within fourteen days. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

#### Division 13-2. Notices

### Section 13-027. Signature of strata notifications

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-081, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for lodging a review application by a further twenty-eight days. For the purposes of this section, the Clerk of the Review Panel must file the register entry by registered post within thirty days after the completion of the works.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-049, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for serving a notice by a further twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-045, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving an access request by a further ninety days. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-073. Notwithstanding section 13-092, an application served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(4) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each review application for at least ten years after the close of the inspection.

(5) The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter.

(6) Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the person concerned has died, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to certify the certificate. An access request that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. An access request that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(7) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Review Panel must lodge the register entry by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a meter test report. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-060.

(8) For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the application within sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a record that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(9) A notice that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in writing. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each application for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the summons.

### Section 13-028. Lodgment of summonses to witnesses

(1) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 13-073. (1) Notwithstanding section 13-014, a certificate served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.
