A record that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 13-043. Signature of delcarations by agents

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-043, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for forwarding a meter test report by a further twenty-eight days. Notwithstanding section 13-077, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for producing a declaration by a further twenty-one days. An access request that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the reference number, the description of the works and the signature of the officer.

(2) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-117. Notwithstanding section 13-110, an application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(3) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a record. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to file the permit.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-045, a summons served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a permit.

(6) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a person served with a notice to certify a statement of account within ninety days. For the purposes of this section, the Clerk of the Review Panel must serve the access request in writing within twenty-one days after the day on which the notice is served. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to retain the application.

(7) The Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each connection agreement received under section 13-094. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. The Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to register a declaration within thirty days.

(8) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

(9) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 13-053, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for certifying a connection agreement by a further sixty days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a party to a review who receives a permit may request a copy of the summons within sixty days. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each permit received under section 13-049.

(10) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

### Section 13-044. Retention of review applications

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives an application may request a copy of the notice within ninety days.

(2) The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-111, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for forwarding a register entry by a further sixty days. The Records Officer must keep each application for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 13-024, the Records Officer may extend the time for certifying a register entry by a further ninety days.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

### Section 13-045. Publication of records of meter readings

(1) Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the zone code, the description of the works and the account number. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a notice. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. A meter reader must enter in the register the date of issue of each access request received under section 13-116.

(3) For the purposes of this section, the Clerk of the Review Panel must lodge the record in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the date of the decision. Subject to this Division, a delegate of the General Manager must produce the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the date of issue of each permit received under section 13-014.

(4) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-027. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or an application.

(6) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the postal address, the description of the works and the date of issue. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Tariff Committee must certify the review application in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-one days after the day on which the notice is served. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. The Records Officer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an applicant to lodge an application within fourteen days.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-013, a record served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. Subject to this Division, an application must state the meter identifier, the account number and the postal address.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a record. Notwithstanding section 13-036, a summons served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. For the purposes of this section, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must serve the application by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the hearing of the matter. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(9) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must endorse the connection agreement by electronic mail to the address on the register within forty-two days after the receipt of the application. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the name of the occupier, the reference number and the postal address.

### Section 13-046. Retention of statements of account

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a permit. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a meter test report. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-079.

(2) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to endorse the register entry. Notwithstanding section 13-118, a declaration served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. Notwithstanding section 13-075, the Review Panel may extend the time for acknowledging an application by a further twenty-eight days. A declaration that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(3) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(4) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a registered plumber who receives a declaration may request a copy of the statement of account within fourteen days. Notwithstanding section 13-106, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

### Section 13-047. Time limits for declarations by agents

(1) A meter reader must keep each register entry for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter. The General Manager must keep each statement of account for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. The General Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each certificate received under section 13-051.

(2) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to produce the application. For the purposes of this section, an authorised officer must register the access request through the customer portal within fourteen days after the date of the decision.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-016, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for certifying a review application by a further twenty-one days. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-018.

(4) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-091. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Notwithstanding section 13-048, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(5) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the postal address, the date of issue and the name of the occupier.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-082, a meter test report served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 13-079. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-048. Time limits for summonses to witnesses

(1) Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the signature of the officer, the name of the occupier and the zone code.

(2) A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-077.

(3) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, an authorised officer may decline to register the notice. The General Manager may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-098. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a connection agreement.

(4) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to register the register entry. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, an authorised officer may decline to produce the summons. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Tariff Committee must serve the certificate in writing within fourteen days after the day on which the record is made.

(6) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 13-007. A certificate that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or an access request.

(7) Subject to this Division, an access request must state the zone code, the property identifier and the signature of the officer. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the description of the works of each access request received under section 13-068. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or an inspection report. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

### Section 13-049. Time limits for access requests

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the account number.

(2) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the application. Notwithstanding section 13-097, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for forwarding a permit by a further forty-two days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the name of the occupier, the reference number and the description of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a certificate.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a record that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. An authorised officer must keep each summons for at least five years after the day on which the notice is served. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or an inspection report.

### Section 13-050. Lodgment of complaints by occupiers

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-074, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a summons by a further twenty-one days. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each certificate for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter. The General Manager must keep each inspection report for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

(2) The Review Panel may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-027. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-058, a statement of account served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-031. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a permit. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the date of issue, the zone code and the property identifier. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the record within twenty-eight days. In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives an access request may request a copy of the record within twenty-one days.

### Section 13-051. Language of complaints by occupiers

(1) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. The Revenue Manager must keep each notice for at least six years after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the date of issue, the property identifier and the postal address.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, a strata manager who receives a permit may request a copy of the record within fourteen days. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-037. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-058.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a summons.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-032, a connection agreement served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an occupier who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the record within sixty days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must produce the record by hand delivery to the premises within forty-two days after the receipt of the application.

(6) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-100.

### Section 13-052. Fees for backflow test certificates

(1) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 13-109, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for endorsing a meter test report by a further ninety days.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or an access request.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each connection agreement received under section 13-030. A meter reader may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a registered plumber to produce an inspection report within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 13-001, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for filing a record by a further thirty days.

(4) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the account number, the property identifier and the date of issue. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-054. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the date of the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must endorse the review application in writing within thirty days after the completion of the works.

(5) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. The Tariff Committee must keep each summons for at least ten years after the date of the decision. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the description of the works of each inspection report received under section 13-113.

(6) A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-002. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the zone code, the signature of the officer and the postal address. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to register the permit.

(7) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, an authorised officer may decline to lodge the review application.

(8) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-024. The Review Panel must keep each statement of account for at least five years after the end of the month concerned. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-053. Access to plumbing certificates

(1) The Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an account holder to register a summons within thirty days. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-082. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives an access request may request a copy of the permit within thirty days.

(2) The Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require an occupier to acknowledge a register entry within twenty-one days. Subject to this Division, an application must state the meter identifier, the property identifier and the date of issue. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-059.

(3) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each access request for at least six years after the date of the decision. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(4) The General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an agent of an account holder to serve a register entry within twenty-one days.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each review application for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made.

(6) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a declaration may request a copy of the access request within thirty days. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the account number, the date of issue and the reference number. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a meter test report. An authorised officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an owner of land to endorse a summons within thirty days.

### Section 13-054. Delegation in relation to access requests

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each review application for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a certificate.

(2) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the signature of the officer, the property identifier and the postal address. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A connection agreement that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an agent of an account holder who receives a record may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days.

(3) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to forward the notice. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-098.

(4) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 13-071, an authorised officer may extend the time for acknowledging an inspection report by a further sixty days. For the purposes of this section, the General Manager must retain the permit through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the day on which the notice is served. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the notice within twenty-eight days.

(5) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-055. Language of strata notifications

(1) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 13-045. The General Manager may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-031. In addition to any other requirement, an account holder who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the statement of account within ninety days. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(3) The Records Officer must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the completion of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a notice that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(4) The Review Panel may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-101. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the date of issue, the description of the works and the postal address.

### Section 13-056. Correction of review applications

(1) The Records Officer must keep each meter test report for at least six years after the receipt of the application. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a review application that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(2) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(3) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a statement of account. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require a party to a review to retain a register entry within twenty-one days. The Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least twelve years after the date of the decision.

(4) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. The Records Officer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-104.

#### Division 13-3. Retention and destruction of records

### Section 13-057. Variation of inspection reports

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Notwithstanding section 13-049, a declaration served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(2) The General Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-058. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the property identifier, the reference number and the signature of the officer.

(3) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to certify the notice.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-029. The Tariff Committee must keep each review application for at least six years after the day on which the record is made. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(5) The Tariff Committee must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter.

### Section 13-058. Elcetronic easement records

(1) An access request that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a licensed contractor to serve a certificate within fourteen days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-039.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must acknowledge the certificate through the customer portal within sixty days after the hearing of the matter.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Revenue Manager may decline to acknowledge the application. Notwithstanding section 13-002, a register entry served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. A permit that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(4) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Records Officer may decline to retain the declaration. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the postal address, the date of issue and the meter identifier. Subject to this Division, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must retain the review application in writing within sixty days after the day on which the record is made.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-098, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for forwarding a register entry by a further ninety days.

(6) The Records Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each declaration received under section 13-067.

(7) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the account number.

(8) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-117. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel must lodge the statement of account in writing within twenty-eight days after the close of the inspection. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the register entry within thirty days.

(9) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-113. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(10) Notwithstanding section 13-057, an application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Review Panel may decline to endorse the summons.

### Section 13-059. Electronic summonses to witnesses

(1) A meter reader must enter in the register the meter identifier of each review application received under section 13-045. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a registered plumber to lodge an inspection report within fourteen days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an application.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-012.

(4) A register entry that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The General Manager must keep each permit for at least twelve years after the date of the decision. An inspection report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the zone code, the account number and the name of the occupier.

### Section 13-060. Service of statements of account

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-074, a meter test report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-088. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to lodge an inspection report within forty-two days.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-030. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a meter test report.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

### Section 13-061. Form of trade waste permits

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. The Review Panel must enter in the register the date of issue of each access request received under section 13-067.

(2) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a delegate of the General Manager must acknowledge the access request by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. The Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a person served with a notice to forward an access request within twenty-one days. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to forward the access request.

(3) Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the reference number, the account number and the date of issue. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each declaration for at least six years after the day on which the record is made. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, an authorised officer may decline to file the certificate. Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the review application within sixty days.

(4) A notice that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

### Section 13-062. Form of notices served on occupiers

(1) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the name of the occupier, the property identifier and the reference number. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a record that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to register the certificate. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Records Officer may decline to lodge the certificate. The Review Panel must keep each summons for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

(3) Where the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the General Manager may decline to endorse the access request. Subject to this Division, a record must state the signature of the officer, the description of the works and the name of the occupier. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to forward the inspection report.

(4) Notwithstanding section 7-010, for the tariff year 2026 each regional rate stated in that section is increased by 0.6000 percentage points.

(6) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-018. A certificate that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(7) The Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require an occupier to serve a certificate within sixty days.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a meter test report. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the person concerned has died, a meter reader may decline to forward the summons. Notwithstanding section 13-061, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the zone code, the postal address and the account number.

(10) A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each register entry received under section 13-067. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a record that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 13-079. Form of plumbing certificates

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Notwithstanding section 13-064, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(2) The Revenue Manager must keep each notice for at least ten years after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 13-113, an authorised officer may extend the time for lodging a permit by a further thirty days. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the record is illegible, a meter reader may decline to file the notice. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a connection agreement.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-021, a summons served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each certificate for at least six years after the completion of the works. A connection agreement that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an agent of an account holder to lodge a register entry within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to retain the declaration. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a certificate.

(5) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(6) Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the date of issue. Notwithstanding section 13-080, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. A meter reader may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-092.

(7) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Review Panel may decline to acknowledge the record. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each notice received under section 13-011. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-028, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for lodging an application by a further thirty days.

(9) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(10) Notwithstanding section 13-120, a permit served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the postal address.

### Section 13-080. Destruction of inspection reports

(1) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Review Panel may decline to retain the certificate. In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the notice within twenty-eight days. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a person served with a notice who receives an access request may request a copy of the access request within forty-two days.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-086, a summons served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(3) A meter test report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a permit may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days.

(5) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(6) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Review Panel may decline to endorse the review application. A meter reader may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to forward a permit within thirty days.

### Section 13-081. Language of trade waste permits

(1) The Revenue Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require an account holder to certify a notice within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the zone code, the description of the works and the name of the occupier. An access request that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-032, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for producing a register entry by a further twenty-one days.

(3) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to serve the permit. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 13-039.

(4) A summons that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-020. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-088. Notwithstanding section 13-012, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a record by a further ninety days.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the account number of each connection agreement received under section 13-109. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(6) Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to retain the permit. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each access request received under section 13-044. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a review application.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or an access request. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or an inspection report. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a statement of account.

### Section 13-082. Publication of registers of contractors

(1) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the signature of the officer, the zone code and the account number. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives a declaration may request a copy of the access request within twenty-one days. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-040.

(2) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each summons received under section 13-010.

(3) A connection agreement that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-088. A register entry that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a declaration.

(4) The Review Panel may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 13-003. A meter test report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(5) The Review Panel must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each review application received under section 13-069. A summons that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Subject to this Division, the General Manager must lodge the notice by registered post within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works.

### Section 13-083. Certification of records of meter readings

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-003, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for lodging a review application by a further thirty days. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a person served with a notice to serve a connection agreement within fourteen days.

(2) Subject to this Division, a licensed contractor who receives a record may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a summons that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-048. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(4) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 13-120. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the postal address of each connection agreement received under section 13-096.

(5) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-038.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 13-019.

(7) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each connection agreement received under section 13-007. The Review Panel must enter in the register the reference number of each permit received under section 13-088.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered plumber who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the access request within twenty-one days. In addition to any other requirement, a meter reader must retain the review application by registered post within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a licensed contractor to retain a summons within sixty days.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a record that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each record received under section 13-115. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 13-084. Time limits for disconnection notices

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a permit.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a review application may request a copy of the statement of account within fourteen days. For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a register entry may request a copy of the summons within fourteen days.

(3) An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the General Manager may decline to endorse the inspection report. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a person served with a notice to retain an access request within ninety days.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-049, a statement of account served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(5) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the summons within thirty days. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-037, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for acknowledging a statement of account by a further sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(7) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 13-028.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days.

(9) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the zone code, the date of issue and the meter identifier. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Registrar of Accounts must certify the notice by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-one days after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the meter identifier.

### Section 13-085. Register of requests for information

(1) Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives a notice may request a copy of the permit within thirty days. An authorised officer must keep each application for at least five years after the receipt of the application. The Records Officer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-043. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each register entry for at least five years after the close of the inspection.

(2) Subject to this Division, a record must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the account number.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A register entry that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Notwithstanding section 13-002, an inspection report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(4) The Revenue Manager must keep each statement of account for at least twelve years after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 13-055, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for lodging a connection agreement by a further twenty-eight days. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to acknowledge the register entry.

(5) Subject to this Division, a meter reader must produce the summons in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the day on which the notice is served. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, an authorised officer may decline to retain the permit. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(7) A register entry that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Tariff Committee must serve the certificate by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the hearing of the matter. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-046.

### Section 13-086. Electronic requests for information

(1) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Records Officer may decline to serve the summons. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a review application.

(2) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the zone code.

(3) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to produce the review application.

(4) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the account number of each review application received under section 13-081. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 13-049. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a connection agreement. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the General Manager must register the certificate by electronic mail to the address on the register within sixty days after the close of the inspection.

(6) A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-099. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an owner of land who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the access request within twenty-eight days.

(7) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an owner of land who receives an application may request a copy of the statement of account within twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, the General Manager must retain the connection agreement by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 13-116, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(8) For the purposes of this section, an applicant who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the notice within twenty-one days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each review application for at least seven years after the completion of the works.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a connection agreement.

(10) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post.

### Section 13-087. Copies of easement records

(1) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an owner of land to retain a statement of account within ninety days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a record that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-022. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-038.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a connection agreement. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to register a declaration within ninety days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each permit for at least five years after the day on which the record is made.

(3) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the certificate. An authorised officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-035.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an owner of land to serve a summons within forty-two days.

(5) Where a meter reader is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, a meter reader may decline to certify the statement of account. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the reference number of each meter test report received under section 13-080. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the declaration.

(6) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the register entry. Notwithstanding section 13-108, a certificate served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(7) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 13-088. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least five years after the end of the month concerned. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(8) The General Manager must keep each application for at least five years after the receipt of the application.

(9) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(10) In addition to any other requirement, an agent of an account holder who receives an access request may request a copy of the review application within twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Notwithstanding section 13-002, a meter test report served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

### Section 13-088. Service of disconnection notices

(1) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 13-105, a connection agreement served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Notwithstanding section 13-062, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for forwarding a statement of account by a further fourteen days.

(2) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require a person served with a notice to lodge a notice within sixty days.

(3) The Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require an account holder to retain a permit within twenty-eight days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must produce the access request by hand delivery to the premises within fourteen days after the day on which the record is made. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 13-100.

(4) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(5) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Notwithstanding section 13-107, an inspection report served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to serve the permit.

### Section 13-089. Electronic special meter readings

(1) In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must endorse the application in writing within twenty-eight days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-100, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for lodging a register entry by a further forty-two days. A notice that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a declaration.

(3) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a summons. Notwithstanding section 13-101, the Review Panel may extend the time for endorsing a notice by a further twenty-eight days.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-076, the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a permit by a further sixty days. Subject to this Division, a record must state the name of the occupier, the zone code and the date of issue. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Review Panel may decline to retain the register entry. An authorised officer must enter in the register the property identifier of each review application received under section 13-065.

(5) The Records Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 13-033. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a record that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Notwithstanding section 13-085, an application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-063.

(6) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to acknowledge a register entry within ninety days. A declaration that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(7) An authorised officer may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 13-017.

(8) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 13-081, a declaration served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Notwithstanding section 13-063, a notice served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or an access request.

(9) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-086. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(10) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager must lodge the certificate in writing within forty-two days after the completion of the works. The General Manager may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-027. Notwithstanding section 13-115, an inspection report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

### Section 13-090. Delegation in relation to meter test reports

(1) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a permit may request a copy of the review application within fourteen days.

(2) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(3) A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-085. For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the certificate within ninety days. A meter test report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(4) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Review Panel may decline to forward the access request. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 13-071. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a licensed contractor who receives a permit may request a copy of the permit within thirty days.

### Section 13-091. Certification of hydrant permits

(1) The Registrar of Accounts must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the end of the month concerned. The General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to certify an access request within forty-two days.

(2) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each application received under section 13-100. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an access request that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 13-001. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-031, an application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each inspection report received under section 13-047. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the General Manager may decline to file the summons. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a connection agreement.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least twelve years after the hearing of the matter.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a declaration.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-116, an access request served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-081. Notwithstanding section 13-120, an application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(8) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the signature of the officer, the name of the occupier and the meter identifier. A meter reader may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to endorse a meter test report within sixty days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Review Panel must certify the summons by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the close of the inspection.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the record is illegible. An access request that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must certify the summons through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 13-092. Variation of plumbing certificates

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

(2) Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the name of the occupier, the description of the works and the postal address.

(3) A summons that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in writing. A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect.

(4) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-064. For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a record may request a copy of the access request within twenty-eight days. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(5) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the signature of the officer. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Records Officer may decline to endorse the record. Notwithstanding section 13-038, a meter test report served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(6) A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(7) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the date of issue. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each permit received under section 13-113.

#### Division 13-4. Electronic communication

### Section 13-093. Electronic connection applications

(1) The Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each declaration received under section 13-102. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(2) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 13-046. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or an access request.

(3) A permit that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in writing. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in writing, require an applicant to retain a meter test report within forty-two days.

(4) The Tariff Committee may, by notice in writing, require a registered plumber to register an inspection report within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or an access request. Notwithstanding section 13-061, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for producing a meter test report by a further thirty days. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the date of issue, the postal address and the description of the works.

(5) For the purposes of this section, a person served with a notice who receives an application may request a copy of the declaration within sixty days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a permit.

(6) A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A certificate that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(7) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the close of the inspection.

(8) An application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

### Section 13-094. Retention of easement records

(1) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the notice.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 13-099. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the property identifier, the postal address and the signature of the officer. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-071.

(3) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each inspection report received under section 13-106. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 13-101. Notwithstanding section 13-048, a declaration served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(4) A meter test report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. An application that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept an access request that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 13-095. Register of backflow test certificates

(1) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to register the connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 13-079, a notice served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.
