Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A register entry that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a review application.

(3) A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a certificate. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an owner of land to serve a meter test report within sixty days.

(5) The Records Officer must keep each register entry for at least five years after the date of the decision. The Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to produce an inspection report within forty-two days.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-087, the Records Officer may extend the time for forwarding an inspection report by a further twenty-eight days. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each record for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served. The General Manager must keep each summons for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(7) The Records Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a person served with a notice to acknowledge a connection agreement within twenty-one days.

### Section 13-096. Correction of declarations by agents

(1) A connection agreement that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the postal address of each application received under section 13-119. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 13-035.

(2) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 13-092. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-048. An authorised officer must keep each certificate for at least twelve years after the completion of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-019, the Records Officer may extend the time for endorsing a permit by a further twenty-eight days.

(4) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-079. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-006. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

### Section 13-097. Copies of works approvals

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a record that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the record. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(2) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-075. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Records Officer may decline to retain the record. The Review Panel must enter in the register the reference number of each summons received under section 13-073. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-086, an application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(4) Subject to this Division, an application must state the meter identifier, the date of issue and the postal address.

(5) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-019. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a certificate.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a summons.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-037, an access request served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(8) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the receipt of the application. An authorised officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a strata manager to serve an inspection report within twenty-eight days.

(9) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Notwithstanding section 13-088, the Records Officer may extend the time for forwarding a statement of account by a further ninety days. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-023.

### Section 13-098. Certification of works approvals

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a register entry. Subject to this Division, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must retain the certificate by hand delivery to the premises within ninety days after the day on which the notice is served.

(2) A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-090, the General Manager may extend the time for certifying a record by a further sixty days.

(4) A statement of account that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-009. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the reference number, the signature of the officer and the property identifier.

(5) The Tariff Committee must keep each permit for at least six years after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 13-120, a certificate served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a statement of account.

(6) The Records Officer may, by notice by registered post, require a person served with a notice to endorse an inspection report within ninety days.

(7) Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the summons within thirty days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice through the customer portal, require an applicant to retain a register entry within fourteen days.

(8) Subject to this Division, an agent of an account holder who receives an access request may request a copy of the review application within twenty-one days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. For the purposes of this section, the General Manager must retain the inspection report in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the day on which the record is made.

### Section 13-099. Retention of backflow test certificates

(1) The Registrar of Accounts must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the receipt of the application. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Notwithstanding section 13-020, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a review application by a further ninety days. Notwithstanding section 13-038, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for producing a register entry by a further thirty days.

(2) A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-067. The General Manager must enter in the register the postal address of each access request received under section 13-066.

(3) A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(4) A meter reader must enter in the register the zone code of each application received under section 13-006.

(5) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each permit received under section 13-003. Notwithstanding section 13-111, a summons served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

(6) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Review Panel must enter in the register the reference number of each register entry received under section 13-025.

(7) A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must acknowledge the register entry through the customer portal within fourteen days after the completion of the works.

### Section 13-100. Delegation in relation to works approvals

(1) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-099. The Revenue Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require an account holder to certify a permit within twenty-eight days. The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each register entry received under section 13-050. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-024.

(2) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-036.

(3) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each access request for at least six years after the day on which the record is made. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the meter identifier, the account number and the zone code.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a record that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must forward the statement of account through the customer portal within sixty days after the end of the month concerned.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a permit that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

### Section 13-101. Destruction of declarations by agents

(1) Notwithstanding section 13-106, an authorised officer may extend the time for lodging a meter test report by a further fourteen days. A register entry that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in writing. The General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an applicant to lodge a connection agreement within twenty-eight days.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-068, the Records Officer may extend the time for lodging a register entry by a further ninety days. An authorised officer must enter in the register the postal address of each record received under section 13-061.

(3) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-001.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-035, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a permit by a further ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an inspection report. In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must forward the declaration by registered post within fourteen days after the receipt of the application.

(5) An inspection report that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Notwithstanding section 13-088, a summons served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(6) A meter reader must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each connection agreement received under section 13-097. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to retain the application. Notwithstanding section 13-113, a record served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(7) The Review Panel must keep each inspection report for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter.

(8) The General Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each inspection report received under section 13-098. Subject to this Division, an application must state the property identifier, the meter identifier and the zone code. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the access request within sixty days. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the description of the works of each record received under section 13-015.

### Section 13-102. Access to copmlaints by occupiers

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 13-050. Notwithstanding section 13-100, the Records Officer may extend the time for acknowledging a permit by a further ninety days. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, an authorised officer may decline to file the access request.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a review application. The Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a licensed contractor to file a register entry within thirty days. Notwithstanding section 13-063, the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding an access request by a further ninety days. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 13-034.

(3) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a party to a review who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the permit within forty-two days.

(4) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to register the declaration. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a register entry. Notwithstanding section 13-067, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for acknowledging a connection agreement by a further twenty-eight days.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-018, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-017, a connection agreement served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a connection agreement. A permit that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(7) A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a notice may request a copy of the statement of account within thirty days. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in writing, require an owner of land to serve a permit within twenty-one days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must forward the inspection report in the manner approved by the General Manager within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served.

(8) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 13-103. Retention of disconnection notices

(1) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an owner of land who receives a permit may request a copy of the record within twenty-eight days.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a certificate. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each declaration received under section 13-080.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-003, a certificate served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each declaration for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served.

(4) The General Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a registered plumber to acknowledge a permit within forty-two days. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the postal address, the name of the occupier and the signature of the officer.

(5) The Review Panel must keep each certificate for at least five years after the hearing of the matter. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the property identifier, the reference number and the zone code. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Subject to this Division, a record must state the property identifier, the account number and the date of issue.

(7) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a review application. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 13-119. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the application within thirty days.

### Section 13-104. Access to disconnection notices

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a review application that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. A record that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. The Records Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each meter test report received under section 13-016. Notwithstanding section 13-095, an application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(2) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Records Officer may decline to lodge the connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 13-077, an inspection report served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. The Revenue Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require a licensed contractor to forward a permit within ninety days. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 13-097.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-014, a review application served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Notwithstanding section 13-005, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for producing a register entry by a further twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the meter identifier, the account number and the property identifier.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-031, a record served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. The General Manager must enter in the register the postal address of each inspection report received under section 13-001.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-009, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for producing an inspection report by a further twenty-one days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a certificate. A meter reader must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 13-108.

### Section 13-105. Custody of plumbing certificates

(1) An authorised officer must enter in the register the zone code of each notice received under section 13-101. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a strata manager to produce a summons within twenty-one days. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the zone code, the property identifier and the name of the occupier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager must forward the access request through the customer portal within forty-two days after the date of the decision.

(2) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 13-039.

(3) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in writing, require an agent of an account holder to register a meter test report within thirty days. The Review Panel may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 13-029.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives a notice may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a strata manager who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the register entry within forty-two days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an owner of land who receives a notice may request a copy of the record within twenty-eight days.

(5) A connection agreement that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept an access request that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives a permit may request a copy of the inspection report within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a register entry. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-001.

(7) The Tariff Committee may, by notice in writing, require an occupier to register a statement of account within thirty days. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Notwithstanding section 13-031, the Review Panel may extend the time for certifying a summons by a further fourteen days.

(8) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Tariff Committee must file the application by electronic mail to the address on the register within ninety days after the completion of the works. In addition to any other requirement, the Registrar of Accounts must certify the meter test report by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works.

(9) For the purposes of this section, a delegate of the General Manager must lodge the inspection report in writing within thirty days after the end of the month concerned. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the date of issue, the reference number and the signature of the officer.

### Section 13-106. Delegation in relation to complaints by occupiers

(1) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 13-054. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(2) The Review Panel must enter in the register the date of issue of each connection agreement received under section 13-066. A certificate that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-108, a review application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a certificate.

(4) The Review Panel may, by notice in writing, require an agent of an account holder to forward an application within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a register entry may request a copy of the inspection report within twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, an authorised officer may decline to file the application.

(5) In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must forward the connection agreement by electronic mail to the address on the register within forty-two days after the receipt of the application. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a party to a review to produce a certificate within forty-two days.

(6) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must certify the review application by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to retain the application. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(7) The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to certify a notice within twenty-eight days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(8) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(9) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A connection agreement that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(10) Notwithstanding section 13-008, a statement of account served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the postal address of each register entry received under section 13-120.

### Section 13-107. Register of summonses to witnesses

(1) An authorised officer may, by notice in writing, require a registered plumber to forward a statement of account within forty-two days.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must forward the access request in the approved electronic form within twenty-eight days after the hearing of the matter.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an applicant who receives a summons may request a copy of the register entry within fourteen days. A meter reader must enter in the register the property identifier of each summons received under section 13-005. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a meter test report. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must endorse the summons by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the receipt of the application.

(4) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 13-057. The Records Officer may, by notice in writing, require a licensed contractor to produce a statement of account within thirty days. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-066.

(5) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(6) In addition to any other requirement, an agent of an account holder who receives a review application may request a copy of the connection agreement within sixty days. In addition to any other requirement, the Customer Liaison Officer must produce the access request through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the date of the decision. A meter reader may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a party to a review to retain a notice within thirty days.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an occupier who receives a record may request a copy of the access request within sixty days. An authorised officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each connection agreement received under section 13-019.

(8) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-065. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-008. For the purposes of this section, the Tariff Committee must lodge the declaration in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Records Officer must lodge the review application in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the day on which the record is made.

(9) An authorised officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an account holder to forward an application within ninety days. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. An authorised officer must keep each meter test report for at least five years after the close of the inspection.

### Section 13-108. Form of easement records

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a register entry. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the signature of the officer, the property identifier and the zone code. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-111. Notwithstanding section 13-061, a summons served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(2) For the purposes of this section, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must endorse the summons by registered post within twenty-one days after the receipt of the application. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an owner of land to certify a record within ninety days. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(3) A meter reader may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to serve a register entry within ninety days. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 13-109. Notwithstanding section 13-111, an access request served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(4) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 13-096. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must register the notice by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the close of the inspection. In addition to any other requirement, an agent of an account holder who receives a record may request a copy of the access request within sixty days.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. The Review Panel must keep each notice for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served.

(6) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the date of issue, the name of the occupier and the property identifier. Subject to this Division, a record must state the account number, the date of issue and the meter identifier.

(7) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. An access request that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(8) A meter reader may, by notice by registered post, require an account holder to endorse a register entry within forty-two days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or an access request.

(9) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the General Manager must produce the application by hand delivery to the premises within thirty days after the completion of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

### Section 13-109. Detsruction of notices of entry

(1) The Review Panel may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-009. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager must produce the connection agreement in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the hearing of the matter. Notwithstanding section 13-006, a declaration served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Revenue Manager must certify the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. An access request that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(3) The Records Officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each review application received under section 13-017. An application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(4) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A review application that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A review application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a strata manager to acknowledge a register entry within thirty days.

(5) The Review Panel must enter in the register the postal address of each notice received under section 13-076. Notwithstanding section 13-114, the Review Panel may extend the time for serving an access request by a further thirty days.

(6) An authorised officer must enter in the register the zone code of each register entry received under section 13-058. In addition to any other requirement, a strata manager who receives a register entry may request a copy of the connection agreement within ninety days. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each access request received under section 13-017. Notwithstanding section 13-055, a declaration served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-003, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for filing a statement of account by a further twenty-eight days.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the property identifier. Notwithstanding section 13-023, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(9) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, an authorised officer may decline to certify the application. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require an owner of land to serve a declaration within thirty days. A record that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 13-110. Access to declarations by agents

(1) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the summons. An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, a meter reader may decline to produce the register entry.

(2) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the reference number, the account number and the description of the works. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. The General Manager may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 13-114. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(3) A summons that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. For the purposes of this section, the Registrar of Accounts must lodge the permit by registered post within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(4) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the certificate. Subject to this Division, an application must state the postal address, the description of the works and the name of the occupier. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the zone code, the signature of the officer and the postal address.

### Section 13-111. Lodgment of applications by owners

(1) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the statement of account within twenty-eight days. A declaration that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. In addition to any other requirement, an account holder who receives a review application may request a copy of the summons within twenty-one days.

(2) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each connection agreement received under section 13-088. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to register the declaration. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must forward the declaration by registered post within twenty-eight days after the end of the month concerned. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(3) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each declaration received under section 13-118. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post.

(4) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-033.

(5) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 13-054.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Notwithstanding section 13-059, a certificate served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(7) A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-115, a review application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(9) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(10) Subject to this Division, a party to a review who receives a certificate may request a copy of the summons within forty-two days. Notwithstanding section 13-025, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-003.

### Section 13-112. Service of summonses to witnesses

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a registered plumber who receives a permit may request a copy of the declaration within ninety days. In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the declaration within fourteen days. The Review Panel may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 13-096.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered plumber who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the connection agreement within fourteen days. An authorised officer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 13-072.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. The Records Officer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a person served with a notice to forward a certificate within thirty days. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to certify the review application.

(4) A permit that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives an application may request a copy of the connection agreement within sixty days. A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a record must state the name of the occupier, the date of issue and the zone code.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-052, a declaration served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-040.

(6) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a delegate of the General Manager must produce the application by hand delivery to the premises within thirty days after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 13-072, a permit served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. The General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an occupier to acknowledge an application within forty-two days.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a registered plumber who receives a summons may request a copy of the inspection report within forty-two days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

### Section 13-113. Transfer of strata notifications

(1) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 13-113, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for lodging an application by a further forty-two days. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a connection agreement.

(2) In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives an application may request a copy of the declaration within forty-two days. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the zone code, the reference number and the account number. For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must certify the review application through the customer portal within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a review application.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the record is illegible. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-073. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the declaration. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(4) Notwithstanding section 13-052, a review application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. The Review Panel must enter in the register the postal address of each statement of account received under section 13-010. The Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a licensed contractor to serve a statement of account within twenty-eight days.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or an inspection report.

### Section 13-114. Transfer of backflow test certificates

(1) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a notice. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the property identifier, the zone code and the name of the occupier.

(2) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a registered plumber to retain a notice within forty-two days. Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the description of the works, the reference number and the account number. For the purposes of this section, the Review Panel must retain the connection agreement in the manner approved by the General Manager within thirty days after the receipt of the application. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-028.

(3) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to acknowledge the certificate. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(4) Subject to this Division, a summons must state the zone code, the date of issue and the name of the occupier.

(5) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Revenue Manager may decline to register the meter test report. For the purposes of this section, a registered plumber who receives a permit may request a copy of the summons within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. The Records Officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an account holder to register a statement of account within ninety days.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-103, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(7) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to forward the certificate. For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the notice within forty-two days.

### Section 13-115. Destruction of trade waste permits

(1) A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(2) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(3) A meter reader must keep each access request for at least five years after the close of the inspection.

(4) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-107. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. The General Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an owner of land to lodge a summons within twenty-one days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a notice that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

### Section 13-116. Custody of disconnection notices

(1) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must lodge the permit in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the end of the month concerned. In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must forward the statement of account by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-eight days after the completion of the works.

(2) Notwithstanding section 13-119, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for forwarding a certificate by a further forty-two days. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(3) The General Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each application received under section 13-043.

(4) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-023. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 13-107. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-071.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel must endorse the record in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the hearing of the matter.

(6) The Review Panel may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 13-068. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-073.

(7) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 13-114. Notwithstanding section 13-025, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Notwithstanding section 13-077, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(8) Notwithstanding section 13-035, a record served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(9) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an owner of land to endorse a declaration within twenty-eight days. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, an authorised officer may decline to produce the certificate. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

### Section 13-117. Delegation in relation to applications by owners

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept an application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the name of the occupier, the meter identifier and the zone code. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a notice. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(2) Subject to this Division, the Customer Liaison Officer must retain the meter test report in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-one days after the date of the decision. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. The Records Officer must enter in the register the account number of each statement of account received under section 13-114. For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a certificate may request a copy of the summons within twenty-eight days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. Notwithstanding section 13-002, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for serving an application by a further forty-two days. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Records Officer may decline to acknowledge the permit. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(4) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to file the summons. The General Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 13-077. A meter reader may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 13-086.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a summons that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to endorse a permit within ninety days. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the meter identifier, the date of issue and the signature of the officer. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

(6) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the meter identifier of each application received under section 13-106. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Review Panel may decline to acknowledge the application.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-038, an inspection report served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a meter test report.

(8) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Revenue Manager must acknowledge the declaration by registered post within twenty-eight days after the close of the inspection. In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must produce the connection agreement in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the day on which the notice is served. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 13-042. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(9) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the meter identifier of each inspection report received under section 13-023.

### Section 13-118. Retention of meter reading records

(1) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 13-046. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a person served with a notice to serve a register entry within twenty-one days. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the close of the inspection.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Notwithstanding section 13-107, a declaration served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(3) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each permit received under section 13-031. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the statement of account within sixty days. Subject to this Division, an application must state the property identifier, the reference number and the date of issue. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(4) An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-074, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for filing an inspection report by a further ninety days.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. The General Manager must keep each record for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made. Subject to this Division, an application must state the zone code, the postal address and the account number.

(7) Subject to this Division, the daily service charge stated in section 6-020(2)(a) for a meter of 20 mm has effect for the tariff year 2026 as if it were $0.8860.

(8) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Customer Liaison Officer must acknowledge the statement of account by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 13-062, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for endorsing a notice by a further thirty days. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 13-039. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a registered plumber who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days.

(9) A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post.

(10) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a meter test report. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice through the customer portal, require an account holder to forward a connection agreement within sixty days. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the account number, the meter identifier and the property identifier. The Tariff Committee must keep each summons for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served.

(11) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Revenue Manager may decline to acknowledge the access request. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 13-021.

(12) Notwithstanding section 13-097, a register entry served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 13-052.

(13) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts must file the application through the customer portal within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 13-085. Notwithstanding section 13-008, a meter test report served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(14) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each permit received under section 13-101. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the property identifier, the signature of the officer and the description of the works.

(15) Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Revenue Manager may decline to lodge the register entry. The General Manager may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 13-023.

### Section 13-119. Custody of requests for inofrmation

(1) Subject to this Division, a registered plumber who receives a notice may request a copy of the statement of account within twenty-one days.

(2) The Revenue Manager must keep each access request for at least six years after the close of the inspection. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in writing, require a person served with a notice to serve an inspection report within twenty-eight days. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to file the record. The Records Officer must keep each permit for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made.

(3) Notwithstanding section 13-092, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a declaration.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept an application that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(5) Notwithstanding section 13-048, a record served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a review application. In addition to any other requirement, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must endorse the statement of account in the manner approved by the General Manager within thirty days after the date of the decision. The General Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each inspection report received under section 13-077.

(6) Notwithstanding section 13-006, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for acknowledging a register entry by a further ninety days.

(7) Notwithstanding section 13-056, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a register entry by a further ninety days. The Records Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 13-069.

### Section 13-120. Copies of registers of cotnractors

(1) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the reference number of each summons received under section 13-084. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or an application. Notwithstanding section 13-036, the Review Panel may extend the time for producing a permit by a further ninety days.

(2) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to acknowledge the statement of account. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the date of issue, the account number and the reference number. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 13-101. Notwithstanding section 13-004, a meter reader may extend the time for forwarding a connection agreement by a further twenty-eight days.

(3) An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 13-028. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. The Tariff Committee must keep each permit for at least ten years after the close of the inspection.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or an access request. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. The Review Panel must keep each statement of account for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 13-014.

## Part 14. Review and procedure

#### Division 14-1. The Review Panel

### Section 14-001. Time limits for inspection reports

(1) The General Manager must keep each access request for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-040, a register entry served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each summons received under section 14-011.

(2) Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Records Officer may decline to acknowledge the permit. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each summons for at least five years after the receipt of the application.

(3) An application that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a permit that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the name of the occupier.

(4) The Tariff Committee must keep each register entry for at least twelve years after the day on which the record is made.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. For the purposes of this section, the Records Officer must forward the summons by registered post within sixty days after the hearing of the matter. Notwithstanding section 14-069, the Records Officer may extend the time for certifying a statement of account by a further sixty days.

(6) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the postal address. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each review application received under section 14-064. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an account holder who receives a declaration may request a copy of the meter test report within twenty-one days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-020.

### Section 14-002. Service of notices to occupiers

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or an access request. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a register entry that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. For the purposes of this section, a registered plumber who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the certificate within twenty-one days.

(2) Notwithstanding section 14-038, a summons served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each notice received under section 14-005. Notwithstanding section 14-008, a connection agreement served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, an authorised officer must acknowledge the record by registered post within sixty days after the date of the decision.

(4) A statement of account that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. The General Manager must keep each declaration for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served. The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 14-041.

### Section 14-003. Access to inspection reports

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-011, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for producing a record by a further ninety days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-013.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-006. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

(3) Subject to this Division, the Records Officer must forward the inspection report through the customer portal within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each record for at least five years after the day on which the notice is served. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the day on which the record is made. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-006, the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a record by a further twenty-one days. Notwithstanding section 14-060, a declaration served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. An authorised officer must enter in the register the postal address of each statement of account received under section 14-010.

(5) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each certificate received under section 14-072. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-057. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(6) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to forward the register entry. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice through the customer portal, require an agent of an account holder to register a meter test report within ninety days. Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Tariff Committee may decline to file the certificate.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(8) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-017. Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the date of issue, the account number and the description of the works.

### Section 14-004. Withdrawal of easement records

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-046, a connection agreement served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(2) For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a permit may request a copy of the access request within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, an account holder who receives an access request may request a copy of the summons within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. The General Manager must keep each certificate for at least seven years after the receipt of the application.

(3) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. A summons that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Subject to this Division, a licensed contractor who receives an access request may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days. The Review Panel must keep each record for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a summons that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-070. An inspection report that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each access request for at least seven years after the day on which the notice is served.

(5) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-024. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by registered post, require a person served with a notice to register an access request within forty-two days.

(6) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 14-044. The General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a licensed contractor to endorse a review application within thirty days. The Review Panel may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 14-072.

### Section 14-005. Delegation in relation to connection applications

(1) The Records Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an agent of an account holder to file a review application within thirty days. Notwithstanding section 14-024, a register entry served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(2) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. The General Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require a strata manager to register a connection agreement within twenty-one days.

(3) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.
