(4) A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-071. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the close of the inspection. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter.

(5) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-033. An authorised officer may, by notice by registered post, require a strata manager to file a review application within twenty-eight days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. A meter reader must enter in the register the zone code of each notice received under section 14-056.

(6) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the signature of the officer, the name of the occupier and the description of the works.

(7) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to register a meter test report within forty-two days. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the date of issue of each certificate received under section 14-066.

### Section 14-006. Certification of summonses to witnesses

(1) Subject to this Division, the Review Panel must acknowledge the review application in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the close of the inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 14-069. A meter reader must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each record received under section 14-055.

(2) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-026.

(3) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a certificate. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel must lodge the declaration by registered post within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. The General Manager must keep each connection agreement for at least twelve years after the day on which the notice is served.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a summons. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-058.

(5) Notwithstanding section 14-060, the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a certificate by a further ninety days. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 14-030.

(6) Subject to this Division, a delegate of the General Manager must forward the declaration in writing within forty-two days after the receipt of the application.

### Section 14-007. Correction of registers of contractors

(1) For the purposes of this section, a party to a review who receives a notice may request a copy of the connection agreement within twenty-eight days.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a meter test report. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each record received under section 14-046.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-071, a meter reader may extend the time for lodging an access request by a further twenty-eight days. For the purposes of this section, the Customer Liaison Officer must produce the permit in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the completion of the works. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(4) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the property identifier, the description of the works and the account number. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the account number of each record received under section 14-040.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a summons. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each application for at least seven years after the day on which the notice is served. An access request that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(6) A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the signature of the officer, the account number and the reference number.

(8) An authorised officer must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the completion of the works. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Registrar of Accounts must register the connection agreement by hand delivery to the premises within sixty days after the end of the month concerned.

(9) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must serve the review application by hand delivery to the premises within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 14-008. Destruction of connection applications

(1) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the account number, the signature of the officer and the meter identifier. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Records Officer must endorse the register entry by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-one days after the close of the inspection.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts must keep each record for at least ten years after the receipt of the application. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A declaration that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 14-052.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a permit. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each application for at least ten years after the close of the inspection.

(6) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the zone code of each certificate received under section 14-054. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a meter test report.

(7) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. Subject to this Division, a record must state the meter identifier, the reference number and the account number. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a registered plumber who receives a summons may request a copy of the meter test report within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 14-044, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for forwarding a statement of account by a further fourteen days.

(8) A record that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each application for at least seven years after the close of the inspection. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-039.

(9) Notwithstanding section 14-003, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a register entry by a further forty-two days. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(10) Notwithstanding section 14-033, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for producing a certificate by a further sixty days.

### Section 14-009. Lodgment of notices of entry

(1) For the purposes of this section, the Revenue Manager must certify the record by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-eight days after the end of the month concerned. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the reference number, the property identifier and the date of issue. The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each meter test report received under section 14-059. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 14-021.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(4) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(5) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The General Manager must keep each application for at least ten years after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, an application must state the account number, the reference number and the postal address. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(7) A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-027. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a register entry. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 14-070. Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a record may request a copy of the meter test report within twenty-eight days.

(8) Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to file the access request.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a connection agreement.

(10) Notwithstanding section 14-036, a record served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. An access request that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. In addition to any other requirement, a licensed contractor who receives a register entry may request a copy of the meter test report within sixty days.

### Section 14-010. Retention of complaints by occupiers

(1) In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must forward the declaration through the customer portal within sixty days after the hearing of the matter. The General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each inspection report received under section 14-065.

(2) A declaration that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Notwithstanding section 14-069, a connection agreement served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the date of issue, the property identifier and the description of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a register entry.

(3) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a meter reader must serve the notice by hand delivery to the premises within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. In addition to any other requirement, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must endorse the register entry through the customer portal within forty-two days after the date of the decision. The Records Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-008.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-019, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for producing a connection agreement by a further twenty-one days.

(5) Notwithstanding section 14-042, the Review Panel may extend the time for serving a certificate by a further twenty-eight days. Notwithstanding section 14-073, a connection agreement served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(6) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each register entry received under section 14-003. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-063, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

### Section 14-011. Form of notices to occupiers

(1) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept an application that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(3) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a review application. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(4) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each certificate for at least five years after the day on which the record is made. Notwithstanding section 14-039, the Records Officer may extend the time for serving a record by a further thirty days.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must serve the meter test report through the customer portal within twenty-one days after the hearing of the matter. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a record that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. In addition to any other requirement, a delegate of the General Manager must file the inspection report through the customer portal within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made.

(6) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the completion of the works. The Records Officer must enter in the register the zone code of each summons received under section 14-040.

### Section 14-012. Access to meter test reports

(1) A statement of account that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or an application. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Registrar of Accounts must acknowledge the meter test report through the customer portal within twenty-eight days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must endorse the record by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the completion of the works.

(3) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each register entry received under section 14-035. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the signature of the officer, the description of the works and the property identifier. Notwithstanding section 14-053, a register entry served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(4) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to file the meter test report. A connection agreement that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Notwithstanding section 14-020, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a review application by a further sixty days.

(5) The Tariff Committee must keep each access request for at least six years after the hearing of the matter. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Review Panel may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a strata manager to lodge a certificate within fourteen days. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 14-038.

(6) For the purposes of this section, an account holder who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the meter test report within fourteen days. A register entry that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The General Manager may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to acknowledge an access request within fourteen days. Notwithstanding section 14-034, a record served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

### Section 14-013. Register of trade waste permits

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the property identifier of each meter test report received under section 14-011.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a permit that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Subject to this Division, a record must state the postal address, the zone code and the meter identifier. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a meter test report. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-017.

(3) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 14-055. The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the description of the works of each connection agreement received under section 14-032.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a meter test report. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. An authorised officer may, by notice by registered post, require an account holder to register a statement of account within thirty days.

### Section 14-014. Time limits for permits for standpipes

(1) The Review Panel must keep each connection agreement for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(2) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to acknowledge the access request.

(3) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(4) A certificate that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

### Section 14-015. Register of registers of contractors

(1) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 14-017. In addition to any other requirement, an account holder who receives a record may request a copy of the meter test report within thirty days. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(2) The Records Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each declaration received under section 14-030. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 14-073.

(3) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-065, a meter reader may extend the time for filing a notice by a further fourteen days.

(4) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-031. Notwithstanding section 14-060, an inspection report served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. The Records Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 14-037. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

### Section 14-016. Form of complaints by occupiers

(1) The Records Officer may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to file a record within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 14-021, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a notice by a further sixty days.

(2) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Review Panel must enter in the register the zone code of each register entry received under section 14-029.

(3) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must file the certificate in writing within thirty days after the day on which the record is made.

(4) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. A statement of account that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 14-072. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the account number of each summons received under section 14-056.

(6) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the postal address, the zone code and the signature of the officer. In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must acknowledge the review application in the approved electronic form within sixty days after the completion of the works. Subject to this Division, the General Manager must forward the review application by registered post within ninety days after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 14-017. Register of notices to ocucpiers

(1) A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-020. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-008. Notwithstanding section 14-061, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for producing a review application by a further fourteen days.

(2) Subject to this Division, an application must state the account number, the name of the occupier and the description of the works. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 14-058. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a declaration.

(3) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-039. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. A meter reader must enter in the register the account number of each permit received under section 14-063.

(4) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-057. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Notwithstanding section 14-065, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for filing a connection agreement by a further twenty-eight days.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a review application that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel.

(6) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 14-070.

(7) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-036.

(8) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each declaration for at least ten years after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 14-048, a declaration served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(9) An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-073, the Records Officer may extend the time for certifying a meter test report by a further forty-two days.

(10) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to retain the inspection report. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-060. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the certificate within twenty-eight days. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-038.

### Section 14-018. Correction of strata notifications

(1) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to register the declaration. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-067.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a register entry. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 14-037.

(3) An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-052, the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a review application by a further forty-two days. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-073.

(4) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 14-048. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a person served with a notice to endorse a notice within ninety days. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, a meter reader may decline to acknowledge the application.

(5) A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(6) A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the postal address, the date of issue and the property identifier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each declaration received under section 14-013.

(7) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the General Manager may decline to acknowledge the inspection report.

(8) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an owner of land to serve a permit within twenty-one days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the account number of each certificate received under section 14-001.

#### Division 14-2. Applications for review

### Section 14-019. Access to statements of account

(1) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must produce the notice in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the hearing of the matter.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a register entry that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or an access request. A meter reader may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a party to a review to certify an inspection report within forty-two days.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-007, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may extend the time for certifying a statement of account by a further twenty-one days.

(4) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-065. A connection agreement that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(5) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each notice for at least five years after the close of the inspection.

(6) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect.

(7) Notwithstanding section 14-051, a notice served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-052. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(8) Subject to this Division, a summons must state the property identifier, the date of issue and the description of the works. The Records Officer may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 14-032. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a review application.

(9) The General Manager may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 14-017. Notwithstanding section 14-060, a meter reader may extend the time for acknowledging a statement of account by a further thirty days.

### Section 14-020. Inspection of meter test reports

(1) The Review Panel must keep each register entry for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned.

(2) A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-031. An authorised officer must keep each register entry for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. An authorised officer must enter in the register the account number of each connection agreement received under section 14-068. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-045.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to retain the record. An authorised officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-046. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a permit.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 14-042, a summons served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a party to a review who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the summons within twenty-one days.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Notwithstanding section 14-035, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving a permit by a further fourteen days.

(7) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a certificate. In addition to any other requirement, an occupier who receives a notice may request a copy of the application within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

### Section 14-021. Fees for hydrant permits

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a permit that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(2) A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The General Manager may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 14-050. A meter reader must keep each record for at least twelve years after the hearing of the matter.

(3) A register entry that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Review Panel must retain the notice in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each connection agreement for at least ten years after the date of the decision.

(4) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the summons. Notwithstanding section 14-008, an inspection report served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 14-003, the General Manager may extend the time for filing a notice by a further forty-two days.

(6) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Records Officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each record received under section 14-054. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a connection agreement.

(7) A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(8) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each review application for at least five years after the date of the decision. Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to endorse the inspection report. Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the reference number, the signature of the officer and the account number.

### Section 14-022. Inspection of records of meter readings

(1) An application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-050.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a register entry. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the description of the works of each certificate received under section 14-038. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a record that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. The Records Officer must keep each application for at least five years after the date of the decision.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a meter reader may decline to certify the meter test report. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-049.

(5) The Records Officer must keep each access request for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

(6) For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the application within ninety days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a strata manager who receives an application may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days.

(7) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-025. A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-062. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a declaration. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

### Section 14-023. Form of decisions of the Review Panel

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a register entry may request a copy of the permit within forty-two days. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A meter reader must keep each access request for at least five years after the day on which the record is made.

(2) The Tariff Committee must keep each review application for at least five years after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the reference number, the postal address and the name of the occupier. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a record that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(3) The Review Panel must keep each notice for at least six years after the completion of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a certificate. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-049. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an owner of land to certify a summons within thirty days.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an occupier to certify a meter test report within forty-two days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an owner of land who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the register entry within sixty days. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the description of the works, the signature of the officer and the postal address. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a declaration.

(5) Notwithstanding section 14-011, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a certificate by a further fourteen days.

(6) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a certificate. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-066, the Records Officer may extend the time for certifying a permit by a further sixty days.

### Section 14-024. Withdrawal of hydrant permits

(1) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice by registered post, require an occupier to register an application within twenty-eight days. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-025. Notwithstanding section 14-014, a review application served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-030.

(2) Notwithstanding section 14-039, a summons served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the record is illegible. The Review Panel must keep each application for at least six years after the completion of the works. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an occupier who receives a review application may request a copy of the summons within twenty-one days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept an application that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(4) The General Manager may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-067. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a permit that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Records Officer must acknowledge the application through the customer portal within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision.

(6) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, an authorised officer may decline to forward the record. Subject to this Division, the Records Officer must serve the declaration in writing within forty-two days after the receipt of the application. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-006. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(7) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each connection agreement received under section 14-040. For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must forward the register entry by registered post within sixty days after the hearing of the matter. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer must certify the review application in writing within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to certify the summons.

### Section 14-025. Service of review applications

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-053, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for endorsing a connection agreement by a further twenty-one days. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 14-067.

(2) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each notice for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. A declaration that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. An authorised officer may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-071. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Records Officer may decline to register the summons.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-074, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Notwithstanding section 14-057, a declaration served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

### Section 14-026. Language of plumbing certificates

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each declaration received under section 14-073.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a review application. Notwithstanding section 14-027, a record served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(3) An application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-073. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-011. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the account number.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-003, a permit served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(5) Notwithstanding section 14-056, a notice served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Records Officer may decline to retain the permit. Notwithstanding section 14-067, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for forwarding a summons by a further forty-two days. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to serve the declaration.

(6) The Review Panel must keep each application for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served.

(7) A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 14-033. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 14-027. Copies of applications by owners

(1) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a person served with a notice to register a statement of account within sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a registered plumber to acknowledge a notice within twenty-one days.

(2) A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the zone code of each notice received under section 14-017. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the record is illegible. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a party to a review to serve a notice within fourteen days.

(3) For the purposes of this section, a person served with a notice who receives a register entry may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days.

(4) The Records Officer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-057. Subject to this Division, a person served with a notice who receives a permit may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each meter test report for at least six years after the completion of the works. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-046.

(5) A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an applicant who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the application within twenty-one days. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(6) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 14-028. Inspection of hydrant permits

(1) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must register the certificate in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served.

(2) Subject to this Division, a meter reader must retain the inspection report in the approved electronic form within twenty-one days after the completion of the works.

(3) For the purposes of this section, an agent of an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each review application for at least seven years after the receipt of the application. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives a certificate may request a copy of the record within thirty days.

(4) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require a person served with a notice to serve a permit within fourteen days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(5) A notice that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 14-067, a meter reader may extend the time for forwarding a register entry by a further thirty days. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 14-035.

(6) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a licensed contractor to serve a certificate within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 14-025, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for filing a certificate by a further twenty-one days. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Records Officer may decline to register the statement of account. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a certificate may request a copy of the register entry within forty-two days.

(7) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Tariff Committee may decline to produce the meter test report. Notwithstanding section 14-010, the Records Officer may extend the time for lodging a statement of account by a further thirty days. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 14-012. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each inspection report for at least twelve years after the day on which the record is made.

(8) Notwithstanding section 14-020, a register entry served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Notwithstanding section 14-015, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for filing an inspection report by a further fourteen days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(9) An authorised officer may, by notice in writing, require a strata manager to lodge a record within ninety days. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 14-045. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each permit for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 14-029. Variation of special meter readings

(1) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives a permit may request a copy of the inspection report within sixty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a permit.

(2) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-055. (4) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each record received under section 14-029. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Records Officer must retain the certificate by registered post within sixty days after the close of the inspection. Notwithstanding section 14-027, the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a declaration by a further twenty-one days.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(6) A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. (7) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Tariff Committee may decline to register the inspection report. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each statement of account received under section 14-032. Subject to this Division, a record must state the date of issue, the property identifier and the reference number.

(8) An access request that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 14-009.

(9) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a licensed contractor to acknowledge an access request within ninety days. The Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each application received under section 14-037. For the purposes of this section, a person served with a notice who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the permit within forty-two days.

(10) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 14-015. In addition to any other requirement, an owner of land who receives a notice may request a copy of the review application within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 14-042, a statement of account served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

### Section 14-030. Correction of access requests

(1) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(2) The Records Officer must keep each access request for at least seven years after the completion of the works. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. A certificate that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(3) Subject to this Division, an occupier who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the statement of account within fourteen days.

(4) In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a review application may request a copy of the application within thirty days. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 14-010. The Records Officer must enter in the register the reference number of each declaration received under section 14-065.

(5) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to certify the register entry. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(6) The Tariff Committee must keep each certificate for at least six years after the close of the inspection. The Tariff Committee must keep each review application for at least twelve years after the date of the decision.

### Section 14-031. Adjournment of review hearings

(1) A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-039. Notwithstanding section 14-066, a permit served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. (2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a connection agreement. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a meter reader must acknowledge the review application through the customer portal within ninety days after the completion of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a register entry. Notwithstanding section 14-072, a meter test report served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

(4) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the property identifier, the reference number and the date of issue. A statement of account that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. A summons that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or an access request.

(5) In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives a certificate may request a copy of the notice within thirty days. Notwithstanding section 14-038, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for lodging a connection agreement by a further ninety days.

(7) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. The General Manager must keep each notice for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a summons. The Review Panel may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an owner of land to register a meter test report within sixty days.

(10) Notwithstanding section 14-065, a statement of account served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(11) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. (12) The Review Panel may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require an applicant to produce a review application within twenty-one days. In addition to any other requirement, the Customer Liaison Officer must produce the declaration in the approved electronic form within thirty days after the day on which the record is made.

### Section 14-032. Electronic access requests

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-038, the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding a review application by a further twenty-eight days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an account holder who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the declaration within sixty days. An access request that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A record that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(2) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-052. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an owner of land who receives a register entry may request a copy of the statement of account within forty-two days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a record. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(3) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect.

(4) In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must file the review application by electronic mail to the address on the register within forty-two days after the receipt of the application. The Tariff Committee may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an occupier to acknowledge a connection agreement within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 14-015, a declaration served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(5) Where a meter reader is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, a meter reader may decline to endorse the inspection report. The General Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each certificate received under section 14-054. An authorised officer must keep each access request for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application. A meter reader may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-037.

### Section 14-033. Language of disconnection notices

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a permit. Notwithstanding section 14-044, the General Manager may extend the time for endorsing a certificate by a further twenty-one days. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 14-045. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing.

(2) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the reference number, the date of issue and the account number. A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-037.

(3) A review application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Accounts must acknowledge the review application in writing within ninety days after the date of the decision. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) Subject to this Division, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must serve the meter test report by electronic mail to the address on the register within twenty-one days after the end of the month concerned.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. The Records Officer must enter in the register the property identifier of each inspection report received under section 14-024. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, a meter reader may decline to lodge the inspection report. Notwithstanding section 14-036, a notice served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(6) Subject to this Division, an access request must state the account number, the zone code and the property identifier. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-030.

### Section 14-034. Withdrawal of special meter readings

(1) A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-038. A record that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a licensed contractor to forward a connection agreement within twenty-eight days.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a review application or a meter test report. A connection agreement given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. The Tariff Committee must keep each statement of account for at least five years after the date of the decision.

(3) An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. For the purposes of this section, the Review Panel must endorse the summons in writing within sixty days after the receipt of the application. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. A notice that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(4) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. A statement of account that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Notwithstanding section 14-057, a permit served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by registered post.

(5) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(6) A reference in this section to a record includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-047.

(7) Notwithstanding section 14-057, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for producing a connection agreement by a further thirty days.

(8) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a person served with a notice to register an inspection report within ninety days.

(9) Notwithstanding section 14-022, a meter reader may extend the time for filing a record by a further ninety days. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-062. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to produce an access request within twenty-eight days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a meter reader must certify the permit by registered post within thirty days after the day on which the record is made.

(10) The Records Officer must enter in the register the postal address of each certificate received under section 14-019. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in writing, require an occupier to register a permit within thirty days. A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect.

### Section 14-035. Correction of connection applications

(1) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-040. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a register entry.

(2) Where a meter reader is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a meter reader may decline to file the application. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each review application for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned.

(3) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A meter reader must keep each register entry for at least six years after the day on which the record is made. For the purposes of this section, an account holder who receives an access request may request a copy of the connection agreement within forty-two days. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect.

(4) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Tariff Committee may decline to register the declaration. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. A statement of account that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to acknowledge the meter test report.

(5) A connection agreement that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a person served with a notice to produce a meter test report within ninety days.

(6) Subject to this Division, a connection agreement must state the meter identifier, the signature of the officer and the zone code. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Records Officer may decline to certify the connection agreement. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or an application. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the zone code of each register entry received under section 14-015.

### Section 14-036. Certification of disconnection notices

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a delegate of the General Manager must file the record by registered post within thirty days after the end of the month concerned. A review application that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a certificate may request a copy of the statement of account within sixty days.

(2) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each certificate for at least twelve years after the receipt of the application.

(3) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Tariff Committee may decline to file the application.

(4) Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the reference number, the property identifier and the name of the occupier. The Tariff Committee may, by notice through the customer portal, require a licensed contractor to serve a permit within thirty days.

(5) A review application that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged in writing. A meter reader must keep each review application for at least twelve years after the hearing of the matter.

(6) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the signature of the officer, the date of issue and the zone code. An authorised officer must keep each inspection report for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each summons for at least five years after the completion of the works.

(7) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 14-048. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the meter identifier of each inspection report received under section 14-003.

(8) An authorised officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a licensed contractor to retain a summons within twenty-one days. The Review Panel must keep each meter test report for at least twelve years after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 14-026, the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a meter test report by a further forty-two days. An authorised officer must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 14-058.

(9) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each notice for at least five years after the date of the decision. A register entry that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Notwithstanding section 14-014, a summons served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice. The Records Officer may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a registered plumber to certify a statement of account within thirty days.

#### Division 14-3. Hearings

### Section 14-037. Service of requests for information

(1) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the reference number, the description of the works and the postal address. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(3) A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the signature of the officer, the name of the occupier and the reference number. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. An inspection report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a statement of account. Notwithstanding section 14-052, a record served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an applicant to acknowledge a review application within thirty days.

(6) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each application for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned. A connection agreement that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010.

(7) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. In addition to any other requirement, the General Manager must register the record by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-one days after the completion of the works. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect.
