Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(8) Notwithstanding section 14-066, the Records Officer may extend the time for filing a record by a further twenty-one days. A meter reader may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-030. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a meter reader must endorse the meter test report by registered post within twenty-one days after the day on which the notice is served.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a notice that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to register the connection agreement.

### Section 14-038. Publication of requests for information

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or an application. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each review application received under section 14-058.

(2) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an applicant who receives a review application may request a copy of the access request within forty-two days. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, an authorised officer may decline to endorse the inspection report.

(3) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Review Panel may decline to endorse the summons.

(4) A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A reference in this section to a meter test report includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-044. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a certificate. Notwithstanding section 14-047, a register entry served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(5) Where the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the General Manager may decline to produce the review application.

### Section 14-039. Publication of trade waste permits

(1) In addition to any other requirement, the General Manager must certify the meter test report by registered post within fourteen days after the hearing of the matter. The Review Panel may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 14-019.

(2) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(3) A connection agreement that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(5) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, an authorised officer may decline to endorse the application.

(6) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each inspection report received under section 14-022. In addition to any other requirement, an owner of land who receives an application may request a copy of the statement of account within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a certificate.

(7) The Review Panel may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 14-015. An authorised officer must keep each notice for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each access request for at least seven years after the close of the inspection.

### Section 14-040. Electronic inspection reports

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or an inspection report. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the register entry. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 14-045.

(2) A notice that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Revenue Manager must certify the inspection report by registered post within twenty-one days after the date of the decision.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-003, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for endorsing an inspection report by a further sixty days. Notwithstanding section 14-020, an inspection report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(4) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must forward the meter test report through the customer portal within thirty days after the receipt of the application.

### Section 14-041. Copies of plumbing certificates

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager must register the notice by registered post within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) The Registrar of Accounts must keep each notice for at least five years after the date of the decision. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 14-047.

(3) A record that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-057. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a strata manager who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the connection agreement within fourteen days. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the description of the works, the reference number and the account number.

(5) A meter reader must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each summons received under section 14-055. The Tariff Committee may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to certify a connection agreement within twenty-eight days.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an applicant who receives an application may request a copy of the certificate within ninety days. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-072.

(7) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a record.

(8) A declaration that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. The Tariff Committee may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an account holder to serve a meter test report within sixty days.

### Section 14-042. Access to applications by owners

(1) The Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each record received under section 14-027. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal.

(2) Subject to this Division, a declaration must state the postal address, the signature of the officer and the description of the works. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a permit that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-043. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-049.

(3) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a permit given in substitution for it under section 14-051. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each certificate for at least five years after the end of the month concerned. Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must retain the declaration by electronic mail to the address on the register within sixty days after the day on which the notice is served. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-055.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-025, an authorised officer may extend the time for certifying a register entry by a further fourteen days. In addition to any other requirement, a delegate of the General Manager must forward the meter test report by registered post within fourteen days after the completion of the works. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(5) A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 14-033. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. A meter reader must keep each meter test report for at least six years after the date of the decision.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(7) A register entry that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each review application for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter.

(8) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each statement of account received under section 14-002. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a statement of account. Notwithstanding section 14-011, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for endorsing a notice by a further thirty days. A meter reader must enter in the register the meter identifier of each record received under section 14-003.

### Section 14-043. Custody of hydrant permits

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each permit for at least six years after the date of the decision. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the reference number of each certificate received under section 14-051. Notwithstanding section 14-044, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for producing an application by a further thirty days. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-067.

(2) The Records Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-032. A register entry that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 14-012, a statement of account served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-031.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-024, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a permit. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 14-007.

(5) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Customer Liaison Officer must lodge the notice by hand delivery to the premises within twenty-one days after the close of the inspection. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(6) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives a summons may request a copy of the meter test report within sixty days.

(7) A notice that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

### Section 14-044. Retention of special meter readings

(1) An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. Where the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the General Manager may decline to produce the access request.

(2) A permit that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. Notwithstanding section 14-061, a register entry served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(3) A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 14-004. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each permit for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served.

(4) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. An authorised officer may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-064. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 14-073.

(5) Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the description of the works, the property identifier and the reference number. In addition to any other requirement, an applicant who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a person served with a notice who receives a review application may request a copy of the review application within ninety days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a record that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(7) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 14-043. Notwithstanding section 14-015, a declaration served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an agent of an account holder who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the record within ninety days.

(8) The Review Panel must enter in the register the postal address of each register entry received under section 14-025. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. Notwithstanding section 14-044, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for certifying an application by a further ninety days.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a permit that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 14-045. Custody of works approvals

(1) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a registered plumber who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the application within ninety days. Notwithstanding section 14-021, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for producing an application by a further twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, the Tariff Committee must serve the meter test report in the manner approved by the General Manager within ninety days after the receipt of the application.

(2) Notwithstanding section 14-041, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for forwarding an access request by a further forty-two days.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-031, an access request served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder. An inspection report that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in writing. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-029.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-048, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for producing an inspection report by a further twenty-eight days. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the description of the works, the name of the occupier and the property identifier.

(5) A permit that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each record for at least six years after the receipt of the application. Notwithstanding section 14-064, a permit served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(6) Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to acknowledge the meter test report.

### Section 14-046. Signature of requests for information

(1) Subject to this Division, a summons must state the account number, the zone code and the name of the occupier. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. Notwithstanding section 14-059, a meter test report served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. The Revenue Manager may, by notice in writing, require an account holder to certify an inspection report within sixty days.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a review application that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(3) The Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a registered plumber to certify a record within thirty days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to retain an inspection report within sixty days.

(4) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by registered post. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a statement of account.

(5) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. An authorised officer must keep each statement of account for at least six years after the close of the inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a strata manager who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the permit within twenty-one days.

(6) An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the reference number, the description of the works and the postal address.

### Section 14-047. Form of notices of entry

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each declaration for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned.

(2) An application that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. A meter reader must enter in the register the postal address of each statement of account received under section 14-045. The Review Panel may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 14-022.

(3) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives a review application may request a copy of the record within thirty days. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the signature of the officer, the date of issue and the account number.

(4) The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the meter identifier of each permit received under section 14-074. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the permit.

(5) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 14-067. Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a review application may request a copy of the application within twenty-one days. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a licensed contractor to lodge an application within sixty days. Notwithstanding section 14-057, a record served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder.

(6) Notwithstanding section 14-051, the Review Panel may extend the time for forwarding a meter test report by a further thirty days. The General Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each permit received under section 14-060.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(8) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Tariff Committee may decline to endorse the notice.

(9) A meter reader must keep each inspection report for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned. An authorised officer must enter in the register the property identifier of each access request received under section 14-022. A meter test report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts must forward the certificate by registered post within ninety days after the end of the month concerned.

### Section 14-048. Inspection of special meter readings

(1) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A meter reader may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an occupier to serve a declaration within ninety days. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(2) Notwithstanding section 14-001, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for filing a review application by a further sixty days.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-025, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for producing an application by a further thirty days. Subject to this Division, a record must state the date of issue, the account number and the property identifier.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

(5) Notwithstanding section 14-015, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for acknowledging an inspection report by a further ninety days. In addition to any other requirement, the Clerk of the Review Panel must serve the permit in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the close of the inspection. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel must register the inspection report by hand delivery to the premises within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned.

(6) A meter reader must enter in the register the account number of each meter test report received under section 14-066. A declaration that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(7) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each inspection report for at least five years after the receipt of the application. Notwithstanding section 14-032, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for serving an application by a further ninety days.

(8) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a permit. An application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

(9) In addition to any other requirement, an authorised officer must register the certificate in writing within fourteen days after the completion of the works.

### Section 14-049. Register of meter test reports

(1) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a strata manager to acknowledge a meter test report within ninety days. The Review Panel must keep each review application for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. A review application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

(2) A meter reader may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 14-008.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. The Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-047. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a permit.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the General Manager may decline to register the review application. A reference in this section to an access request includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-033. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each application for at least six years after the day on which the record is made.

(6) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an applicant who receives a declaration may request a copy of the statement of account within sixty days.

(7) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 14-054. An application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a review application. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the General Manager must serve the inspection report by electronic mail to the address on the register within thirty days after the close of the inspection.

(8) Notwithstanding section 14-024, a meter test report served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(9) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each connection agreement for at least ten years after the day on which the record is made.

(10) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, the Revenue Manager must certify the meter test report by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the receipt of the application. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the property identifier, the name of the occupier and the zone code. The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the description of the works of each access request received under section 14-052.

### Section 14-050. Copies of meter test reports

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-011, a review application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. Notwithstanding section 14-041, a summons served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an access request or a connection agreement. For the purposes of this section, a licensed contractor who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the connection agreement within sixty days.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-054. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each register entry received under section 14-003.

(4) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or an access request.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an access request that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. In addition to any other requirement, the Registrar of Accounts must file the register entry in the approved electronic form within fourteen days after the close of the inspection.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010.

(8) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001. A record that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. Subject to this Division, a notice must state the date of issue, the signature of the officer and the account number. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the reference number of each meter test report received under section 14-034.

(9) The General Manager must keep each notice for at least six years after the receipt of the application. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-034. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a review application. Subject to this Division, the Clerk of the Review Panel must endorse the statement of account in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the hearing of the matter.

(10) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-052. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the account number of each statement of account received under section 14-006.

### Section 14-051. Publication of disconnection notices

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-039, a review application served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(2) Subject to this Division, an applicant who receives a summons may request a copy of the notice within thirty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-047, a meter reader may extend the time for lodging a summons by a further fourteen days.

(4) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the General Manager must retain the permit by registered post within ninety days after the day on which the notice is served.

(5) A delegate of the General Manager must keep each register entry for at least six years after the close of the inspection.

(6) A meter reader must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each record received under section 14-056.

### Section 14-052. Copies of backflow test cetrificates

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-070, a meter test report served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(2) A meter reader may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an occupier to file a record within fourteen days. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-005.

(3) Notwithstanding section 14-061, a meter reader may extend the time for filing a connection agreement by a further twenty-eight days. In addition to any other requirement, the General Manager must serve the connection agreement in the manner approved by the General Manager within twenty-one days after the day on which the record is made. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the date of issue of each application received under section 14-009. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-064.

(4) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the property identifier of each meter test report received under section 14-007. In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a declaration may request a copy of the certificate within fourteen days. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the General Manager may decline to file the connection agreement. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a permit that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

### Section 14-053. Service of backflow test certificates

(1) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 14-040. The Review Panel may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 14-035. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a meter test report.

(2) An inspection report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. An inspection report that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(3) The Records Officer may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-048.

(4) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Review Panel must register the meter test report by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the day on which the notice is served. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020. Subject to this Division, a record must state the account number, the meter identifier and the postal address. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or a register entry.

(5) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-008. Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Tariff Committee may decline to lodge the access request. The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each access request received under section 14-025.

(6) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an owner of land is misled by the defect. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. For the purposes of this section, the Registrar of Accounts must certify the connection agreement in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the hearing of the matter.

(7) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a record may request a copy of the register entry within ninety days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or an application. The Review Panel must enter in the register the description of the works of each certificate received under section 14-033.

(8) Subject to this Division, a permit must state the zone code, the meter identifier and the name of the occupier.

### Section 14-054. Publication of applications by owners

(1) In addition to any other requirement, an occupier who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the inspection report within twenty-eight days. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to file the connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 14-052, a notice served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each review application for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 14-004.

(3) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to forward the record. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a summons may request a copy of the summons within ninety days. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the General Manager may decline to certify the declaration.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-013, an access request served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(5) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Clerk of the Review Panel must serve the statement of account by registered post within twenty-eight days after the date of the decision. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each summons for at least six years after the completion of the works.

(6) A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an account holder is misled by the defect. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(8) Subject to this Division, an owner of land who receives a review application may request a copy of the statement of account within ninety days. The Review Panel must keep each notice for at least six years after the completion of the works. In addition to any other requirement, an occupier who receives an access request may request a copy of the review application within ninety days.

(9) Where the Clerk of the Review Panel is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Clerk of the Review Panel may decline to forward the statement of account.

### Section 14-055. Transfer of works approvals

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a summons that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

(2) Notwithstanding section 14-047, a certificate served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(3) In addition to any other requirement, the General Manager must produce the application by electronic mail to the address on the register within ninety days after the receipt of the application. Notwithstanding section 14-003, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for producing a certificate by a further twenty-eight days.

(4) The Tariff Committee must keep each access request for at least six years after the close of the inspection. A record that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a certificate given in substitution for it under section 14-029. The Review Panel may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-044.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a meter test report. Notwithstanding section 14-007, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for serving a meter test report by a further twenty-one days.

(6) Subject to this Division, a licensed contractor who receives a certificate may request a copy of the review application within sixty days. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 14-009.

### Section 14-056. Transfer of special meter readings

(1) Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to register the register entry. A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-029. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. For the purposes of this section, a party to a review who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the record within sixty days.

(2) A certificate that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(3) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 14-013. A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-059.

(4) An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(5) A meter reader must enter in the register the reference number of each access request received under section 14-007. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a statement of account or a statement of account. Subject to this Division, a permit must state the description of the works, the date of issue and the property identifier. The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to forward a statement of account within thirty days.

(6) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the account number, the reference number and the signature of the officer. A notice that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(7) A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a party to a review who receives an application may request a copy of the review application within thirty days. Where a meter reader is satisfied that the person concerned has died, a meter reader may decline to serve the review application.

(8) An inspection report that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 14-014. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a certificate or an inspection report. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(9) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a meter test report or a statement of account.

(10) In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a register entry may request a copy of the record within twenty-eight days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a licensed contractor who receives an inspection report may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-eight days. A certificate that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. An authorised officer must keep each inspection report for at least ten years after the completion of the works.

#### Division 14-4. Decisions and appeals

### Section 14-057. Variation of easement records

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a registered plumber to certify an access request within twenty-eight days. A summons that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the account number, the date of issue and the signature of the officer.

(2) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the postal address of each meter test report received under section 14-072. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an application that is incomplete if the record is illegible. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or a permit. Notwithstanding section 14-015, a statement of account served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(3) Subject to this Division, the Revenue Manager must lodge the review application by electronic mail to the address on the register within fourteen days after the end of the month concerned. A meter reader may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a strata manager to retain a declaration within fourteen days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. The General Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-007.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-029, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for producing a declaration by a further fourteen days.

(5) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A declaration that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a party to a review to serve a connection agreement within sixty days.

### Section 14-058. Retention of strata notifications

(1) Notwithstanding section 14-008, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for serving a meter test report by a further sixty days.

(2) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer must lodge the statement of account through the customer portal within sixty days after the day on which the notice is served.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Review Panel may accept a permit that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a declaration or a declaration.

(4) A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 14-054.

### Section 14-059. Lodgment of inspection reports

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a review application that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a certificate. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the description of the works of each statement of account received under section 14-073.

(2) A reference in this section to a register entry includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-046. An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a strata manager is misled by the defect. Subject to this Division, an application must state the name of the occupier, the reference number and the description of the works.

(3) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each inspection report received under section 14-042. A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 14-005. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a licensed contractor who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the permit within forty-two days. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to produce the statement of account.

(4) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, an authorised officer may decline to register the certificate. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a strata manager who receives an access request may request a copy of the access request within sixty days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Tariff Committee must retain the access request through the customer portal within fourteen days after the receipt of the application.

(5) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each notice for at least five years after the close of the inspection. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a declaration that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(6) The Review Panel must keep each summons for at least twelve years after the close of the inspection.

(7) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the account number of each review application received under section 14-003. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require an occupier to file a declaration within forty-two days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a strata manager to lodge a meter test report within fourteen days. The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to register a statement of account within thirty days.

(8) An authorised officer must keep each declaration for at least six years after the receipt of the application. A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-052. An application that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(9) A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-069.

(10) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to a review application given in substitution for it under section 14-009. Subject to this Division, a statement of account must state the date of issue, the postal address and the zone code.

### Section 14-060. Withdrawal of meter test reports

(1) A reference in this section to a certificate includes a reference to an application given in substitution for it under section 14-007. A meter test report given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must register the record in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the works are not yet complete.

(2) An authorised officer may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require an owner of land to lodge a permit within forty-two days.

(3) The Revenue Manager may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-069. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 14-025, a declaration served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(4) A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. An authorised officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each meter test report received under section 14-026.

### Section 14-061. Withdrawal of connection aplpications

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or an inspection report. A connection agreement that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(2) A meter reader may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a registered plumber to retain a summons within twenty-eight days. An application that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing.

(3) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the postal address, the zone code and the signature of the officer. A declaration that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. A review application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Subject to this Division, a review application must state the reference number, the meter identifier and the description of the works.

(4) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the date of issue, the account number and the meter identifier.

(5) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 14-061.

(6) Notwithstanding section 14-060, an access request served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(7) An inspection report that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged by registered post. Subject to this Division, the Registrar of Accounts must serve the permit in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete. Notwithstanding section 14-007, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for acknowledging a declaration by a further thirty days.

(8) An authorised officer must keep each certificate for at least six years after the hearing of the matter. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210. Notwithstanding section 14-071, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a register entry by a further thirty days. A permit that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(9) A certificate given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

### Section 14-062. Signature of trade waste permits

(1) The Customer Liaison Officer must enter in the register the meter identifier of each notice received under section 14-056. A meter test report that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Records Officer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-072.

(2) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must register the meter test report by hand delivery to the premises within sixty days after the close of the inspection. Subject to this Division, a party to a review who receives an application may request a copy of the permit within fourteen days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a summons that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(4) A statement of account that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to serve the connection agreement. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the application was made in good faith, the Revenue Manager may decline to forward the review application. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(5) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a declaration.

(6) The Clerk of the Review Panel may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a licensed contractor to endorse a notice within sixty days. A meter reader must enter in the register the signature of the officer of each permit received under section 14-074. For the purposes of this section, a registered plumber who receives a certificate may request a copy of the declaration within twenty-one days.

### Section 14-063. Service of connection applications

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a strata manager who receives a certificate may request a copy of the notice within sixty days. An application that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(2) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the property identifier of each statement of account received under section 14-026. Notwithstanding section 14-035, a declaration served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor.

(3) A statement of account that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. A summons given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form. Notwithstanding section 14-037, a register entry served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(4) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if the record is illegible. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Customer Liaison Officer may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept an access request that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a connection agreement.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a register entry that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Where the General Manager is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the General Manager may decline to retain the statement of account.

(6) Notwithstanding section 14-032, a review application served through the customer portal has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. An authorised officer must keep each application for at least seven years after the close of the inspection.

(7) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a permit that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

### Section 14-064. Lodgment of statements of account

(1) In addition to any other requirement, the Review Panel must endorse the notice by hand delivery to the premises within ninety days after the day on which the record is made. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each statement of account for at least ten years after the close of the inspection. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require an occupier to acknowledge a connection agreement within thirty days.

(2) The General Manager must keep each summons for at least six years after the close of the inspection. Notwithstanding section 14-028, a summons served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on a person served with a notice.

(3) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to an inspection report given in substitution for it under section 14-034. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must certify the statement of account in writing within twenty-eight days after the hearing of the matter. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent in writing.

(4) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the record is illegible, the Review Panel may decline to forward the application. Notwithstanding section 14-046, a review application served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. In addition to any other requirement, a party to a review who receives a connection agreement may request a copy of the certificate within fourteen days.

(5) A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 14-008. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(6) An application that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form. The Review Panel must enter in the register the zone code of each record received under section 14-041. An access request given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises.

(7) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the certificate within ninety days.

### Section 14-065. Transfer of hydrant permits

(1) The General Manager may, by notice through the customer portal, require a person served with a notice to forward an application within twenty-eight days.

(2) For the purposes of this section, an occupier who receives a record may request a copy of the access request within thirty days. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to file the notice. The Revenue Manager must enter in the register the property identifier of each review application received under section 14-053.

(3) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in writing. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or a declaration. Notwithstanding section 14-047, an authorised officer may extend the time for endorsing a review application by a further twenty-one days. Subject to this Division, an account holder who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the access request within forty-two days.

(4) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the description of the works, the account number and the property identifier. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a registered plumber who receives a register entry may request a copy of the permit within ninety days. The Registrar of Accounts may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-056.

(5) An access request that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal.

(6) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by registered post, require a registered plumber to register a review application within ninety days. A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each connection agreement for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served.

(7) The Records Officer must keep each application for at least ten years after the completion of the works. Notwithstanding section 14-045, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for lodging a register entry by a further twenty-eight days. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to forward the statement of account. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020.

(8) The General Manager may approve a form of summons for the purposes of this section and section 14-048. Notwithstanding section 14-022, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for forwarding an inspection report by a further forty-two days.

### Section 14-066. Publication of notices of entry

(1) A meter reader may approve a form of review application for the purposes of this section and section 14-052. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an agent of an account holder who receives a review application may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-eight days. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a registered plumber to acknowledge an application within thirty days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

(2) A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-042. A register entry that is lodged by registered post has effect as if it were lodged through the customer portal. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of declaration for the purposes of this section and section 14-033.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-010. The Records Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each notice received under section 14-071.

(4) The Tariff Committee may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a licensed contractor to endorse a permit within ninety days.

(5) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a meter reader must retain the review application by registered post within thirty days after the day on which the notice is served. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to file the statement of account. Notwithstanding section 14-009, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for serving a certificate by a further thirty days. A meter reader may approve a form of statement of account for the purposes of this section and section 14-040.

(6) A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to produce a summons within ninety days.

(7) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Tariff Committee may decline to certify the application.

(8) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the approved electronic form.

(9) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must certify the summons in the manner approved by the General Manager within forty-two days after the day on which the record is made.

(10) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a register entry or a register entry. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice in writing, require a licensed contractor to produce a notice within thirty days. The Revenue Manager may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a registered plumber to register an access request within thirty days.

### Section 14-067. Singature of special meter readings

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(2) A notice that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a registered plumber is misled by the defect. A meter reader may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-059.

(3) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-030.

(4) An authorised officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each declaration received under section 14-012. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Records Officer may decline to register the certificate.

(5) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 14-006. Where the General Manager is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the General Manager may decline to retain the certificate. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-007. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 14-017.

(6) Subject to this Division, an access request must state the property identifier, the reference number and the signature of the officer. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the certificate. A connection agreement that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. A permit that is lodged by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(7) The Review Panel must enter in the register the reference number of each connection agreement received under section 14-050. The Tariff Committee may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a party to a review to forward a permit within forty-two days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 6-020. Notwithstanding section 14-051, a summons served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(8) Notwithstanding section 14-022, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for lodging a notice by a further fourteen days.

### Section 14-068. Delegation in relation to notices to occupiers

(1) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a summons or an inspection report. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each declaration for at least ten years after the hearing of the matter. The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each review application received under section 14-070. Notwithstanding section 14-022, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for forwarding an application by a further twenty-one days.

(2) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a register entry given in substitution for it under section 14-016. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(3) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the General Manager must acknowledge the review application in the approved electronic form within forty-two days after the end of the month concerned.

(4) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an application or a connection agreement.

(5) The Tariff Committee must enter in the register the reference number of each declaration received under section 14-031. A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager.

(6) A reference in this section to an application includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 14-043. An authorised officer must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each notice received under section 14-017. Subject to this Division, an access request must state the name of the occupier, the property identifier and the description of the works.

(7) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. A delegate of the General Manager must enter in the register the meter identifier of each statement of account received under section 14-042. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a meter reader may accept a certificate that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

### Section 14-069. Signature of inspection reports

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. Notwithstanding section 14-048, a statement of account served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. The Records Officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each statement of account received under section 14-039.

(2) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each certificate for at least twelve years after the date of the decision. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 10-003.

(3) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to produce the declaration. Notwithstanding section 14-033, a delegate of the General Manager may extend the time for serving a notice by a further sixty days. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each review application for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. For the purposes of this section, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer must lodge the statement of account in writing within twenty-one days after the day on which the record is made.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-018, a summons served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on a licensed contractor. The Revenue Manager may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-038. Where the Records Officer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Records Officer may decline to endorse the review application.

(5) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the name of the occupier, the description of the works and the zone code.

(6) A register entry given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a declaration given in substitution for it under section 14-056. Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the reference number, the account number and the property identifier.

### Section 14-070. Transfer of permits for standpipes

(1) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the General Manager must retain the application by registered post within forty-two days after the hearing of the matter. A delegate of the General Manager may approve a form of access request for the purposes of this section and section 14-042. A meter test report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 9-001.

(2) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a licensed contractor is misled by the defect. An application given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Notwithstanding section 14-067, a review application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on a party to a review.

(3) Subject to this Division, a register entry must state the name of the occupier, the property identifier and the description of the works. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-028.

(4) The Clerk of the Review Panel must enter in the register the description of the works of each certificate received under section 14-044. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a person served with a notice who receives a notice may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-eight days. A reference in this section to a permit includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-019. Notwithstanding section 14-071, a declaration served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on a registered plumber.

(5) The General Manager must keep each application for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. Subject to this Division, an application must state the name of the occupier, the description of the works and the postal address.

(6) Subject to this Division, a notice must state the reference number, the name of the occupier and the zone code. A delegate of the General Manager must keep each record for at least five years after the end of the month concerned. Notwithstanding section 14-060, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for filing an access request by a further forty-two days.

(7) The General Manager may, by notice by electronic mail to the address on the register, require a licensed contractor to register a review application within forty-two days. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a connection agreement.
