(8) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a strata manager who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the review application within twenty-one days. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to certify the certificate. A statement of account that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(9) The General Manager may approve a form of application for the purposes of this section and section 14-052. A meter test report that is lodged in writing has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. The Tariff Committee may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 14-025.

(10) Notwithstanding section 14-034, a notice served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an agent of an account holder. Notwithstanding section 14-030, the Registrar of Accounts may extend the time for lodging a statement of account by a further ninety days. A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 14-050, the Clerk of the Review Panel may extend the time for lodging a permit by a further twenty-eight days.

### Section 14-071. Form of hydrant permits

(1) Except as otherwise provided in this Part, a licensed contractor who receives a review application may request a copy of the record within sixty days. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a delegate of the General Manager must forward the access request in the approved electronic form within ninety days after the completion of the works. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

(2) Subject to this Division, a licensed contractor who receives a meter test report may request a copy of the meter test report within forty-two days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Records Officer may accept a review application that is incomplete if the record is illegible.

(4) Notwithstanding section 14-031, the Customer Liaison Officer may extend the time for forwarding an access request by a further twenty-one days.

### Section 14-072. Certification of requests for information

(1) Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to acknowledge the connection agreement.

(2) A statement of account given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by registered post. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept an application that is incomplete if the person concerned has died.

(3) For the purposes of this section, an owner of land who receives a certificate may request a copy of the register entry within sixty days.

(4) A reference in this section to a summons includes a reference to a statement of account given in substitution for it under section 14-054.

(5) The Records Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each access request received under section 14-043.

(6) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002.

### Section 14-073. Inspection of registers of contractors

(1) Where the Review Panel is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Review Panel may decline to certify the review application. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, an authorised officer may decline to certify the inspection report. Notwithstanding section 14-021, a review application served by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 4-020.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a party to a review who receives a certificate may request a copy of the statement of account within sixty days. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each access request for at least ten years after the close of the inspection. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must keep each certificate for at least six years after the day on which the notice is served. The Records Officer must enter in the register the description of the works of each register entry received under section 14-007.

(4) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an occupier is misled by the defect. A meter reader must enter in the register the zone code of each permit received under section 14-015. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Revenue Manager may decline to acknowledge the certificate. Where the Customer Liaison Officer is satisfied that a meter has been tampered with, the Customer Liaison Officer may decline to register the register entry.

(5) Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, an authorised officer may decline to endorse the access request. A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the third business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(6) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Clerk of the Review Panel may accept a review application that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(7) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a summons that is incomplete if a dispute is pending before the Review Panel. The Revenue Manager must keep each certificate for at least seven years after the close of the inspection.

(8) A summons that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect.

(9) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 14-051. The General Manager must enter in the register the account number of each certificate received under section 14-034. The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each certificate for at least ten years after the day on which the notice is served.

(10) Notwithstanding section 14-017, the Review Panel may extend the time for serving a permit by a further thirty days. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to register a connection agreement within twenty-one days. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives an access request may request a copy of the application within twenty-eight days.

### Section 14-074. Destruction of hydrant permits

(1) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 14-054. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a permit or a connection agreement. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each meter test report for at least ten years after the end of the month concerned. Notwithstanding section 14-045, the Revenue Manager may extend the time for forwarding an access request by a further twenty-eight days.

(2) For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a review application may request a copy of the certificate within sixty days. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an access request that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with. The Review Panel must enter in the register the meter identifier of each permit received under section 14-062.

(3) The Review Panel may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 14-006. The Chief Hydraulic Engineer must enter in the register the zone code of each access request received under section 14-003. Notwithstanding section 14-032, a notice served in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant. For the purposes of this section, a meter reader must forward the notice through the customer portal within sixty days after the completion of the works.

(4) The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of permit for the purposes of this section and section 14-002. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each summons for at least six years after the completion of the works. Where the Revenue Manager is satisfied that a dispute is pending before the Review Panel, the Revenue Manager may decline to file the connection agreement. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, a party to a review who receives a summons may request a copy of the record within forty-two days.

(5) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a delegate of the General Manager must file the statement of account in the manner approved by the General Manager within fourteen days after the date of the decision. For the purposes of this section, an authorised officer must serve the record in the manner approved by the General Manager within sixty days after the day on which the notice is served.

(6) Subject to this Division, a meter test report must state the property identifier, the meter identifier and the description of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a connection agreement or an access request.

## Part 15. Amending and corrective provisions

#### Division 15-0. Preliminary

### Section 15-001. Arrangement of this Part

(1) Division 15-1 makes amendments, Division 15-2 makes corrections of errors in printing, Division 15-3 makes miscellaneous and transitional provisions, and Division 15-9 sets out proposals published for consultation.

(2) A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a record or a declaration. The Records Officer may approve a form of record for the purposes of this section and section 15-002.

### Section 15-002. Making of amendments

(1) Subsection (1) of each section of Division 15-1 states the day on which the section was made.

(2) The sections of Division 15-1 are arranged by subject and not in the order in which they were made.

### Section 15-003. Corrections

(1) Each provision of Division 15-2 corrects an error in the printing of the provision it names, which may be a provision of Division 15-1.

(2) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register. An inspection report that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register.

### Section 15-004. Reprints

(1) Notwithstanding section 15-001, the Review Panel may extend the time for certifying a meter test report by a further twenty-eight days. A reference in this section to an inspection report includes a reference to an access request given in substitution for it under section 15-001. Subject to this Division, an inspection report must state the zone code, the description of the works and the signature of the officer.

(2) An application that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the General Manager may accept a connection agreement that is incomplete if the information supplied is incomplete.

#### Division 15-3. Miscellaneous and transitional provisions

### Section 15-601. Savings for applications pending

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, a strata manager who receives an access request may request a copy of the register entry within twenty-one days. Where the Review Panel is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Review Panel may decline to produce the access request. A statement of account that is lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(2) An inspection report that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in writing. A review application that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by electronic mail to the address on the register. A meter reader may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 15-001. For the purposes of this section, a strata manager who receives a permit may request a copy of the permit within twenty-one days.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept an inspection report that is incomplete if the person concerned has died. The General Manager must keep each meter test report for at least seven years after the hearing of the matter.

### Section 15-602. Accounts opened before commencement

(1) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may, by notice by hand delivery to the premises, require a strata manager to produce a certificate within ninety days.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 15-001. A record given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the premises cannot safely be entered, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to retain the certificate.

(3) The Chief Hydraulic Engineer may approve a form of register entry for the purposes of this section and section 15-003. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Tariff Committee may accept a review application that is incomplete if the occupier consents in writing. The General Manager must keep each review application for at least seven years after the receipt of the application.

### Section 15-603. Continuation of permits

(1) Subject to this Division, the Records Officer must forward the access request by registered post within twenty-one days after the end of the month concerned.

(2) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 7-010. The Records Officer may, by notice through the customer portal, require a party to a review to register a register entry within sixty days. In addition to any other requirement, a person served with a notice who receives a statement of account may request a copy of the notice within forty-two days.

### Section 15-604. Meters installed under former tariffs

(1) The Registrar of Accounts must enter in the register the name of the occupier of each connection agreement received under section 15-003. Subject to this Division, a certificate must state the name of the occupier, the signature of the officer and the account number.

(2) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to retain a permit within sixty days. A permit that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. The Tariff Committee must keep each record for at least six years after the close of the inspection. For the purposes of this section, the Revenue Manager must retain the notice in the manner approved by the General Manager within ninety days after the close of the inspection.

(3) Where the Tariff Committee is satisfied that the works are not yet complete, the Tariff Committee may decline to lodge the register entry. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the account number, the signature of the officer and the name of the occupier. An access request that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

### Section 15-605. Review applications lodged before commencement

(1) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Revenue Manager may accept a notice that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered.

(2) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of certificate for the purposes of this section and section 15-003. Where an authorised officer is satisfied that the person concerned has died, an authorised officer may decline to forward the application. A register entry that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a party to a review is misled by the defect.

(3) A review application that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged in writing. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the postal address, the zone code and the property identifier. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the record is illegible, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to serve the connection agreement. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may accept a statement of account that is incomplete if a meter has been tampered with.

(4) A reference in this section to a connection agreement includes a reference to a meter test report given in substitution for it under section 15-003.

### Section 15-606. Transitional registers

(1) A permit given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent by electronic mail to the address on the register.

(2) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of inspection report for the purposes of this section and section 15-001. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040. The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an agent of an account holder to acknowledge an application within forty-two days.

### Section 15-607. References to former Parts

(1) Notwithstanding section 15-003, a summons served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on an owner of land.

(2) A record that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by registered post.

### Section 15-608. Water Efficiency Audit Rebate

(1) Establishment. This section establishes the Water Efficiency Audit Rebate, identified by rebate code RB-11, as a rebate under Part 9.

(2) Priority. The rebate has priority 40.

(3) Eligibility. An account is eligible if it is an account.

(4) Amount. The amount of the rebate is 30 per cent of the expenditure claimed.

(5) Limit. The rebate applied must not exceed $380.00.

(6) Carry-forward. An amount of the rebate not applied may be carried forward for two tariff years.

(7) Exclusion. No holder form is excluded from the rebate.

(8) Administration. A record that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an applicant is misled by the defect. The Customer Liaison Officer may approve a form of connection agreement for the purposes of this section and section 9-003.

## Part 16. Rulings, explanatory memoranda and examples

### Section 16-001. Explanatory Memorandum: the tariff for 2026

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) The tariff for 2026 continues the catchment levy at 3.2500 per cent and leaves the regional surcharges at 4.2500 per cent for the North region, 3.6000 per cent for the South region, 5.1000 per cent for the West region and 2.9500 per cent for the East region.

(2) The daily service charge for a meter of 20 mm remains $0.8240 and the block 1 allowance for Band A remains 450 litres per day.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 5-210.

### Section 16-005. Explanatory Memorandum: interest

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) Interest on late instalments is charged at 9.5000 per cent per annum throughout 2026 and 2027, and the period of grace is ten days.

(2) Interest is rounded to the nearest cent, ties to even, in the same way as the other charges.

### Section 16-008. Illustrative Example: a residential account

Designation: Illustrative Example.

(1) A residential account of Class TC-101 consumes 61,200 litres over a meter interval of 90 days, an average of 680 litres a day. Block 1 is the first 450 litres a day, so 40,500 litres are charged at the block 1 rate and 20,700 litres at the block 2 rate.

(2) A pensioner holding the account receives 30 per cent of its Service Charges, limited to $260.00, before any other rebate, and the Hardship Rebate is applied last of all, at priority 70.

### Section 16-012. Binding Ruling BR 2026/1: billing zone

Designation: Binding Ruling.

(1) This Ruling concerns section 3-002.

(2) The billing zone of an account is the zone in which the total consumption of all meter intervals of the properties of the account in the tariff year is greatest. Where two or more zones have the same greatest consumption, the billing zone is the one of them with the lowest zone number.

(3) Where the account has no consumption in the tariff year, section 3-002 applies.

### Section 16-015. Explanatory Memorandum: rebates

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) Rebates are applied in order of rebate code, beginning with RB-01. The overall limit on rebates is 50 per cent of the Gross Charge.

(2) A review application that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises.

### Section 16-019. Binding Ruling BR 2026/2: minimum charges for part of a year

Designation: Binding Ruling.

(1) This Ruling concerns section 11-010.

(2) The minimum annual charge of an account is reduced to the minimum annual charge multiplied by its active days and divided by 365, rounded under section 1-082, and section 11-010(3) does not apply.

### Section 16-022. Illustrative Example: interest on a late instalment

Designation: Illustrative Example.

(1) An instalment of $400.00 due on 15 May 2026 and paid on 30 May 2026 bears interest for fifteen days at 9.5000 per cent per annum, that is $1.56, and a payment made on a Saturday is treated as made on that Saturday.

### Section 16-027. Binding Ruling BR 2026/3: payments made on a day that is not a business day

Designation: Binding Ruling.

(1) This Ruling concerns sections 12-020 to 12-040.

(2) A payment made on a day that is not a business day is treated, for the purposes of the order in which payments are applied, the period of grace and interest, as made on the next business day.

(3) Whether a payment was made after the assessment date is determined by the day on which it was actually made.

### Section 16-030. Explanatory Memorandum: carried-forward amounts

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) Amounts carried forward are applied before the current-year amount of the same rebate, oldest first, as section 10-003 provides.

### Section 16-034. Binding Ruling BR 2026/4: order of amounts of one rebate

Designation: Binding Ruling.

(1) This Ruling concerns section 10-003.

(2) The current-year amount of a rebate is applied before any amount of that rebate carried forward, and the amounts carried forward are then applied oldest year of origin first.

### Section 16-040. Illustrative Example: wastewater

Designation: Illustrative Example.

(1) A property with a discharge factor of 85 per cent that consumes 100,000 litres in an interval is charged wastewater on 85,000 litres at the rate of its zone in force on the last day of the interval.

### Section 16-047. Explanatory Memorandum: corrections and amendments

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) Where an amendment and a correction affect the same figure, the provision with the higher section number prevails, so that Division 15-2 always prevails over Division 15-1.

(2) Proposals in Division 15-9 indicate the figures the Corporation expects to apply from 1 July 2026.

### Section 16-053. Illustrative Example: the overall limit

Designation: Illustrative Example.

(1) An account with a Gross Charge of $1,000.00 can receive at most $400.00 of rebates, and the Fire Service Rebate is applied before the Leak Allowance.

### Section 16-061. Explanatory Memorandum: billing zones

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) The billing zone of an account with several properties is the zone of its property with the largest land area; where two have the same land area, the one with the lowest property identifier.

### Section 16-070. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 1

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) The Clerk of the Review Panel must keep each certificate for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. A meter reader must enter in the register the reference number of each certificate received under section 8-010.

(2) Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, an authorised officer must retain the record by registered post within twenty-one days after the date of the decision. Notwithstanding section 8-010, a permit served by hand delivery to the premises has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. A reference in this section to a statement of account includes a reference to a connection agreement given in substitution for it under section 8-010. A declaration that is lodged in the approved electronic form has effect as if it were lodged in the approved electronic form.

(3) Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 3-002. Without limiting any other provision of this Part, an authorised officer may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the application was made in good faith.

### Section 16-073. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 2

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) Notwithstanding section 8-010, an access request served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an applicant.

(2) The Tariff Committee may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-010. A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Registrar of Accounts must keep each inspection report for at least five years after the hearing of the matter. A reference in this section to a declaration includes a reference to a summons given in substitution for it under section 8-010.

(3) Without limiting any other provision of this Part, the Registrar of Accounts may accept a meter test report that is incomplete if the premises cannot safely be entered. A record that is lodged through the customer portal has effect as if it were lodged by hand delivery to the premises. Notwithstanding section 8-010, a review application served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an account holder.

(4) A declaration given under this section is taken to be given on the second business day after it is sent through the customer portal. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the certificate within forty-two days.

### Section 16-076. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 3

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) A reference in this section to a review application includes a reference to a record given in substitution for it under section 8-010. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 11-010. Except as otherwise provided in this Part, an occupier who receives a permit may request a copy of the review application within thirty days.

(2) The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each permit for at least seven years after the day on which the notice is served. Where the Chief Hydraulic Engineer is satisfied that the occupier consents in writing, the Chief Hydraulic Engineer may decline to serve the connection agreement. Notwithstanding section 8-010, an inspection report served by registered post has effect as if it were served personally on a strata manager.

(3) The Registrar of Accounts may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an applicant to file a summons within twenty-eight days.

### Section 16-079. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 4

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) The Review Panel may approve a form of notice for the purposes of this section and section 8-010.

(2) A certificate that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless a person served with a notice is misled by the defect.

(3) Subject to this Division, a review application must state the zone code, the description of the works and the postal address. An authorised officer must enter in the register the date of issue of each record received under section 8-010.

(4) Subject to this Division, an access request must state the signature of the officer, the name of the occupier and the postal address.

### Section 16-082. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 5

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) A meter reader must enter in the register the account number of each application received under section 8-010.

(2) Notwithstanding section 8-010, an application served in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. Notwithstanding section 8-010, an access request served in writing has effect as if it were served personally on an occupier. An authorised officer may, by notice by registered post, require an occupier to retain a review application within twenty-eight days. The Revenue Manager must keep each review application for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned.

(3) The Customer Liaison Officer may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require an owner of land to acknowledge a certificate within twenty-one days. Nothing in this section affects the operation of section 12-040.

(4) Subject to this Division, a summons must state the account number, the meter identifier and the name of the occupier. Notwithstanding section 8-010, the Tariff Committee may extend the time for forwarding a record by a further ninety days.

### Section 16-085. Explanatory Memorandum: administrative arrangements No. 6

Designation: Explanatory Memorandum.

(1) The Clerk of the Review Panel may approve a form of meter test report for the purposes of this section and section 8-010. A reference in this section to a notice includes a reference to a notice given in substitution for it under section 8-010.

(2) An authorised officer must keep each register entry for at least six years after the completion of the works. Where a delegate of the General Manager is satisfied that the record is illegible, a delegate of the General Manager may decline to retain the review application. An inspection report that is defective in form only is taken to be valid unless an agent of an account holder is misled by the defect. An authorised officer may, by notice by registered post, require an agent of an account holder to certify a meter test report within twenty-eight days.

(3) The Review Panel must enter in the register the property identifier of each meter test report received under section 8-010. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate a notice or a record.

(4) The Review Panel may, by notice in the approved electronic form, require a person served with a notice to endorse a connection agreement within sixty days. Subject to this Division, a summons must state the property identifier, the account number and the meter identifier.

(5) A notice given under this section is taken to be given on the fifth business day after it is sent in writing. Where the Registrar of Accounts is satisfied that the information supplied is incomplete, the Registrar of Accounts may decline to certify the application. A meter reader must keep each declaration for at least seven years after the end of the month concerned. A delegate of the General Manager may, by notice in the manner approved by the General Manager, require a strata manager to retain a notice within twenty-eight days.

(6) A summons that is lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager has effect as if it were lodged in the manner approved by the General Manager. The Customer Liaison Officer must keep each record for at least six years after the completion of the works. A failure to comply with this section does not invalidate an inspection report or a review application. Unless the General Manager otherwise directs, the Revenue Manager must register the review application by electronic mail to the address on the register within sixty days after the hearing of the matter.
